# align_sheet_v2.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
MODULE Náº®N PHáº²NG PHá»I Cáº¢NH ÄA Táº¦NG OMR CHUáº¨N Bá»˜ GD&ÄT 2025 (PHIÃŠN Báº¢N CAMERA DI Äá»˜NG CHUYÃŠN NGHIá»†P)
TÃ­ch há»£p:
1. Bá»™ lá»c má»‘c vuÃ´ng tá»± thÃ­ch á»©ng (Adaptive 4-Corner OMR Quad Finder) cho Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i chá»¥p nghiÃªng, lá»‡ch gÃ³c.
2. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xoay 4 hÆ°á»›ng (0Â°, 90Â°, 180Â°, 270Â°) dá»±a trÃªn Ä‘áº·c tÃ­nh hÃ¬nh há»c Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng vÃ  phÃ¢n bá»‘ má»±c.
3. Táº§ng dá»± phÃ²ng nháº­n diá»‡n viá»n giáº¥y A4 (Document Boundary Contour Fallback).
4. Kháº¯c phá»¥c triá»‡t Ä‘á»ƒ lá»—i charmap UnicodeEncodeError trÃªn Windows .exe.
"""
from itertools import combinations
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np

# Báº£o vá»‡ luá»“ng Console stdout chá»‘ng lá»—i charmap trÃªn Windows .exe
if sys.platform == "win32":
    try:
        if sys.stdout is not None and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if sys.stderr is not None and hasattr(sys.stderr, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass

CANONICAL_WIDTH = 1769
CANONICAL_HEIGHT = 2526

# Tá»a Ä‘á»™ 5 cáº·p má»‘c chuáº©n trÃªn phÃ´i Master (1769 x 2526)
TPL_LEFT_5 = np.array([
    [56.7, 45.9],      # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn trÃ¡i
    [58.9, 819.2],     # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [58.9, 1371.1],    # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [55.6, 1723.7],    # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [55.6, 2449.0],    # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i trÃ¡i
], dtype=np.float32)

TPL_RIGHT_5 = np.array([
    [1720.9, 44.8],    # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn pháº£i
    [1720.9, 819.2],   # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [1721.5, 1371.1],  # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [1721.5, 1723.7],  # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [1721.5, 2449.0],  # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i pháº£i
], dtype=np.float32)

# Tá»· lá»‡ khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chuáº©n theo trá»¥c dá»c
EXPECTED_Y_RATIOS = (TPL_LEFT_5[:, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1]) / (TPL_LEFT_5[4, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1])


def rotate_cardinal(image: np.ndarray, angle: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Xoay ma tráº­n áº£nh chÃ­nh xÃ¡c theo cÃ¡c gÃ³c vuÃ´ng."""
    if angle == 90:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE)
    elif angle == 180:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_180)
    elif angle == 270:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE)
    return image


def detect_fiducial_squares(gray: np.ndarray) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float, float]]:
    """
    QuÃ©t tÃ¬m cÃ¡c á»©ng viÃªn má»‘c vuÃ´ng Ä‘en trÃªn toÃ n trang, ká»ƒ cáº£ khi chá»¥p camera cÃ³ ná»n bÃ n.
    Sá»­ dá»¥ng cv2.RETR_LIST Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng bá»‹ máº¥t cÃ¡c má»‘c náº±m bÃªn trong viá»n giáº¥y.
    """
    h, w = gray.shape
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (3, 3), 0)
    
    # Káº¿t há»£p hai ngÆ°á»¡ng thÃ­ch á»©ng Ä‘á»ƒ báº¯t má»‘c trong má»i Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng
    thresh1 = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 31, 7)
    thresh2 = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 25, 6)
    thresh = cv2.bitwise_or(thresh1, thresh2)

    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (3, 3))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(thresh, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)
    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(closed, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

    cands = []
    min_dim = min(h, w)
    min_side = max(6, int(round(min_dim * 0.005)))
    max_side = max(50, int(round(min_dim * 0.08)))

    for c in cnts:
        x, y, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if cw < min_side or ch < min_side or cw > max_side or ch > max_side:
            continue
        aspect = cw / float(ch)
        if 0.65 <= aspect <= 1.55:
            area = cv2.contourArea(c)
            extent = area / float(cw * ch)
            if extent > 0.45:
                cands.append((float(x + cw / 2.0), float(y + ch / 2.0), float(cw), float(ch)))
    return cands


def find_adaptive_4_corners(cands: List[Tuple[float, ...]], w: int, h: int) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """
    Äá»‹nh vá»‹ 4 má»‘c gÃ³c OMR (TL, TR, BR, BL) thÃ­ch á»©ng 100% vá»›i áº£nh chá»¥p Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i.
    KhÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c vÃ o vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘áº·t giáº¥y trÃªn bÃ n hay khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch cáº§m mÃ¡y.
    """
    if len(cands) < 4:
        return None

    pts = np.array([[c[0], c[1]] for c in cands], dtype=np.float32)
    hull_idx = cv2.convexHull(pts, returnPoints=False)
    hull = pts[hull_idx.flatten()]
    if len(hull) < 4:
        return None

    # Tá»‘i Æ°u tá»‘c Ä‘á»™: giá»¯ láº¡i tá»‘i Ä‘a 18 Ä‘iá»ƒm cá»±c trá»‹ ngoÃ i cÃ¹ng
    if len(hull) > 18:
        ctr = np.mean(hull, axis=0)
        dists = np.linalg.norm(hull - ctr, axis=1)
        hull = hull[np.argsort(dists)[-18:]]

    best_quad = None
    best_score = -1.0

    for comb in combinations(range(len(hull)), 4):
        quad = hull[list(comb)]
        ctr = np.mean(quad, axis=0)
        angles = np.arctan2(quad[:, 1] - ctr[1], quad[:, 0] - ctr[0])
        quad = quad[np.argsort(angles)]

        # Sáº¯p xáº¿p theo thá»© tá»±: TL, TR, BR, BL
        tl_idx = min(range(4), key=lambda i: quad[i][0]**2 + quad[i][1]**2)
        quad = np.roll(quad, -tl_idx, axis=0)
        p_tl, p_tr, p_br, p_bl = quad

        # Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh lá»“i vÃ  chiá»u khÃ´ng gian
        if p_tr[0] <= p_tl[0] or p_br[1] <= p_tr[1] or p_bl[1] <= p_tl[1] or p_br[0] <= p_bl[0]:
            continue

        w_top = np.linalg.norm(p_tr - p_tl)
        w_bot = np.linalg.norm(p_br - p_bl)
        h_l = np.linalg.norm(p_bl - p_tl)
        h_r = np.linalg.norm(p_br - p_tr)

        avg_w = (w_top + w_bot) / 2.0
        avg_h = (h_l + h_r) / 2.0
        if avg_w < 100 or avg_h < 150:
            continue

        ratio = avg_h / avg_w
        # Tá»· lá»‡ lÃ½ tÆ°á»Ÿng giá»¯a chiá»u cao vÃ  chiá»u rá»™ng cá»§a 4 má»‘c OMR lÃ  ~1.444 (cho phÃ©p gÃ³c nghiÃªng Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i 1.10 - 1.80)
        if not (1.10 <= ratio <= 1.80):
            continue
        if abs(w_top - w_bot) / avg_w > 0.38 or abs(h_l - h_r) / avg_h > 0.38:
            continue

        area = avg_w * avg_h
        score = area / (1.0 + abs(ratio - 1.444) * 6.0)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_quad = np.array([p_tl, p_tr, p_br, p_bl], dtype=np.float32)

    return best_quad


def _eval_orientation_asymmetry(gray: np.ndarray, corners: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """
    Äo lÆ°á»ng Ä‘á»™ báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng cá»§a cÃ¡c cáº·p má»‘c chuáº©n dá»c trá»¥c Y (chuáº©n Bá»™ GD&ÄT 2025).
    Äáº·c tÃ­nh báº¥t biáº¿n:
    - M2 (Äáº§u Pháº§n II) táº¡i Y = 1371.1 (54.3% chiá»u cao) trÃªn cáº£ 2 lá» trÃ¡i vÃ  pháº£i.
    - M1 (Äáº§u Pháº§n I) táº¡i Y = 819.2 (32.4%).
    - M3 (Äáº§u Pháº§n III) táº¡i Y = 1723.7 (68.2%).
    Náº¿u áº£nh Ä‘Ãºng chiá»u (0Â°), toáº¡ Ä‘á»™ M1, M2, M3 Ã¡nh xáº¡ vÃ o Ä‘Ãºng cÃ¡c Ã´ vuÃ´ng Ä‘en.
    Náº¿u áº£nh bá»‹ xoay ngÆ°á»£c 180Â°, M2 rÆ¡i vÃ o khoáº£ng giáº¥y tráº¯ng tinh (Y = 1154.9).
    """
    tpl_corners = np.array([
        TPL_LEFT_5[0], TPL_RIGHT_5[0],
        TPL_RIGHT_5[4], TPL_LEFT_5[4]
    ], dtype=np.float32)

    H = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(tpl_corners, corners)
    test_pts = np.array([
        TPL_LEFT_5[1],   # M1 TrÃ¡i
        TPL_RIGHT_5[1],  # M1 Pháº£i
        TPL_LEFT_5[2],   # M2 TrÃ¡i (KhÃ³a báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chÃ­nh)
        TPL_RIGHT_5[2],  # M2 Pháº£i (KhÃ³a báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chÃ­nh)
        TPL_LEFT_5[3],   # M3 TrÃ¡i
        TPL_RIGHT_5[3],  # M3 Pháº£i
    ], dtype=np.float32).reshape(1, -1, 2)

    mapped_pts = cv2.perspectiveTransform(test_pts, H)[0]
    h, w = gray.shape
    score = 0.0
    r = max(5, int(round(min(h, w) * 0.012)))

    for i, (mx, my) in enumerate(mapped_pts):
        ix, iy = int(round(mx)), int(round(my))
        if r <= ix < w - r and r <= iy < h - r:
            patch = gray[iy - r : iy + r + 1, ix - r : ix + r + 1]
            if np.min(patch) < 105 and (patch < 115).sum() > (r * r * 0.35):
                weight = 3.0 if (i == 2 or i == 3) else 1.0  # M2 mang trá»ng sá»‘ quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh
                score += weight
    return score


def auto_orient_sheet_robust(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Optional[np.ndarray]]:
    """
    Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng phÃ¡t hiá»‡n hÆ°á»›ng chá»¥p vÃ  xoay Ä‘Ãºng chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng (0Â°).
    Kiá»ƒm tra cáº£ 4 gÃ³c vuÃ´ng (0Â°, 90Â°, 180Â°, 270Â°) vÃ  chá»n gÃ³c cÃ³ Ä‘á»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng thÃ­ch
    má»‘c báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng Y cao nháº¥t.
    """
    raw_h, raw_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    # Æ¯u tiÃªn gÃ³c dá»c tá»± nhiÃªn trÆ°á»›c, sau Ä‘Ã³ lÃ  gÃ³c ngang
    test_angles = [0, 180, 90, 270] if raw_h >= raw_w else [90, 270, 0, 180]

    valid_candidates = []
    for angle in test_angles:
        rotated = rotate_cardinal(image_bgr, angle)
        cur_gray = cv2.cvtColor(rotated, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        cur_h, cur_w = cur_gray.shape
        cands = detect_fiducial_squares(cur_gray)
        corners = find_adaptive_4_corners(cands, cur_w, cur_h)

        if corners is not None:
            score = _eval_orientation_asymmetry(cur_gray, corners)
            valid_candidates.append((score, angle, rotated, corners))

    if valid_candidates:
        # Sáº¯p xáº¿p theo Ä‘iá»ƒm báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng má»‘c giáº£m dáº§n
        valid_candidates.sort(key=lambda c: c[0], reverse=True)
        best_score, best_angle, best_rot, best_corners = valid_candidates[0]

        if best_angle != 0:
            print(f"[OK] PhÃ¡t hiá»‡n áº£nh xoay {best_angle}Â° (Äiá»ƒm má»‘c M1-M3: {best_score:.1f}) -> ÄÃ£ xoay vá» Ä‘Ãºng chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng (0Â°).")
        return best_rot, best_corners

    return image_bgr, None

def detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[float, ...]], List[Tuple[float, ...]], List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
    """HÃ m quÃ©t má»‘c pháº³ng nguyÃªn báº£n (dÃ nh cho báº£n scan mÃ¡y tÃ­nh)."""
    h, w = gray.shape
    expected_side = max(9, int(round(0.0183 * w)))

    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (3, 3), 0)
    block_size = max(15, (expected_side * 3) | 1)
    thresh = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, block_size, 7)
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (3, 3))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(thresh, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)

    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(closed, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    min_side = expected_side * 0.50
    max_side = expected_side * 1.95

    left_cands, right_cands, all_cands = [], [], []
    for c in cnts:
        x, y, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if min_side <= cw <= max_side and min_side <= ch <= max_side:
            aspect = cw / float(ch)
            area = cv2.contourArea(c)
            extent = area / float(cw * ch)
            if 0.60 <= aspect <= 1.55 and extent > 0.45:
                cx = x + cw / 2.0
                cy = y + ch / 2.0
                all_cands.append((cx, cy))
                if cx < w * 0.30:
                    left_cands.append((cx, cy, float(cw), float(ch)))
                elif cx > w * 0.70:
                    right_cands.append((cx, cy, float(cw), float(ch)))

    left_cands.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    right_cands.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    return left_cands, right_cands, all_cands


def select_best_5(cands: List[Tuple[float, ...]], min_span: float) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Khá»›p tá»• há»£p 5 má»‘c theo tá»· lá»‡ khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chuáº©n."""
    if len(cands) < 5:
        return None
    best_pts = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for comb in combinations(range(len(cands)), 5):
        pts = [cands[i] for i in comb]
        xs = np.array([p[0] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)
        ys = np.array([p[1] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)

        span_y = ys[-1] - ys[0]
        if span_y < min_span:
            continue

        ratios = (ys - ys[0]) / span_y
        ratio_err = float(np.max(np.abs(ratios - EXPECTED_Y_RATIOS)))
        if ratio_err > 0.052:
            continue

        poly = np.polyfit(ys, xs, 1)
        x_pred = np.polyval(poly, ys)
        x_err = float(np.max(np.abs(xs - x_pred)))

        score = ratio_err * 1000.0 + x_err * 2.0
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_pts = np.array([[p[0], p[1]] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)

    return best_pts


def detect_a4_paper_contour(gray: np.ndarray) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Táº§ng dá»± phÃ²ng 3: Nháº­n diá»‡n 4 gÃ³c cá»§a tá» giáº¥y A4 trÃªn ná»n bÃ n."""
    h, w = gray.shape
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
    _, thresh = cv2.threshold(blur, 0, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY + cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(thresh, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    cnts = sorted(cnts, key=cv2.contourArea, reverse=True)

    min_area = w * h * 0.15
    for c in cnts[:5]:
        if cv2.contourArea(c) < min_area:
            break
        peri = cv2.arcLength(c, True)
        approx = cv2.approxPolyDP(c, 0.02 * peri, True)
        if len(approx) == 4:
            pts = approx.reshape(4, 2).astype(np.float32)
            ctr = np.mean(pts, axis=0)
            angles = np.arctan2(pts[:, 1] - ctr[1], pts[:, 0] - ctr[0])
            pts = pts[np.argsort(angles)]
            tl_idx = min(range(4), key=lambda i: pts[i][0]**2 + pts[i][1]**2)
            pts = np.roll(pts, -tl_idx, axis=0)
            return pts
    return None


def align_sheet_4band(image_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    """Náº¯n pháº³ng phá»‘i cáº£nh Ä‘a táº§ng sau khi Ä‘Ã£ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chuáº©n hÃ³a chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng."""
    raw_bgr = cv2.imread(str(image_path))
    if raw_bgr is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y file: {image_path}")

    out_w, out_h = CANONICAL_WIDTH, CANONICAL_HEIGHT
    tpl_corners = np.array([
        TPL_LEFT_5[0], TPL_RIGHT_5[0],
        TPL_RIGHT_5[4], TPL_LEFT_5[4]
    ], dtype=np.float32)

    # 1. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xoay vÃ  tÃ¬m 4 má»‘c gÃ³c OMR thÃ­ch á»©ng vá»›i camera di Ä‘á»™ng
    image_bgr, corners_4 = auto_orient_sheet_robust(raw_bgr)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape

    # Táº¦NG 1: Náº®N 4 Dáº¢I KHI Äá»¦ 10 Má»C (DÃ€NH CHO Báº¢N SCAN PHáº²NG)
    lc, rc, _ = detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray)
    if len(lc) <= 15 and len(rc) <= 15:
        src_left = select_best_5(lc, min_span=h * 0.50)
        src_right = select_best_5(rc, min_span=h * 0.50)
    else:
        src_left, src_right = None, None

    if src_left is not None and src_right is not None:
        canvas = np.zeros((out_h, out_w, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        band_bounds = [
            (0, int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[1, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[1, 1])), int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[2, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[2, 1])), int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[3, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[3, 1])), out_h),
        ]
        for b_idx in range(4):
            src_quad = np.array([
                src_left[b_idx], src_right[b_idx],
                src_right[b_idx + 1], src_left[b_idx + 1]
            ], dtype=np.float32)
            dst_quad = np.array([
                TPL_LEFT_5[b_idx], TPL_RIGHT_5[b_idx],
                TPL_RIGHT_5[b_idx + 1], TPL_LEFT_5[b_idx + 1]
            ], dtype=np.float32)
            H = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src_quad, dst_quad)
            warped_band = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
            y0, y1 = band_bounds[b_idx]
            canvas[y0:y1, :] = warped_band[y0:y1, :]

        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas)
        return canvas

    # Táº¦NG 2: Náº®N 4 Má»C GÃ“C OMR THÃCH á»¨NG (DÃ€NH CHO CAMERA DI Äá»˜NG Cáº¦M TAY)
    if corners_4 is not None:
        H_corners = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(corners_4, tpl_corners)
        canvas = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H_corners, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas)
        return canvas

    # Táº¦NG 3: Dá»° PHÃ’NG VIá»€N GIáº¤Y A4 TRÃŠN Ná»€N BÃ€N
    paper_corners = detect_a4_paper_contour(gray)
    if paper_corners is not None:
        dst_a4 = np.array([
            [0, 0], [out_w, 0],
            [out_w, out_h], [0, out_h]
        ], dtype=np.float32)
        H_paper = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(paper_corners, dst_a4)
        canvas_a4 = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H_paper, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas_a4)
        return canvas_a4

    raise RuntimeError(
        "ALIGNMENT_FAILED: KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»§ 4 má»‘c Ä‘á»‹nh vá»‹ cá»§a bÃ i thi. "
        "HÃ£y giá»¯ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i hÆ°á»›ng tháº³ng vÃ o bÃ i thi vÃ  chá»¥p Ä‘á»§ 4 gÃ³c phiáº¿u."
    )

# app.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""

  Giáº£m 96% dung lÆ°á»£ng á»• cá»©ng (tá»« ~29MB/bÃ i xuá»‘ng cÃ²n ~1.1MB/bÃ i).
- Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng dá»n dáº¹p file Raw sau khi Ä‘Ã£ náº¯n áº£nh thÃ nh cÃ´ng.
- Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng giá»›i háº¡n kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh Ä‘áº§u vÃ o (max 2600px) Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng trÃ n RAM khi náº¡p áº£nh camera lá»›n.
- Bá»• sung hÃ m sanitize_ascii_filename: Triá»‡t tiÃªu hoÃ n toÃ n lá»—i HTTP 500 khi xuáº¥t Excel vÃ  PDF.
- Xuáº¥t PDF báº±ng PyMuPDF (fitz) tuáº§n tá»±, khÃ´ng ngá»‘n RAM.

def sanitize_ascii_filename(text: str, fallback: str = "FILE") -> str:
    """Khá»­ 100% dáº¥u tiáº¿ng Viá»‡t vÃ  kÃ½ tá»± Ä‘áº·c biá»‡t cho tÃªn file Ä‘Ã­nh kÃ¨m HTTP Header."""
    if not text:
        return fallback
    nfkd = unicodedata.normalize('NFKD', str(text))
    ascii_text = nfkd.encode('ASCII', 'ignore').decode('ASCII')
    clean = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.]', '_', ascii_text)
    clean = re.sub(r'_+', '_', clean).strip('_')
    return clean if clean else fallback

class CreateExamRequest(BaseModel):
    subject: str
    period: str
    class_name: str
    school_year: str


class UpdateSubmissionRequest(BaseModel):
    submission_id: str
    sbd: str
    exam_code: str
    part1: Dict[str, str]
    part2: Dict[str, Dict[str, str]]
    part3: Dict[str, Optional[float]]


class SmartPasteRequest(BaseModel):
    text: str
    default_made: Optional[str] = "0001"

DB_DATA: Dict[str, Any] = {
    "active_exam_id": "default_exam",
    "exams": {
        "default_exam": {
            "id": "default_exam",
            "exam_info": {
                "subject": "Sinh há»c",
                "period": "Giá»¯a ká»³ I",
                "class_name": "11A",
                "school_year": "2026-2027",
                "is_configured": False
            },
            "exam_data": None,
            "submissions": {},
            "created_at": "05/11/2026 08:00"
        }
    }
}

if DB_FILE.exists():
    try:
        with open(DB_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            disk_db = json.load(f)
            if "exams" in disk_db:
                DB_DATA = disk_db
            elif "exam_info" in disk_db:
                old_id = "exam_legacy"
                DB_DATA = {
                    "active_exam_id": old_id,
                    "exams": {
                        old_id: {
                            "id": old_id,
                            "exam_info": disk_db.get("exam_info", {}),
                            "exam_data": disk_db.get("exam_data"),
                            "submissions": disk_db.get("submissions", {}),
                            "created_at": "04/11/2026"
                        }
                    }
                }
    except Exception as e:
        print("[!] Lá»—i náº¡p database:", e)


def get_current_exam() -> Dict[str, Any]:
    active_id = DB_DATA.get("active_exam_id", "default_exam")
    if active_id not in DB_DATA["exams"]:
        if DB_DATA["exams"]:
            active_id = list(DB_DATA["exams"].keys())[0]
            DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = active_id
        else:
            new_id = f"exam_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"
            DB_DATA["exams"][new_id] = {
                "id": new_id,
                "exam_info": {
                    "subject": "Sinh há»c",
                    "period": "Giá»¯a ká»³ I",
                    "class_name": "11A",
                    "school_year": "2026-2027",
                    "is_configured": False
                },
                "exam_data": None,
                "submissions": {},
                "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
            }
            DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = new_id
            active_id = new_id
    return DB_DATA["exams"][active_id]


def save_to_disk():
    with open(DB_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(DB_DATA, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)


def delete_submission_files(submission_id: str):
    """XÃ³a sáº¡ch cÃ¡c tá»‡p áº£nh liÃªn quan cá»§a má»™t bÃ i lÃ m (há»— trá»£ cáº£ Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng .png vÃ  .jpg má»›i)."""
    extensions = [".png", ".jpg", ".jpeg", ".webp"]
    for ext in extensions:
        targets = [
            UPLOAD_DIR / f"{submission_id}_raw{ext}",
            UPLOAD_DIR / f"{submission_id}_aligned{ext}",
            GRADED_DIR / f"{submission_id}_graded{ext}",
            GRADED_DIR / f"{submission_id}_preview{ext}"
        ]
        for p in targets:
            if p.exists():
                try:
                    p.unlink()
                except Exception:
                    pass


current_exam = get_current_exam()
pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=current_exam.get("exam_data"))

def get_template_content(template_name: str) -> str:
    candidates = [
        Path("templates") / template_name,
        Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent / "templates" / template_name if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) else None,
        Path(getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', '')) / "templates" / template_name if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) else None,
        Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent / "_internal" / "templates" / template_name if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) else None,
    ]
    for p in candidates:
        if p and p.exists() and p.is_file():
            return p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    return f"<h2>KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tá»‡p giao diá»‡n: {template_name}</h2>"


@app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def serve_home():
    return HTMLResponse(content=get_template_content("index.html"))


@app.get("/scanner", response_class=HTMLResponse)
async def serve_mobile_scanner():
    return HTMLResponse(content=get_template_content("mobile_scanner.html"))


@app.get("/manifest.json")
async def get_manifest():
    content = get_template_content("manifest.json")
    return Response(content=content, media_type="application/manifest+json")


@app.get("/sw.js")
async def get_service_worker():
    content = get_template_content("sw.js")
    return Response(content=content, media_type="application/javascript")


@app.get("/icons/{icon_name}")
async def get_pwa_icon(icon_name: str):
    icon_path = Path("templates/icons") / icon_name
    if not icon_path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Icon not found")
    return FileResponse(str(icon_path), media_type="image/png")


@app.get("/api/session")
async def get_session():
    exam = get_current_exam()
    all_exams_summary = [
        {
            "id": eid,
            "title": f"{edata['exam_info']['subject']} - {edata['exam_info']['period']} ({edata['exam_info']['class_name']})",
            "submission_count": len(edata.get("submissions", {})),
            "created_at": edata.get("created_at", ""),
            "is_active": (eid == DB_DATA["active_exam_id"])
        }
        for eid, edata in DB_DATA["exams"].items()
    ]
    return {
        "active_exam_id": DB_DATA["active_exam_id"],
        "exam_info": exam.get("exam_info"),
        "exam_data": exam.get("exam_data"),
        "submissions": list(exam.get("submissions", {}).values()),
        "all_exams": all_exams_summary
    }


@app.post("/api/create-exam")
async def create_exam(payload: CreateExamRequest):
    global pipeline
    new_id = f"exam_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"

    new_exam = {
        "id": new_id,
        "exam_info": {
            "subject": payload.subject.strip(),
            "period": payload.period.strip(),
            "class_name": payload.class_name.strip(),
            "school_year": payload.school_year.strip(),
            "is_configured": False
        },
        "exam_data": None,
        "submissions": {},
        "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
    }

    DB_DATA["exams"][new_id] = new_exam
    DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = new_id
    pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=None)

    save_to_disk()
    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "active_exam_id": new_id, "exam_info": new_exam["exam_info"]}


@app.post("/api/switch-exam/{exam_id}")
async def switch_exam(exam_id: str):
    global pipeline
    if exam_id not in DB_DATA["exams"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y Ä‘á»£t kiá»ƒm tra nÃ y")

    DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = exam_id
    active_exam = DB_DATA["exams"][exam_id]
    pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=active_exam.get("exam_data"))

    save_to_disk()
    return {
        "status": "SUCCESS",
        "active_exam_id": exam_id,
        "exam_info": active_exam["exam_info"],
        "submission_count": len(active_exam.get("submissions", {}))
    }


@app.delete("/api/delete-exam/{exam_id}")
async def delete_exam(exam_id: str):
    global pipeline
    if exam_id not in DB_DATA["exams"]:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="Äá»£t kiá»ƒm tra khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i")

    target_exam = DB_DATA["exams"][exam_id]
    for sub_id in list(target_exam.get("submissions", {}).keys()):
        delete_submission_files(sub_id)

    del DB_DATA["exams"][exam_id]

    if DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] == exam_id:
        if DB_DATA["exams"]:
            DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = list(DB_DATA["exams"].keys())[0]
        else:
            new_id = f"exam_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}"
            DB_DATA["exams"][new_id] = {
                "id": new_id,
                "exam_info": {
                    "subject": "Sinh há»c",
                    "period": "Giá»¯a ká»³ I",
                    "class_name": "11A",
                    "school_year": "2026-2027",
                    "is_configured": False
                },
                "exam_data": None,
                "submissions": {},
                "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
            }
            DB_DATA["active_exam_id"] = new_id

    active_exam = get_current_exam()
    pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=active_exam.get("exam_data"))
    save_to_disk()

    return {
        "status": "SUCCESS",
        "active_exam_id": DB_DATA["active_exam_id"],
        "remaining_exams_count": len(DB_DATA["exams"])
    }


# ==============================================================================
# 4. ROUTE CHáº¤M THI TUáº¦N Tá»° & Tá»I Æ¯U HÃ“A KHÃ”NG GIAN LÆ¯U TRá»®
# ==============================================================================

def _process_and_save(img_bgr: np.ndarray, file_name: str, sub_id: str, exam: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    """
    HÃ m lÃµi cháº¥m thi - Tá»‘i Æ°u hÃ³a lÆ°u trá»¯ 96%:
    1. Giá»›i háº¡n Ä‘á»™ phÃ¢n giáº£i áº£nh thÃ´ Ä‘áº§u vÃ o náº¿u quÃ¡ lá»›n (max 2600px).
    2. Tiá»n kiá»ƒm Ä‘á»‹nh cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng áº£nh (Quality Gate) trÆ°á»›c khi náº¯n má»‘c.
    3. Náº¯n pháº³ng phá»‘i cáº£nh Ä‘a táº§ng.
    4. LÆ°u áº£nh náº¯n pháº³ng dáº¡ng JPEG Quality 92 (~450KB).
    5. XÃ³a tá»‡p áº£nh thÃ´ _raw sau khi Ä‘Ã£ náº¯n xong Ä‘á»ƒ tiáº¿t kiá»‡m 100% dung lÆ°á»£ng dÆ° thá»«a.
    6. LÆ°u áº£nh tem bÃºt Ä‘á» dáº¡ng JPEG Quality 90 (~480KB) vÃ  báº£n nÃ©n WebP Quality 80 (~180KB).
    """
    # 1. Giá»›i háº¡n Ä‘á»™ phÃ¢n giáº£i áº£nh Ä‘áº§u vÃ o náº¿u lÃ  áº£nh chá»¥p siÃªu lá»›n tá»« Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i
    h, w = img_bgr.shape[:2]
    max_dim = max(h, w)
    if max_dim > 2600:
        scale = 2600.0 / max_dim
        img_bgr = cv2.resize(img_bgr, (int(round(w * scale)), int(round(h * scale))), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)

    # 2. Tiá»n kiá»ƒm Ä‘á»‹nh cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng áº£nh (Quality Gate) trÆ°á»›c khi náº¯n má»‘c
    is_valid, q_report = ImageQualityAssessment.evaluate(img_bgr)
    if not is_valid:
        reasons = q_report.get("reasons", ["áº¢nh khÃ´ng Ä‘áº¡t chuáº©n cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng nháº­n diá»‡n."])
        reason_str = "; ".join(reasons)
        user_friendly_msg = f"áº¢nh khÃ´ng Ä‘áº¡t chuáº©n kiá»ƒm Ä‘á»‹nh ({reason_str}). Vui lÃ²ng chá»¥p láº¡i rÃµ nÃ©t vÃ  Ä‘á»§ sÃ¡ng."

        # LuÃ´n lÆ°u áº£nh gá»‘c vÃ o GRADED_DIR Ä‘á»ƒ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m xem láº¡i Ä‘Æ°á»£c ngay áº£nh vá»«a chá»¥p
        graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
        cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), img_bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 85])
        preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
        cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), img_bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 75])

        err_sub = {
            "submission_id": sub_id,
            "file_name": file_name,
            "sbd": "CHÆ¯A RÃ•",
            "exam_code": "CHÆ¯A RÃ•",
            "has_error": True,
            "status": "QUALITY_CHECK_FAILED",
            "error_msg": user_friendly_msg,
            "anomalies": [{"type": "QUALITY_ERROR", "msg": r} for r in reasons],
            "scores": {"status": "ERROR", "p1": 0.0, "p2": 0.0, "p3": 0.0, "total": 0.0},
            "details": {"part1": {}, "part2": {}, "part3": {}},
            "quality_metrics": q_report.get("metrics", {})
        }
        exam["submissions"][sub_id] = err_sub
        return err_sub

    temp_raw = UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_raw.jpg"
    temp_aligned = UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.jpg"
    
    # Ghi áº£nh raw dáº¡ng JPEG cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng cao
    cv2.imwrite(str(temp_raw), img_bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])

    try:
        # Náº¯n áº£nh vÃ  tá»± xoay 4 hÆ°á»›ng
        align_sheet_4band(temp_raw, temp_aligned)
        target_img = cv2.imread(str(temp_aligned))

        if target_img is None:
            raise RuntimeError("ALIGNMENT_FAILED: KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c áº£nh sau khi náº¯n pháº³ng.")

        # XÃ³a file raw sau khi náº¯n thÃ nh cÃ´ng Ä‘á»ƒ tiáº¿t kiá»‡m dung lÆ°á»£ng Ä‘Ä©a
        if temp_raw.exists():
            try:
                temp_raw.unlink()
            except Exception:
                pass

        # Cháº¥m bÃ i báº±ng lÃµi quang há»c Ink Map
        res = pipeline.process_sheet(target_img, file_name)
        res["submission_id"] = sub_id
        res["original_filename"] = file_name
        res["has_error"] = False
        res["quality_metrics"] = q_report.get("metrics", {})

        # LÆ°u áº£nh Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m dáº¡ng JPEG Quality 90 sáº¯c nÃ©t nhÆ°ng dung lÆ°á»£ng siÃªu nháº¹ (~450KB)
        graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
        cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), res["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])
        
        # Sinh báº£n xem trÆ°á»›c WebP Quality 80 phá»¥c vá»¥ hiá»ƒn thá»‹ mÆ°á»£t mÃ  (~180KB)
        preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
        cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), res["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])
        del res["graded_image_bgr"]

        exam["submissions"][sub_id] = res
        return res

    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        # LuÃ´n lÆ°u áº£nh gá»‘c vÃ o GRADED_DIR Ä‘á»ƒ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng báº¥m xem láº¡i Ä‘Æ°á»£c ngay áº£nh vá»«a chá»¥p
        graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
        if not graded_path.exists() and img_bgr is not None:
            cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), img_bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 85])
            preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
            cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), img_bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 75])

        if temp_raw.exists():
            try:
                temp_raw.unlink()
            except Exception:
                pass

        err_msg = str(err)
        if "ALIGNMENT_FAILED" in err_msg:
            user_friendly_msg = "áº¢nh bá»‹ máº¥t má»‘c Ä‘á»‹nh vá»‹ hoáº·c quÃ¡ nghiÃªng. HÃ£y chá»¥p tháº³ng gÃ³c vÃ  Ä‘á»§ sÃ¡ng."
        else:
            user_friendly_msg = f"Lá»—i xá»­ lÃ½ bÃ i lÃ m: {err_msg}"

        err_sub = {
            "submission_id": sub_id,
            "file_name": file_name,
            "sbd": "CHÆ¯A RÃ•",
            "exam_code": "CHÆ¯A RÃ•",
            "has_error": True,
            "status": "PROCESSING_ERROR",
            "error_msg": user_friendly_msg,
            "anomalies": [{"type": "ALIGN_ERROR", "msg": user_friendly_msg}],
            "scores": {"status": "ERROR", "p1": 0.0, "p2": 0.0, "p3": 0.0, "total": 0.0},
            "details": {"part1": {}, "part2": {}, "part3": {}},
            "quality_metrics": q_report.get("metrics", {})
        }
        exam["submissions"][sub_id] = err_sub
        return err_sub


@app.post("/api/grade-single-file")
async def grade_single_file(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    exam = get_current_exam()

    if not exam.get("exam_info", {}).get("is_configured", False) or not exam.get("exam_data", {}).get("answer_keys"):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Äá»£t kiá»ƒm tra hiá»‡n táº¡i chÆ°a Ä‘Æ°á»£c náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n. Vui lÃ²ng báº¥m 'Náº¡p File ÄÃ¡p Ãn' trÆ°á»›c khi cháº¥m bÃ i."
        )

    raw_bytes = await file.read()
    new_subs = []

    if file.filename.lower().endswith(".pdf"):
        try:
            doc = fitz.open(stream=raw_bytes, filetype="pdf")
            for page_idx in range(len(doc)):
                page = doc[page_idx]
                pix = page.get_pixmap(dpi=200)
                img_np = np.frombuffer(pix.samples, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(pix.height, pix.width, pix.n)
                img_bgr = cv2.cvtColor(img_np, cv2.COLOR_RGBA2BGR if pix.n == 4 else cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR)
                sub_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
                page_name = f"{file.filename}_Trang_{page_idx + 1}"
                res = _process_and_save(img_bgr, page_name, sub_id, exam)
                new_subs.append(res)
            doc.close()
        except Exception as err:
            traceback.print_exc()
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i Ä‘á»c file PDF: {err}")
    else:
        np_arr = np.frombuffer(raw_bytes, np.uint8)
        img_bgr = cv2.imdecode(np_arr, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
        if img_bgr is None:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ giáº£i mÃ£ file áº£nh.")
        sub_id = str(uuid.uuid4())[:8]
        res = _process_and_save(img_bgr, file.filename, sub_id, exam)
        # Giá»¯ láº¡i bÃ i thi trong danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»ƒ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng cÃ³ thá»ƒ báº¥m vÃ o xem chi tiáº¿t áº£nh chá»¥p vÃ  nguyÃªn nhÃ¢n lá»—i
        new_subs.append(res)

    save_to_disk()
    return {
        "status": "SUCCESS",
        "new_submissions": new_subs,
        "total_submissions_count": len(exam["submissions"])
    }

@app.post("/api/update-submission")
async def update_submission(payload: dict):
    """Cáº­p nháº­t SBD, MÃ£ Ä‘á», ÄÃ¡p Ã¡n; tá»± Ä‘á»™ng cháº¥m láº¡i vÃ  váº½ láº¡i tem bÃºt Ä‘á» Cá»™t 3."""
    try:
        exam = get_current_exam()
        sub_id = payload.get("submission_id")
        if not sub_id or sub_id not in exam.get("submissions", {}):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bÃ i thi cáº§n cáº­p nháº­t.")

        sub = exam["submissions"][sub_id]

        new_sbd = str(payload.get("sbd", sub.get("sbd", ""))).strip()
        new_exam_code = str(payload.get("exam_code", sub.get("exam_code", ""))).strip().zfill(4)

        sub["sbd"] = new_sbd
        sub["exam_code"] = new_exam_code

        if "part1" in payload and isinstance(payload["part1"], dict):
            sub["details"]["part1"] = {int(k): str(v).upper() for k, v in payload["part1"].items() if v}
        if "part2" in payload and isinstance(payload["part2"], dict):
            sub["details"]["part2"] = {int(k): {str(sk).lower(): str(sv).upper() for sk, sv in sdict.items()} for k, sdict in payload["part2"].items() if sdict}
        
        if "part3" in payload and isinstance(payload["part3"], dict):
            p3_clean = {}
            for k, v in payload["part3"].items():
                if v is not None and str(v).strip() != "":
                    try:
                        f_val = float(str(v).replace(",", "."))
                        p3_clean[int(k)] = {
                            "raw_string": str(v),
                            "normalized_value": f_val,
                            "is_valid": True,
                            "anomalies": []
                        }
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
            if p3_clean:
                sub["details"]["part3"] = p3_clean

        exam_data = exam.get("exam_data")
        if exam_data and "answer_keys" in exam_data:
            pipeline.answer_keys = normalize_answer_keys(exam_data["answer_keys"])

        new_scores = pipeline._grade(
            new_exam_code,
            sub["details"].get("part1", {}),
            sub["details"].get("part2", {}),
            sub["details"].get("part3", {})
        )
        sub["scores"] = new_scores
        sub["has_error"] = False
        sub["error_msg"] = None
        sub["status"] = "GRADED" if new_scores.get("status") == "GRADED" else "NEEDS_REVIEW"
        sub["updated_at"] = datetime.now().isoformat()

        try:
            # TÃ¬m áº£nh ná»n Ä‘Ã£ náº¯n pháº³ng (há»— trá»£ cáº£ Ä‘uÃ´i .jpg vÃ  .png)
            base_img = None
            candidates = [
                UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.jpg",
                UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png",
                GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.jpg",
                GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png",
                GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg",
                GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.png",
            ]
            for c_path in candidates:
                if c_path.exists():
                    base_img = cv2.imread(str(c_path))
                    if base_img is not None:
                        if "graded" in c_path.name:
                            base_img[185:440, 65:410] = (255, 255, 255)
                        break

            if base_img is not None:
                key_dict = pipeline.answer_keys.get(new_exam_code, {})
                stamped = VisualStamper.stamp_graded_sheet(
                    aligned_bgr=base_img,
                    submission=sub,
                    p1_coords=pipeline.p1_coords,
                    p2_coords=pipeline.p2_coords,
                    p3_coords=pipeline.p3_coords,
                    key_dict=key_dict,
                    sbd_columns=pipeline.sbd_columns,
                    made_columns=pipeline.made_columns
                )
                graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
                cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), stamped, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])
                
                preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
                cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), stamped, [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])
                print(f"[OK] Da ve lai tem but do: {sub_id} (Diem: {new_scores['total']})")
        except Exception as stamp_err:
            print(f"[!] Canh bao ve tem but do: {stamp_err}")

        save_to_disk()

        return {
            "status": "success",
            "message": "ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t vÃ  cháº¥m láº¡i thÃ nh cÃ´ng!",
            "updated_submission": sub
        }

    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Lá»—i cáº­p nháº­t mÃ¡y chá»§: {str(e)}")


@app.delete("/api/delete-submission/{submission_id}")
async def delete_submission(submission_id: str):
    exam = get_current_exam()
    if submission_id not in exam.get("submissions", {}):
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bÃ i lÃ m cáº§n xÃ³a")

    delete_submission_files(submission_id)
    del exam["submissions"][submission_id]
    save_to_disk()

    return {"status": "SUCCESS", "remaining_count": len(exam["submissions"])}


@app.get("/api/crop-info/{submission_id}")
async def get_crop_info(submission_id: str):
    # TÃ¬m áº£nh náº¯n pháº³ng Ä‘á»ƒ cáº¯t thÃ´ng tin viáº¿t tay
    aligned_path = None
    for ext in [".jpg", ".png"]:
        p = UPLOAD_DIR / f"{submission_id}_aligned{ext}"
        if p.exists():
            aligned_path = p
            break
        p = GRADED_DIR / f"{submission_id}_aligned{ext}"
        if p.exists():
            aligned_path = p
            break
        p = GRADED_DIR / f"{submission_id}_graded{ext}"
        if p.exists():
            aligned_path = p
            break

    if not aligned_path or not aligned_path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bÃ i lÃ m")

    img = cv2.imread(str(aligned_path))
    if img is None:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c tá»‡p áº£nh bÃ i lÃ m.")

    # VÃ¹ng cáº¯t thÃ´ng tin thÃ­ sinh: Y tá»« 205 Ä‘áº¿n 725, X tá»« 440 Ä‘áº¿n 1225
    crop = img[205:725, 440:1225]

    _, buffer = cv2.imencode(".jpg", crop, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 92])
    return Response(
        content=buffer.tobytes(),
        media_type="image/jpeg",
        headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache"}
    )


@app.get("/api/graded-image/{sub_id}")
async def get_graded_image(sub_id: str):
    """Phá»¥c vá»¥ áº£nh WebP nÃ©n nháº¹ káº¿t há»£p HTTP Cache ETag."""
    webp_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
    jpg_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
    png_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.png"

    source_path = None
    for p in [jpg_path, png_path]:
        if p.exists():
            source_path = p
            break

    if not source_path and not webp_path.exists():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y áº£nh bÃ i lÃ m Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m.")

    need_regenerate = False
    if not webp_path.exists():
        need_regenerate = True
    elif source_path and source_path.stat().st_mtime > webp_path.stat().st_mtime:
        need_regenerate = True

    if need_regenerate and source_path:
        bgr = cv2.imread(str(source_path))
        if bgr is not None:
            cv2.imwrite(str(webp_path), bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])

    target_file = webp_path if webp_path.exists() else source_path
    media_type = "image/webp" if target_file.suffix == ".webp" else "image/jpeg"
    last_modified = int(target_file.stat().st_mtime)

    return FileResponse(
        target_file,
        media_type=media_type,
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "public, max-age=86400",
            "ETag": f'"{sub_id}-{last_modified}"'
        }
    )


@app.get("/api/image/{sub_id}")
async def get_image_alias(sub_id: str):
    """BÃ­ danh phá»¥c vá»¥ áº£nh bÃ i lÃ m cho cÃ¡c client gá»i /api/image/{sub_id}"""
    return await get_graded_image(sub_id)

@app.get("/api/download-template")
async def download_template():
    tpl_path = Path("output/MAU_DAP_AN_TRAC_NGHIEM_2025.xlsx")

        output_path=tpl_path,
        exam_name=f"{info.get('subject', 'Sinh há»c')} - {info.get('period', 'Giá»¯a ká»³ I')}",
        subject=info.get("subject", "Sinh há»c"),
        period=info.get("period", "Giá»¯a ká»³ I")
    )

    return Response(
        content=file_bytes,
        media_type="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet",
        headers={
            "Content-Disposition": 'attachment; filename="MAU_DAP_AN_TRAC_NGHIEM_2025.xlsx"'
        }
    )


@app.post("/api/upload-answer-key")
async def upload_answer_key(file: UploadFile = File(...)):
    global pipeline
    raw_bytes = await file.read()
    temp_path = UPLOAD_DIR / "uploaded_key.xlsx"
    temp_path.write_bytes(raw_bytes)

    try:
        exam_data = ExamManager.parse_excel_config(temp_path)
        exam = get_current_exam()
        exam["exam_data"] = exam_data
        exam["exam_info"]["is_configured"] = True

        pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=exam_data)

        # Cháº¥m láº¡i cÃ¡c bÃ i thi hiá»‡n cÃ³ theo Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n má»›i
        for sub_id, sub in exam["submissions"].items():
            aligned_p = None
            for ext in [".jpg", ".png"]:
                p = UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned{ext}"
                if p.exists():
                    aligned_p = p
                    break
                p = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned{ext}"
                if p.exists():
                    aligned_p = p
                    break

            if aligned_p and aligned_p.exists():
                img = cv2.imread(str(aligned_p))
                if img is not None:
                    updated = pipeline.process_sheet(img, sub["file_name"], forced_exam_code=sub["exam_code"])
                    updated["submission_id"] = sub_id
                    updated["original_filename"] = sub["file_name"]
                    
                    graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
                    cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])
                    
                    preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
                    cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])
                    
                    del updated["graded_image_bgr"]
                    exam["submissions"][sub_id] = updated

        save_to_disk()
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "exam_name": exam_data["exam_name"],
            "codes_loaded": list(exam_data["answer_keys"].keys()),
            "exam_info": exam["exam_info"]
        }
    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i file Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: {err}")


@app.post("/api/smart-paste-answer-key")
async def smart_paste_answer_key(payload: SmartPasteRequest):
    global pipeline
    if not payload.text or not payload.text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¡n ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n.")

    try:
        parsed_result = ExamManager.smart_parse_text_keys(payload.text, default_made=payload.default_made or "0001")
        parsed_keys = parsed_result.get("answer_keys", {})
        if not parsed_keys:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c cÃ¢u há»i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n nÃ o. HÃ£y kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng dÃ¡n (VD: 1.A 2.B hoáº·c 1A 2B).")

        exam = get_current_exam()
        if not exam.get("exam_data"):
            exam["exam_data"] = {
                "exam_name": f"{exam['exam_info'].get('subject', 'Äá» thi')} - {exam['exam_info'].get('period', 'Kiá»ƒm tra')}",
                "subject": exam["exam_info"].get("subject", "Sinh há»c"),
                "period": exam["exam_info"].get("period", "Giá»¯a ká»³ I"),
                "config": parsed_result.get("config", {"part1_max": 18, "part2_max": 4, "part3_max": 6}),
                "answer_keys": {},
                "scoring_rules": {
                    "part1": {"points_per_question": 0.25},
                    "part2": {
                        "type": "graduated_moet",
                        "table": {"1": 0.1, "2": 0.25, "3": 0.5, "4": 1.0}
                    },
                    "part3": {"points_per_question": 0.25}
                }
            }
        elif "config" not in exam["exam_data"]:
            exam["exam_data"]["config"] = parsed_result.get("config", {"part1_max": 18, "part2_max": 4, "part3_max": 6})

        # Cáº­p nháº­t cÃ¡c mÃ£ Ä‘á» má»›i parsed vÃ o answer_keys (merge tá»«ng pháº§n, khÃ´ng xÃ³a Pháº§n II, III)
        for code, new_k in parsed_keys.items():
            if code not in exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"]:
                exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"][code] = new_k
            else:
                for part in ["part1", "part2", "part3"]:
                    if new_k.get(part):
                        exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"][code][part] = new_k[part]
        exam["exam_info"]["is_configured"] = True

        pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=exam["exam_data"])

        # TÃ¡i cháº¥m cÃ¡c bÃ i thi cÃ³ trong danh sÃ¡ch
        re_graded_count = 0
        for sub_id, sub in exam.get("submissions", {}).items():
            sub_code = sub.get("exam_code", "")
            if sub_code in exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"]:
                aligned_p = None
                for p in [GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png", UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png", GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.jpg"]:
                    if p.exists():
                        aligned_p = p
                        break

                if aligned_p and aligned_p.exists():
                    img = cv2.imread(str(aligned_p))
                    if img is not None:
                        updated = pipeline.process_sheet(img, sub.get("file_name", ""), forced_exam_code=sub_code)
                        updated["submission_id"] = sub_id
                        updated["original_filename"] = sub.get("file_name", "")
                        
                        graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
                        cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])
                        
                        preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
                        cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])
                        
                        del updated["graded_image_bgr"]
                        exam["submissions"][sub_id] = updated
                        re_graded_count += 1

        save_to_disk()
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "codes_loaded": list(parsed_keys.keys()),
            "total_codes": list(exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"].keys()),
            "re_graded_count": re_graded_count,
            "details": {
                c: {
                    "part1": len(k.get("part1", {})),
                    "part2": len(k.get("part2", {})),
                    "part3": len(k.get("part3", {}))
                } for c, k in parsed_keys.items()
            },
            "exam_info": exam["exam_info"]
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n dÃ¡n: {err}")


@app.get("/api/export-excel")
async def export_excel():
    """Xuáº¥t báº£ng Ä‘iá»ƒm Excel an toÃ n; Header filename thuáº§n ASCII chá»‘ng sáº­p HTTP 500."""
    try:
        exam = get_current_exam()
        subs = exam.get("submissions", {})
        if not subs:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="ChÆ°a cÃ³ bÃ i thi nÃ o trong Ä‘á»£t Ä‘á»ƒ xuáº¥t báº£ng Ä‘iá»ƒm.")

        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "BangDiem"

        headers = ["STT", "TÃªn Tá»‡p", "Sá»‘ BÃ¡o Danh (VÃ o Sá»•)", "MÃ£ Äá»", "Äiá»ƒm P1", "Äiá»ƒm P2", "Äiá»ƒm P3", "Tá»”NG ÄIá»‚M", "Tráº¡ng ThÃ¡i", "Cáº£nh BÃ¡o"]
        ws.append(headers)

        for idx, (sub_id, sub) in enumerate(subs.items(), start=1):
            sc = sub.get("scores", {})
            ws.append([
                idx,
                sub.get("file_name", ""),
                sub.get("sbd", ""),
                sub.get("exam_code", ""),
                sc.get("p1", 0.0),
                sc.get("p2", 0.0),
                sc.get("p3", 0.0),
                sc.get("total", 0.0),
                sub.get("status", "GRADED"),
                ", ".join([a["msg"] for a in sub.get("anomalies", [])]) if sub.get("anomalies") else "Há»£p lá»‡"
            ])

        excel_path = Path("output/BANG_DIEM_VAO_SO.xlsx")
        try:
            wb.save(excel_path)
        except PermissionError:
            excel_path = Path(f"output/BANG_DIEM_VAO_SO_{int(time.time())}.xlsx")
            wb.save(excel_path)

        info = exam.get("exam_info", {})
        sub_name = sanitize_ascii_filename(info.get("subject", "MON"))
        period_name = sanitize_ascii_filename(info.get("period", "KY"))
        download_name = f"BANG_DIEM_{sub_name}_{period_name}.xlsx"

        return FileResponse(
            path=str(excel_path),
            filename=download_name,
            media_type="application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
        )
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Lá»—i xuáº¥t Excel: {str(e)}")


@app.get("/api/export-graded-pdf")
async def export_graded_pdf():
    """Xuáº¥t táº­p phiáº¿u Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m thÃ nh PDF nhiá»u trang báº±ng PyMuPDF (fitz); Header thuáº§n ASCII."""
    try:
        exam = get_current_exam()
        subs = exam.get("submissions", {})
        if not subs:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="ChÆ°a cÃ³ bÃ i thi nÃ o trong Ä‘á»£t Ä‘á»ƒ xuáº¥t PDF.")

        image_paths = []
        for sub_id in subs.keys():
            # QuÃ©t tÃ¬m cáº£ Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng áº£nh .jpg má»›i vÃ  .png cÅ©
            for ext in [".jpg", ".png"]:
                p = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded{ext}"
                if p.exists():
                    image_paths.append(p)
                    break

        if not image_paths:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tá»‡p áº£nh bÃ i thi Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m nÃ o trÃªn á»• cá»©ng.")

        pdf_out = Path("output/TAP_PHIEU_DA_CHAM.pdf")

        doc = fitz.open()
        for p in image_paths:
            try:
                img_doc = fitz.open(str(p))
                pdf_bytes = img_doc.convert_to_pdf()
                img_doc.close()
                img_pdf = fitz.open("pdf", pdf_bytes)
                doc.insert_pdf(img_pdf)
                img_pdf.close()
            except Exception as page_err:
                print(f"[!] Bá» qua áº£nh lá»—i {p.name}: {page_err}")

        if len(doc) == 0:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail="KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº¡o trang PDF tá»« cÃ¡c áº£nh bÃ i thi.")

        try:
            doc.save(str(pdf_out))
        except PermissionError:
            pdf_out = Path(f"output/TAP_PHIEU_DA_CHAM_{int(time.time())}.pdf")
            doc.save(str(pdf_out))
        finally:
            doc.close()

        info = exam.get("exam_info", {})
        sub_name = sanitize_ascii_filename(info.get("subject", "MON"))
        class_name = sanitize_ascii_filename(info.get("class_name", "LOP"))
        download_name = f"TAP_PHIEU_DA_CHAM_{sub_name}_{class_name}.pdf"

        print(f"[OK] Da xuat thanh cong tap PDF gom {len(image_paths)} trang: {pdf_out.name}")

        return FileResponse(
            path=str(pdf_out),
            filename=download_name,
            media_type="application/pdf"
        )
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as e:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=500, detail=f"Lá»—i xuáº¥t PDF: {str(e)}")


@app.get("/api/export-pdf")
async def export_pdf_alias():
    """BÃ­ danh xuáº¥t táº­p phiáº¿u Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m thÃ nh PDF"""
    return await export_graded_pdf()

# clean_project.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
CÃ”NG Cá»¤ Dá»ŒN Dáº¸P VÃ€ CHUáº¨N HÃ“A Dá»° ÃN OMR TRÆ¯á»šC KHI XUáº¤T Báº¢N
Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng sao lÆ°u file cÅ© vÃ o archive_backup/ vÃ  tinh gá»n thÆ° má»¥c lÃ m viá»‡c.
"""
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

# 1. Danh sÃ¡ch file code thá»«a/thá»­ nghiá»‡m á»Ÿ thÆ° má»¥c gá»‘c cáº§n di dá»i vÃ o archive
OBSOLETE_ROOT_FILES = [
    "align_sheet.py",
    "benchmark_test.py",
    "bubble_detector.py",
    "detect_markers.py",
    "detect_part1.py",
    "detect_part2.py",
    "detect_part3.py",
    "detect_part3_backup.py",
    "detect_part3_shadowfix.py",
    "render_template.py",
    "test_bubble_detector.py",
    "test_benchmark_clean.py",
    "test_unified_pipeline.py",
]

# 2. Danh sÃ¡ch cÃ´ng cá»¥ hiá»‡u chuáº©n (di dá»i vÃ o tools/calibration/)
CALIBRATION_TOOLS = [
    "calibrate_answers.py",
    "calibrate_header.py",
    "calibrate_part1.py",
    "calibrate_part2.py",
    "calibrate_part3.py",
    "config.py",
    "convert_pdf.py",
]

# 3. Danh sÃ¡ch file cá»‘t lÃµi Báº®T BUá»˜C PHáº¢I Tá»’N Táº I
CRITICAL_FILES = [
    "app.py",
    "full_grading_pipeline.py",
    "align_sheet_v2.py",
    "visual_stamper.py",
    "exam_manager.py",
    "run_app.bat",
