def clean_project():
    root = Path(".")
    archive_dir = root / "archive_backup"
    tools_dir = root / "tools" / "calibration"

    archive_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    tools_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print("=" * 75)
    print("TIáº¾N TRÃŒNH TINH Gá»ŒN Dá»° ÃN OMR EXAM SYSTEM")
    print("=" * 75)

    # 1. Sao lÆ°u & Di dá»i cÃ¡c script thá»­ nghiá»‡m cÅ©
    moved_count = 0
    for fname in OBSOLETE_ROOT_FILES:
        fpath = root / fname
        if fpath.exists():
            shutil.move(str(fpath), str(archive_dir / fname))
            print(f"[*] ÄÃ£ chuyá»ƒn vÃ o archive: {fname}")
            moved_count += 1

    # 2. Gom cÃ¡c cÃ´ng cá»¥ hiá»‡u chuáº©n vÃ o tools/calibration/
    tools_count = 0
    for fname in CALIBRATION_TOOLS:
        fpath = root / fname
        if fpath.exists():
            shutil.move(str(fpath), str(tools_dir / fname))
            print(f"[*] ÄÃ£ chuyá»ƒn vÃ o tools/calibration: {fname}")
            tools_count += 1

    # 3. Dá»n sáº¡ch file rÃ¡c thá»­ nghiá»‡m trong thÆ° má»¥c output/
    output_dir = root / "output"
    deleted_output_count = 0
    if output_dir.exists():
        # XÃ³a cÃ¡c file káº¿t quáº£ scan cÅ© (bai_kiem_tra_*)
        for p in output_dir.glob("bai_kiem_tra_*_part*.*"):
            p.unlink()
            deleted_output_count += 1
        for p in output_dir.glob("bai_kiem_tra_*_debug*.*"):
            p.unlink()
            deleted_output_count += 1
        # XÃ³a database cÅ© v4.0 náº¿u cÃ²n
        old_db = output_dir / "submissions_db.json"
        if old_db.exists():
            old_db.unlink()
            print("[*] ÄÃ£ xÃ³a database cÅ©: output/submissions_db.json")
            deleted_output_count += 1

    print("-" * 75)
    print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ lÆ°u trá»¯ an toÃ n: {moved_count} file script cÅ©.")
    print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ gom gá»n: {tools_count} cÃ´ng cá»¥ hiá»‡u chuáº©n vÃ o tools/calibration/.")
    print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ dá»n dáº¹p: {deleted_output_count} file rÃ¡c trong output/.")

    # 4. Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh toÃ n váº¹n há»‡ thá»‘ng (Integrity Check)
    print("\nKIá»‚M TRA TÃNH TOÃ€N Váº¸N Há»† THá»NG:")
    all_ok = True
    for cfile in CRITICAL_FILES:
        p = root / cfile
        if p.exists():
            print(f"  [OK] {cfile}")
        else:
            print(f"  [MISSING] {cfile} <-- Lá»–I: THIáº¾U Tá»†P Cá»T LÃ•I!")
            all_ok = False

    print("=" * 75)
    if all_ok:
        print("[SUCCESS] Dá»° ÃN ÄÃƒ ÄÆ¯á»¢C LÃ€M Sáº CH VÃ€ Sáº´N SÃ€NG CHO PHÃT HÃ€NH!")
    else:
        print("[WARNING] PHÃT HIá»†N THIáº¾U Tá»†P, HÃƒY KIá»‚M TRA Láº I!")
    print("=" * 75)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    clean_project()


=== FILE: detect_part1.py ===

import cv2
import json
import csv
import argparse
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

FILL_THRESHOLD = 55
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 28
BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5

CHOICES = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]


# ==========================================
# DOC GIA TRI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    r = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    roi = gray[
        y-r:y+r+1,
        x-r:x+r+1
    ]

    if roi.shape != (2*r+1, 2*r+1):
        raise ValueError("O dap an nam ngoai anh.")

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= r**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN O TO
# ==========================================

def detect_part1(image_path):

    # Doc anh bai lam da can chinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc anh phieu mau trang
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do 160 o
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh bai lam khong khop phieu mau. "
            "Hay chay align_sheet.py truoc."
        )

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    questions = coordinate_data["questions"]

    if len(questions) != 40:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 40 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    results = []
    debug = student.copy()

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN I")
    print("=" * 65)

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        scores = {}

        # Doc do den cua 4 phuong an
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]

            reference_value = get_center_value(
                template_gray, x, y
            )

            student_value = get_center_value(
                student_gray, x, y
            )

            # Do muc do toi hon so voi phieu mau
            score = reference_value - student_value

            scores[choice] = round(
                max(0, score), 1
            )

        # Tim cac phuong an duoc to ro
        
        # ======================================
        # PHAN LOAI DAP AN THEO TUONG QUAN
        # ======================================

        ranked = sorted(
            scores.items(),
            key=lambda item: item[1],
            reverse=True
        )

        top_choice, top_score = ranked[0]
        second_choice, second_score = ranked[1]

        # Lay 2 diem thap nhat lam muc nen
        lowest_two = sorted(scores.values())[:2]
        baseline = float(np.mean(lowest_two))

        top_margin = top_score - baseline
        second_margin = second_score - baseline

        MARK_MARGIN = 32
        REVIEW_MARGIN = 15

        filled = []
        uncertain = []

        if top_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:

            if second_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:
                answer = "MULTIPLE"
                status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

                filled = [
                    choice
                    for choice in CHOICES
                    if scores[choice] - baseline >= MARK_MARGIN
                ]

            else:
                answer = top_choice
                status = "VALID"
                filled = [top_choice]

        elif top_margin >= REVIEW_MARGIN:

            answer = "REVIEW"
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
            uncertain = [top_choice]

        else:

            answer = ""
            status = "BLANK"

        
        # ======================================
        # LUU KET QUA CUA TUNG CAU
        # ======================================

        results.append({
            "question": q_number,
            "answer": answer,
            "status": status,
            "scores": scores.copy()
        })

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number:02d}: "
            f"{answer if answer else 'BO TRONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # Danh dau cac o tren anh kiem tra
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]
            x, y = int(x), int(y)

            if choice in filled:
                color = (0, 0, 255)       # Do: da to

            elif choice in uncertain:
                color = (0, 165, 255)     # Cam: can kiem tra

            else:
                color = (150, 150, 150)   # Xam: bo trong

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                9,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        cv2.putText(
            debug,
            answer if answer else "-",
            (int(first_x) + 12, int(first_y) + 4),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (255, 0, 0),
            1
        )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA CSV VA JSON
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.csv"
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.json"
    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1_debug.png"

    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "Cau",
            "Dap an",
            "Trang thai",
            "Diem A",
            "Diem B",
            "Diem C",
            "Diem D"
        ])

        for item in results:

            writer.writerow([
                item["question"],
                item["answer"],
                item["status"],
                item["scores"]["A"],
                item["scores"]["B"],
                item["scores"]["C"],
                item["scores"]["D"]
            ])

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("THONG KE NHAN DIEN")
    print("Nhan dang mot dap an:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu dap an:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDa luu:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default="output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png",
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        detect_part1(Path(args.image))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)



=== FILE: exam_manager.py ===
# exam_manager.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
MODULE QUáº¢N LÃ Cáº¤U HÃŒNH Äá»€ THI & ÄÃP ÃN CHUáº¨N HÃ“A Bá»˜ GD&ÄT 2025
- Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh thang Ä‘iá»ƒm trá»±c quan: ÄÃºng 1, 2, 3, 4 Ã½ chia thÃ nh 2 hÃ ng Ã´ trÃªn - Ã´ dÆ°á»›i rÃµ rÃ ng.
- Äáº§y Ä‘á»§ 40 cÃ¢u P1, 8 cÃ¢u P2 (32 Ã½), 6 cÃ¢u P3.
- Há»— trá»£ xuáº¥t ra cáº£ Ä‘Ä©a vÃ  bá»™ nhá»› Ä‘á»‡m (in-memory buffer) chá»‘ng lá»—i file lock trÃªn Windows.
"""
import io
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional
import openpyxl
from openpyxl.styles import Alignment, Border, Font, PatternFill, Side
from openpyxl.utils import get_column_letter


class ExamManager:
    MAX_P1 = 40
    MAX_P2 = 8
    MAX_P3 = 6

    @classmethod
    def generate_excel_template(
        cls,
        output_path: Optional[Path] = None,
        exam_name: str = "Kiá»ƒm tra tráº¯c nghiá»‡m",
        subject: str = "Sinh há»c",
        period: str = "Giá»¯a ká»³ I"
    ) -> bytes:
        """
        Táº¡o file Excel máº«u chuáº©n:
        - HÃ ng 3-5: Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh thang Ä‘iá»ƒm. Äáº·c biá»‡t Pháº§n II hiá»ƒn thá»‹ 4 cá»™t:
          Ã” trÃªn: ÄÃºng 1 Ã½ | ÄÃºng 2 Ã½ | ÄÃºng 3 Ã½ | ÄÃºng 4 Ã½
          Ã” dÆ°á»›i:   0.10   |   0.25   |   0.50   |   1.00
        - HÃ ng 7: Header toÃ n bá»™ 78 cá»™t (1 cá»™t MÃ£ Ä‘á» + 40 P1 + 32 P2 + 6 P3).
        - HÃ ng 8-9: 2 dÃ²ng mÃ£ Ä‘á» máº«u (0504, 0501).
        """
        wb = openpyxl.Workbook()
        ws = wb.active
        ws.title = "DAP_AN_TRAC_NGHIEM"
        ws.views.sheetView[0].showGridLines = True

        thin = Border(
            left=Side(style='thin', color='D3D3D3'),
            right=Side(style='thin', color='D3D3D3'),
            top=Side(style='thin', color='D3D3D3'),
            bottom=Side(style='thin', color='D3D3D3')
        )

        fill_navy = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="1B365D")
        fill_p1 = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="E8F0FE")    # Xanh dÆ°Æ¡ng nháº¡t
        fill_p2 = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="FEF7E0")    # VÃ ng cam nháº¡t
        fill_p3 = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="E6F4EA")    # Xanh lÃ¡ nháº¡t
        fill_header = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="F1F3F4")
        fill_val = PatternFill("solid", fgColor="FFFFFF")

        font_title = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=13, bold=True, color="1B365D")
        font_sub = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=10, italic=True, color="555555")
        font_label = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=10, bold=True)
        font_val = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=11, bold=True, color="000080")
        font_h = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=10, bold=True)
        font_h_white = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=10, bold=True, color="FFFFFF")

        # 1. TIÃŠU Äá»€ & LÆ¯U Ã
        ws["A1"] = f"Báº¢NG ÄÃP ÃN & Cáº¤U HÃŒNH THANG ÄIá»‚M CHUáº¨N Bá»˜ GD&ÄT: {subject.upper()} - {period.upper()}"
        ws["A1"].font = font_title

        ws["A2"] = "LÆ¯U Ã: Tháº§y/cÃ´ cÃ³ thá»ƒ sá»­a Ä‘iá»ƒm á»Ÿ cÃ¡c Ã´ mÃ u tráº¯ng. Äá» thi cÃ³ bao nhiÃªu cÃ¢u thÃ¬ Ä‘iá»n báº¥y nhiÃªu cÃ¢u, cÃ¢u khÃ´ng thi Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng hoÃ n toÃ n."
        ws["A2"].font = font_sub

        # 2. KHUNG Cáº¤U HÃŒNH THANG ÄIá»‚M TRá»°C QUAN (HÃ€NG 3 - 5)
        # Cá»™t A-B: ThÃ´ng tin chung
        ws["A3"] = "TÃªn bÃ i kiá»ƒm tra:"
        ws["B3"] = f"{subject} - {period}"
        ws["A4"] = "MÃ´n há»c:"
        ws["B4"] = subject
        ws["A5"] = "NÄƒm há»c:"
        ws["B5"] = "2026-2027"

        # Cá»™t C-D: Äiá»ƒm Pháº§n I & Pháº§n III
        ws["C3"] = "Äiá»ƒm 1 cÃ¢u Pháº§n I:"
        ws["D3"] = 0.25
        ws["C4"] = "Äiá»ƒm 1 cÃ¢u Pháº§n III:"
        ws["D4"] = 0.25
        ws["C5"] = "Ká»³ kiá»ƒm tra:"
        ws["D5"] = period

        # Cá»™t E-H: Báº¢NG THANG ÄIá»‚M PHáº¦N II (ÄÃšNG/SAI Báº¬C THANG)
        ws.merge_cells("E3:H3")
        ws["E3"] = "THANG ÄIá»‚M Báº¬C THANG PHáº¦N II (ÄÃšNG / SAI Bá»˜ GD&ÄT)"
        ws["E3"].font = font_label
        ws["E3"].alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
        ws["E3"].fill = fill_p2
        ws["E3"].border = thin

        # HÃ ng 4: Ã” TRÃŠN - TÃªn má»©c Ä‘Ãºng
        p2_headers = [("E4", "ÄÃºng 1 Ã½"), ("F4", "ÄÃºng 2 Ã½"), ("G4", "ÄÃºng 3 Ã½"), ("H4", "ÄÃºng 4 Ã½")]
        for cell_id, text in p2_headers:
            ws[cell_id] = text
            ws[cell_id].font = font_label
            ws[cell_id].alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
            ws[cell_id].fill = fill_header
            ws[cell_id].border = thin

        # HÃ ng 5: Ã” DÆ¯á»šI - Sá»‘ Ä‘iá»ƒm tÆ°Æ¡ng á»©ng
        p2_scores = [("E5", 0.10), ("F5", 0.25), ("G5", 0.50), ("H5", 1.00)]
        for cell_id, val in p2_scores:
            ws[cell_id] = val
            ws[cell_id].font = font_val
            ws[cell_id].alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
            ws[cell_id].fill = fill_val
            ws[cell_id].border = thin

        # Äá»‹nh dáº¡ng viá»n vÃ  mÃ u sáº¯c khá»‘i A3..D5
        for r in range(3, 6):
            for c in ["A", "C"]:
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].font = font_label
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].fill = fill_header
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].border = thin
            for c in ["B", "D"]:
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].font = font_val if c == "D" else Font(name="Times New Roman", size=10)
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].fill = fill_val
                ws[f"{c}{r}"].border = thin
                if c == "D":
                    ws[f"{c}{r}"].alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")

        # 3. HEADER Báº¢NG ÄÃP ÃN (DÃ’NG 7)
        header_row = 7
        headers = ["MÃ£ Äá» Thi"]
        col_types = ["CODE"]

        # 40 cÃ¢u Pháº§n I
        for i in range(1, cls.MAX_P1 + 1):
            headers.append(f"P1_C{i}")
            col_types.append("P1")

        # 8 cÃ¢u Pháº§n II (má»—i cÃ¢u 4 Ã½ a, b, c, d = 32 cá»™t)
        for i in range(1, cls.MAX_P2 + 1):
            for sk in ["a", "b", "c", "d"]:
                headers.append(f"P2_C{i}_{sk}")
                col_types.append("P2")

        # 6 cÃ¢u Pháº§n III
        for i in range(1, cls.MAX_P3 + 1):
            headers.append(f"P3_C{i}")
            col_types.append("P3")

        for c_idx, (h_name, c_type) in enumerate(zip(headers, col_types), start=1):
            cell = ws.cell(row=header_row, column=c_idx, value=h_name)
            cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center", wrap_text=True)
            cell.border = thin
            if c_type == "CODE":
                cell.font = font_h_white
                cell.fill = fill_navy
            elif c_type == "P1":
                cell.font = font_h
                cell.fill = fill_p1
            elif c_type == "P2":
                cell.font = font_h
                cell.fill = fill_p2
            else:
                cell.font = font_h
                cell.fill = fill_p3

        # 4. ÄIá»€N Dá»® LIá»†U MáºªU (2 MÃƒ Äá»€ 0504 & 0501)
        sample_rows = [
            ("0504", {
                "P1": ["B", "C", "A", "A", "C", "D", "C", "C", "B", "A", "C", "C", "C", "D", "A", "B"],
                "P2": [("Ä", "Ä", "Ä", "Ä"), ("Ä", "Ä", "Ä", "Ä")],
                "P3": ["214", "214", "1322"]
            }),
            ("0501", {
                "P1": ["B", "A", "B", "B", "D", "A", "D", "B", "D", "D", "A", "A", "A", "C", "D"],
                "P2": [("Ä", "S", "Ä", "S"), ("S", "S", "Ä", "Ä")],
                "P3": ["6", "2134", "10"]
            })
        ]

        for r_offset, (code, ans_dict) in enumerate(sample_rows, start=1):
            curr_r = header_row + r_offset
            row_data = [code]

            p1_vals = ans_dict.get("P1", [])
            for i in range(cls.MAX_P1):
                row_data.append(p1_vals[i] if i < len(p1_vals) else "")

            p2_tuples = ans_dict.get("P2", [])
            for q_i in range(cls.MAX_P2):
                if q_i < len(p2_tuples):
                    t_a, t_b, t_c, t_d = p2_tuples[q_i]
                    row_data.extend([t_a, t_b, t_c, t_d])
                else:
                    row_data.extend(["", "", "", ""])

            p3_vals = ans_dict.get("P3", [])
            for i in range(cls.MAX_P3):
                row_data.append(p3_vals[i] if i < len(p3_vals) else "")

            for c_idx, val in enumerate(row_data, start=1):
                cell = ws.cell(row=curr_r, column=c_idx, value=val)
                cell.font = Font(name="Times New Roman", size=11, bold=(c_idx == 1))
                cell.alignment = Alignment(horizontal="center", vertical="center")
                cell.border = thin

        ws.column_dimensions["A"].width = 14
        ws.column_dimensions["B"].width = 24
        ws.column_dimensions["C"].width = 18
        ws.column_dimensions["D"].width = 14
        ws.column_dimensions["E"].width = 12
        ws.column_dimensions["F"].width = 12
        ws.column_dimensions["G"].width = 12
        ws.column_dimensions["H"].width = 12

        for col_idx in range(9, len(headers) + 1):
            letter = get_column_letter(col_idx)
            ws.column_dimensions[letter].width = 8

        # Xuáº¥t ra bá»™ nhá»› (BytesIO) Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng hoÃ n toÃ n lá»—i khÃ³a file trÃªn Windows
        mem_buffer = io.BytesIO()
        wb.save(mem_buffer)
        mem_buffer.seek(0)
        file_bytes = mem_buffer.getvalue()

        # LÆ°u xuá»‘ng Ä‘Ä©a náº¿u cÃ³ output_path
        if output_path is not None:
            try:
                output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
                with open(output_path, "wb") as f:
                    f.write(file_bytes)
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[!] Cáº£nh bÃ¡o: KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ ghi file ra Ä‘Ä©a (cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘ang má»Ÿ trong Excel): {e}")

        return file_bytes

    @classmethod
    def parse_excel_config(cls, excel_path: Path) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Äá»c cáº¥u hÃ¬nh & barem Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n tá»« file Excel.
        Há»— trá»£ cáº¥u hÃ¬nh Pháº§n II Ã´ trÃªn (E4-H4) vÃ  Ã´ dÆ°á»›i (E5-H5).
        """
        wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(excel_path, data_only=True)
        ws = wb.active

        exam_name = str(ws["B3"].value or "BÃ i kiá»ƒm tra tráº¯c nghiá»‡m").strip()

        # 1. Äá»c Ä‘iá»ƒm Pháº§n I
        try:
            pts_p1 = float(ws["D3"].value)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pts_p1 = 0.25

        # 2. Äá»c Ä‘iá»ƒm Pháº§n III
        try:
            pts_p3 = float(ws["D4"].value)
        except (ValueError, TypeError):
            pts_p3 = 0.25

        # 3. Äá»c thang Ä‘iá»ƒm Pháº§n II (Ã” trÃªn: E4-H4, Ã” dÆ°á»›i: E5-H5)
        try:
            if ws["E5"].value is not None and ws["F5"].value is not None:
                v1 = float(ws["E5"].value)
                v2 = float(ws["F5"].value)
                v3 = float(ws["G5"].value) if ws["G5"].value is not None else 0.50
                v4 = float(ws["H5"].value) if ws["H5"].value is not None else 1.00
                p2_rule = {0: 0.0, 1: v1, 2: v2, 3: v3, 4: v4}
            else:
                raise ValueError("Use fallback")
        except Exception:
            raw_p2_rule = str(ws["D4"].value or "0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0").strip()
            try:
                p2_list = [float(x.strip()) for x in raw_p2_rule.split(",")]
                p2_rule = {0: 0.0, 1: p2_list[0], 2: p2_list[1], 3: p2_list[2], 4: p2_list[3]}
            except Exception:
                p2_rule = {0: 0.0, 1: 0.1, 2: 0.25, 3: 0.5, 4: 1.0}

        # 4. Äá»c Header á»Ÿ dÃ²ng 7
        header_row = 7
        col_map = {}
        for c in range(1, ws.max_column + 1):
            v = ws.cell(row=header_row, column=c).value
            if v:
                col_map[c] = str(v).strip()

        # 5. Äá»c ÄÃ¡p Ã¡n tá»«ng MÃ£ Ä‘á»
        answer_keys = {}
        for r in range(header_row + 1, ws.max_row + 1):
            code_v = ws.cell(row=r, column=1).value
            if not code_v:
                continue
            exam_code = str(code_v).strip().zfill(4)

            p1_dict = {}
            p2_dict = {}
            p3_dict = {}

            for c, h_name in col_map.items():
                if c == 1:
                    continue
                raw_cell = ws.cell(row=r, column=c).value
                if raw_cell is None:
                    continue
                ans_str = str(raw_cell).strip().upper()
                if not ans_str:
                    continue

                if h_name.startswith("P1_C"):
                    q_num = int(h_name.split("_C")[1])
                    p1_dict[q_num] = ans_str

                elif h_name.startswith("P2_C"):
                    parts = h_name.split("_")
                    q_num = int(parts[1][1:])
                    sub_k = parts[2].lower()
                    if q_num not in p2_dict:
                        p2_dict[q_num] = {}
                    p2_dict[q_num][sub_k] = ans_str

                elif h_name.startswith("P3_C"):
                    q_num = int(h_name.split("_C")[1])
                    try:
                        p3_dict[q_num] = float(ans_str.replace(",", "."))
                    except ValueError:
                        pass

            answer_keys[exam_code] = {
                "part1": p1_dict,
                "part2": p2_dict,
                "part3": p3_dict
            }

        return {
            "exam_name": exam_name,
            "config": {
                "pts_p1": pts_p1,
                "pts_p2_rule": p2_rule,
                "pts_p3": pts_p3
            },
            "answer_keys": answer_keys
        }

    @classmethod
    def smart_parse_text_keys(cls, text: str, default_made: str = "0001") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Bá»™ bÃ³c tÃ¡ch Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n thÃ´ng minh tá»« vÄƒn báº£n thÃ´ (Word, Zalo, SMS, Excel copy-paste).
        Há»— trá»£ dÃ¡n Ä‘á»“ng thá»i nhiá»u mÃ£ Ä‘á» hoáº·c má»™t mÃ£ Ä‘á» Ä‘Æ¡n láº».
        """
        import re
        raw = str(text or "").replace("\r", "\n")
        made_blocks = re.split(r'(?i)(?:^|\n)\s*(?:MÃƒ Äá»€|MÃƒ DE|MA DE|Äá»€|DE)\s*[:#\s]*(\d+)', raw)
        
        answer_keys = {}
        if len(made_blocks) > 1 and made_blocks[1].isdigit():
            i = 1
            while i < len(made_blocks):
                code = made_blocks[i].strip().zfill(4)
                content = made_blocks[i+1] if i+1 < len(made_blocks) else ""
                answer_keys[code] = cls._parse_single_key_block(content)
                i += 2
        else:
            lines = [l.strip() for l in raw.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
            first_line = lines[0] if lines else ""
            code = default_made.zfill(4)
            body = raw
            if re.match(r'^\d{3,4}$', first_line):
                code = first_line.zfill(4)
                body = "\n".join(lines[1:])
            answer_keys[code] = cls._parse_single_key_block(body)
            
        return {
            "exam_name": "Nháº­p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n thÃ´ng minh (Smart Paste)",
            "config": {
                "pts_p1": 0.25,
                "pts_p2_rule": {0: 0.0, 1: 0.1, 2: 0.25, 3: 0.5, 4: 1.0},
                "pts_p3": 0.25
            },
            "answer_keys": answer_keys
        }

    @classmethod
    @classmethod
    def _parse_single_key_block(cls, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        import re
        p1, p2, p3 = {}, {}, {}
        norm = text.upper().replace("Ã", "Ä")
        
        # Sáº¯p xáº¿p cÃ¡c cá»¥m tá»« dÃ i trÆ°á»›c (III rá»“i II rá»“i I) Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh khá»›p tiá»n tá»‘ nháº§m láº«n
        sec_regex = r'(?i)(?:^|\n)\s*(PHáº¦N\s*III\b|PHAN\s*3\b|TLN\b|PHáº¦N\s*II\b|PHAN\s*2\b|DS\b|ÄS\b|PHáº¦N\s*I\b|PHAN\s*1\b|TN\b)\s*[:\-\.]?'
        matches = list(re.finditer(sec_regex, norm))
        
        p1_text, p2_text, p3_text = "", "", ""
        if matches:
            for idx, m in enumerate(matches):
                label = m.group(1).upper()
                start = m.end()
                end = matches[idx+1].start() if idx+1 < len(matches) else len(norm)
                chunk = norm[start:end]
                if any(k in label for k in ["III", "3", "TLN"]):
                    p3_text += "\n" + chunk
                elif any(k in label for k in ["II", "2", "DS", "ÄS"]):
                    p2_text += "\n" + chunk
                elif any(k in label for k in ["I", "1", "TN"]):
                    p1_text += "\n" + chunk
        else:
            lines_text = [l.strip() for l in norm.split("\n") if l.strip()]
            for line in lines_text:
                if re.search(r'[ÄS]', line) and not re.search(r'[A-D]', line):
                    p2_text += "\n" + line
                elif re.search(r'\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?', line) and not re.search(r'[A-DÄS]', line):
                    p3_text += "\n" + line
                else:
                    p1_text += "\n" + line

        # 1. Pháº§n I
        p1_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,]|cÃ¢u\s*)(\d{1,2})[\.:\)\-\s]*([ABCD])\b', p1_text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p1_items:
            for q_str, ans in p1_items:
                q_num = int(q_str)
                if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P1:
                    p1[q_num] = ans.upper()
        else:
            tokens = re.findall(r'\b[ABCD]+\b', p1_text)
            flat_chars = "".join(tokens)
            for idx, char in enumerate(flat_chars[:cls.MAX_P1], start=1):
                p1[idx] = char

        # 2. Pháº§n II: Há»— trá»£ linh hoáº¡t cáº£ khá»‘i cÃ¢u 'CÃ¢u 1: a) Ä, b) S...' vÃ  dáº¡ng láº·p '1a: Ä, 1b: S...'
        q_blocks = re.split(r'(?i)(?:^|\n)\s*(?:cÃ¢u\s*)?(\d{1,2})\s*[:\.\)]', p2_text)
        if len(q_blocks) > 1:
            for i in range(1, len(q_blocks), 2):
                try:
                    q_num = int(q_blocks[i])
                    if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        block = q_blocks[i+1]
                        subs = re.findall(r'([ABCDa-d])[\.:\)\-\s]*([ÄS])', block)
                        if subs:
                            p2[q_num] = {k.lower(): v for k, v in subs}
                except (ValueError, IndexError):
                    pass

        if not p2:
            p2_sub_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,]|cÃ¢u\s*)(\d{1,2})\s*([ABCDa-d])[\.:\)\-\s]*([ÄS])\b', p2_text, re.IGNORECASE)
            if p2_sub_items:
                for q_str, sub_key, val in p2_sub_items:
                    q_num = int(q_str)
                    if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        if q_num not in p2:
                            p2[q_num] = {}
                        p2[q_num][sub_key.lower()] = val.upper()

        if not p2:
            for line in p2_text.split('\n'):
                line = line.strip()
                m = re.match(r'^(?:cÃ¢u\s*)?(\d{1,2})[\.:\)\-\s]*([ÄS\s]{4,})', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                if m:
                    q_num = int(m.group(1))
                    chars = [c for c in m.group(2) if c in "ÄS"]
                    if len(chars) == 4 and 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        p2[q_num] = {"a": chars[0], "b": chars[1], "c": chars[2], "d": chars[3]}
            if not p2:
                all_ds = [c for c in p2_text if c in "ÄS"]
                num_rows = min(len(all_ds) // 4, cls.MAX_P2)
                for r_idx in range(num_rows):
                    chunk = all_ds[r_idx*4 : (r_idx+1)*4]
                    p2[r_idx + 1] = {"a": chunk[0], "b": chunk[1], "c": chunk[2], "d": chunk[3]}

        # 3. Pháº§n III
        p3_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,]|cÃ¢u\s*)(\d{1,2})[:\)\.\s]+(-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\b', p3_text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p3_items:
            for q_str, val_str in p3_items:
                q_num = int(q_str)
                if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P3:
                    try:
                        p3[q_num] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
        else:
            num_tokens = re.findall(r'-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?', p3_text)
            for idx, val_str in enumerate(num_tokens[:cls.MAX_P3], start=1):
                try:
                    p3[idx] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
                except ValueError:
                    pass

        return {"part1": p1, "part2": p2, "part3": p3}

# full_grading_pipeline.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Há»† THá»NG CHáº¤M THI OMR TOÃ€N DIá»†N - PRODUCTION V6.5 (STRICT AUDIT & FAIL-SAFE CORE)
Chuáº©n hÃ³a Giai Ä‘oáº¡n 1:
- Khá»­ hoÃ n toÃ n cÆ¡ cháº¿ Fuzzy Matching mÃ£ Ä‘á» (chá»‘ng cháº¥m nháº§m Ä‘á»).
- Xá»­ lÃ½ tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp Pháº§n I vÃ  Pháº§n II chuáº©n quy cháº¿ thi: GÃ¡n MULTIPLE / ÄS -> 0 Ä‘iá»ƒm.
- KhÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n máº«u ngáº§m: Chá»‰ cháº¥m khi Ä‘á»£t thi Ä‘Ã£ náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n há»£p lá»‡.
- Chuáº©n hÃ³a dá»¯ liá»‡u Pháº§n III: Há»— trá»£ linh hoáº¡t cáº£ Object OMR láº«n giÃ¡ trá»‹ sá»‘ Float tá»« giao diá»‡n sá»­a tay.
- TÆ°Æ¡ng thÃ­ch 100% vá»›i VisualStamper vÃ  cÆ¡ cháº¿ giáº£i quyáº¿t Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n Runtime.
"""
import json
import math
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

import cv2
import numpy as np

from visual_stamper import VisualStamper

CANONICAL_WIDTH = 1769
CANONICAL_HEIGHT = 2526


def resolve_data_path(rel_path: str) -> Path:
    """Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tÃ¬m kiáº¿m Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n tá»‡p tÃ i nguyÃªn an toÃ n trÃªn má»i mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng."""
    p_rel = Path(rel_path)
    candidates = [
        p_rel,
        Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent / p_rel if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) else None,
        Path(getattr(sys, "_MEIPASS", "")) / p_rel if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) else None,
        Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent / "_internal" / p_rel if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) else None,
        Path(__file__).resolve().parent / p_rel,
    ]
    for cand in candidates:
        if cand is not None and cand.exists():
            return cand
    return p_rel


def normalize_answer_keys(raw_keys: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Chuáº©n hÃ³a toÃ n bá»™ sá»‘ thá»© tá»± cÃ¢u há»i vÃ  giÃ¡ trá»‹ barem Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n."""
    normalized = {}
    for code, data in (raw_keys or {}).items():
        code_str = str(code).strip().zfill(4)
        data = data or {}

        p1 = {int(q): str(ans).strip().upper() for q, ans in data.get("part1", {}).items() if str(ans).strip()}
        p2 = {
            int(q): {str(sk).strip().lower(): str(sv).strip().upper() for sk, sv in sdict.items() if str(sv).strip()}
            for q, sdict in data.get("part2", {}).items() if sdict
        }
        p3 = {}
        for q, val in data.get("part3", {}).items():
            if val is not None and str(val).strip() != "":
                try:
                    p3[int(q)] = float(str(val).replace(",", "."))
                except ValueError:
                    pass
        normalized[code_str] = {"part1": p1, "part2": p2, "part3": p3}
    return normalized


class Part3ShadowfixReader:
    def __init__(self, p3_coords: List[Dict[str, Any]], scale: float):
        self.coords = p3_coords
        self.scale = scale
        self.core_r = 3.0 * scale
        self.search_r = int(round(8.0 * scale))
        self.ring_in = 7.2 * scale
        self.ring_out = 10.8 * scale

    def _build_gradient_response(self, gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        smoothed = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (0, 0), sigmaX=max(0.8, 1.0 * self.scale), sigmaY=max(0.8, 1.0 * self.scale))
        gx = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
        gy = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
        mag = cv2.magnitude(gx, gy)
        r = int(np.ceil(self.ring_out))
        yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]
        dist = np.sqrt(xx * xx + yy * yy)
        mask = ((dist >= self.ring_in) & (dist <= self.ring_out)).astype(np.float32)
        kernel = mask / float(mask.sum()) if mask.sum() > 0 else mask
        return cv2.filter2D(mag, cv2.CV_32F, kernel, borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT)

    def _refine_and_score(self, ink_map: np.ndarray, resp: np.ndarray, x: float, y: float) -> Tuple[float, Tuple[int, int]]:
        xi, yi = int(round(x)), int(round(y))
        h, w = resp.shape
        x1, x2 = max(0, xi - self.search_r), min(w, xi + self.search_r + 1)
        y1, y2 = max(0, yi - self.search_r), min(h, yi + self.search_r + 1)
        patch = resp[y1:y2, x1:x2]
        yy, xx = np.mgrid[y1:y2, x1:x2]
        adjusted = patch - 0.4 * np.sqrt((xx - x) ** 2 + (yy - y) ** 2)
        iy, ix = np.unravel_index(np.argmax(adjusted), adjusted.shape)
        cx, cy = int(x1 + ix), int(y1 + iy)

        r = max(1, int(round(self.core_r)))
        if cx - r < 0 or cy - r < 0 or cx + r >= w or cy + r >= h:
            return 0.0, (cx, cy)
        yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]
        val = float(np.mean(ink_map[cy - r:cy + r + 1, cx - r:cx + r + 1][xx * xx + yy * yy <= r * r]))
        return val, (cx, cy)

    def read_filtered(self, gray: np.ndarray, ink_map: np.ndarray, active_q_nums: List[int]) -> Tuple[Dict[int, Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, str]]]:
        resp = self._build_gradient_response(gray)
        results = {}
        anomalies = []
        fill_thresh = 16.0
        active_set = {int(q) for q in active_q_nums}

        for q in self.coords:
            q_num = int(q["question"])
            if q_num not in active_set:
                continue

            s_val, _ = self._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, q["sign"][0], q["sign"][1])
            has_minus = s_val >= fill_thresh

            comma_opt = None
            for idx, cpos in enumerate(q["comma_positions"]):
                c_val, _ = self._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, cpos[0], cpos[1])
                if c_val >= fill_thresh:
                    comma_opt = idx + 1

            col_digits: List[List[str]] = [[] for _ in range(4)]
            for col in range(4):
                scores = []
                for d in range(10):
                    val, _ = self._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, q["digits"][str(d)][col][0], q["digits"][str(d)][col][1])
                    scores.append(val)
                ranked = sorted(enumerate(scores), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
                top_d, top_s = ranked[0]
                sec_d, sec_s = ranked[1]
                hits = [str(d) for d, s in enumerate(scores) if s >= fill_thresh]

                if len(hits) > 1:
                    col_digits[col] = hits
                elif top_s >= fill_thresh and (top_s - sec_s) >= 9.5:
                    col_digits[col] = [str(top_d)]
                else:
                    col_digits[col] = []

            q_errors = []
            col_filled = [len(digits) > 0 for digits in col_digits]

            for c_i, hits in enumerate(col_digits):
                if len(hits) > 1:
                    q_errors.append(f"Cá»™t {c_i + 1} tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp ({', '.join(hits)})")

            if any(col_filled):
                first_idx = col_filled.index(True)
                seen_empty = False
                for c_i in range(first_idx, 4):
                    if not col_filled[c_i]:
                        seen_empty = True
                    elif seen_empty:
                        q_errors.append(f"Cá»™t {c_i + 1} bá»‹ bá» cÃ¡ch quÃ£ng")
                        break

            raw_tokens = []
            if has_minus:
                raw_tokens.append("-")
            for c_i, hits in enumerate(col_digits):
                if not hits:
                    raw_tokens.append("_")
                elif len(hits) == 1:
                    raw_tokens.append(hits[0])
                else:
                    raw_tokens.append(f"[{''.join(hits)}]")
                if comma_opt == (c_i + 1):
                    raw_tokens.append(",")

            raw_str = "".join(raw_tokens)
            norm_val = None
            is_valid = (len(q_errors) == 0 and any(col_filled))

            if is_valid:
                num_str = ("-" if has_minus else "")
                for c_i in range(4):
                    if not col_digits[c_i]:
                        break
                    num_str += col_digits[c_i][0]
                    if comma_opt == (c_i + 1):
                        num_str += "."
                try:
                    norm_val = float(num_str)
                except ValueError:
                    is_valid = False
                    norm_val = None

            if q_errors:
                anomalies.append({
                    "type": f"P3_Q{q_num}_VIOLATION",
                    "msg": f"Pháº§n III CÃ¢u {q_num} pháº¡m quy cháº¿: " + "; ".join(q_errors)
                })

            results[q_num] = {
                "raw_string": raw_str,
                "normalized_value": norm_val,
                "is_valid": is_valid,
                "anomalies": q_errors,
                "digits": col_digits
            }

        return results, anomalies


class FullGradingPipeline:
    def __init__(self, exam_data: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None):
        header_coord_path = resolve_data_path("output/header_coordinates.json")
        p1_path = resolve_data_path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
        p2_path = resolve_data_path("output/part2_coordinates.json")
        p3_path = resolve_data_path("output/part3_coordinates.json")

        if not header_coord_path.exists():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"ChÆ°a tÃ¬m tháº¥y tá»‡p tá»a Ä‘á»™: {header_coord_path}")

        with open(header_coord_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            header_data = json.load(f)
            self.sbd_columns = header_data["sbd_columns"]
            self.made_columns = header_data["made_columns"]

        with open(p1_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            self.p1_coords = json.load(f)["questions"]
        with open(p2_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            self.p2_coords = json.load(f)["questions"]
        with open(p3_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            self.p3_coords = json.load(f)["questions"]

        scale = CANONICAL_WIDTH / 1434.0
        self.p3_reader = Part3ShadowfixReader(self.p3_coords, scale)
        self.stamper = VisualStamper

        # KHÃ“A Bá»˜ ÄÃP ÃN: Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng náº¡p ngáº§m Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n máº«u khi Ä‘á»£t thi chÆ°a cáº¥u hÃ¬nh
        if exam_data and exam_data.get("answer_keys"):
            self.exam_name = exam_data.get("exam_name", "Kiá»ƒm tra tráº¯c nghiá»‡m")
            self.config = exam_data.get("config", {
                "pts_p1": 0.25,
                "pts_p2_rule": {0: 0.0, 1: 0.1, 2: 0.25, 3: 0.5, 4: 1.0},
                "pts_p3": 0.25
            })
            self.answer_keys = normalize_answer_keys(exam_data.get("answer_keys", {}))
            self.is_configured = True
        else:
            self.exam_name = "ChÆ°a náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n"
            self.config = {
                "pts_p1": 0.25,
                "pts_p2_rule": {0: 0.0, 1: 0.1, 2: 0.25, 3: 0.5, 4: 1.0},
                "pts_p3": 0.25
            }
            self.answer_keys = {}
            self.is_configured = False

    @staticmethod
    def _eval_bubble_ink(ink_map: np.ndarray, cx: float, cy: float, r_core: int = 8) -> float:
        xi, yi = int(round(cx)), int(round(cy))
        h, w = ink_map.shape
        if xi - r_core < 0 or yi - r_core < 0 or xi + r_core >= w or yi + r_core >= h:
            return 0.0
        patch = ink_map[yi - r_core : yi + r_core + 1, xi - r_core : xi + r_core + 1]
        yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r_core : r_core + 1, -r_core : r_core + 1]
        mask = (xx * xx + yy * yy) <= (r_core * r_core)
        return float(np.mean(patch[mask]))

    def _read_header_ink(self, ink_map: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[str, str, str, List[Dict[str, str]]]:
        """Äá»c Sá»‘ bÃ¡o danh vÃ  MÃ£ Ä‘á» vá»›i nguyÃªn táº¯c khÃ´ng tá»± Ã½ suy Ä‘oÃ¡n."""
        anomalies = []
        FILL_THRESH = 16.0
        DOUBLE_THRESH = 15.0

        def decode_grid(cols, grid_name="SBD"):
            res = []
            for col_idx, col in enumerate(cols, start=1):
                scores = [self._eval_bubble_ink(ink_map, col["digits"][str(d)][0], col["digits"][str(d)][1], r_core=8) for d in range(10)]
                ranked = sorted(enumerate(scores), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
                top_d, top_s = ranked[0]
                sec_d, sec_s = ranked[1]

                if top_s >= FILL_THRESH and sec_s >= DOUBLE_THRESH and (top_s - sec_s) < 8.0:
                    res.append(f"[{top_d}{sec_d}]")
                    anomalies.append({
                        "type": f"{grid_name}_DOUBLE_FILL",
                        "msg": f"{grid_name} Cá»™t {col_idx} tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp ({top_d} vÃ  {sec_d})"
                    })
                elif top_s >= FILL_THRESH and (top_s - sec_s) >= 7.0:
                    res.append(str(top_d))
                elif top_s >= 25.0:
                    res.append(str(top_d))
                else:
                    res.append("?")
            return "".join(res)

        sbd = decode_grid(self.sbd_columns, "SBD")
        raw_made = decode_grid(self.made_columns, "MADE")

        if "?" in sbd or "[" in sbd:
            anomalies.append({"type": "MISSING_SBD", "msg": f"Sá»‘ bÃ¡o danh chÆ°a rÃµ rÃ ng ({sbd})"})

        # KHá»¬ HOÃ€N TOÃ€N FUZZY MATCHING: Chá»‰ cháº¥p nháº­n khi Ä‘á»c rÃµ Ä‘á»§ 4 chá»¯ sá»‘ há»£p lá»‡
        final_made = ""
        if raw_made.isdigit() and len(raw_made) == 4:
            if not self.answer_keys:
                final_made = raw_made
                anomalies.append({"type": "NO_ANSWER_KEY", "msg": "Äá»£t thi chÆ°a náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘á»‘i chiáº¿u mÃ£ Ä‘á»"})
            elif raw_made in self.answer_keys:
                final_made = raw_made
            else:
                final_made = raw_made
                anomalies.append({"type": "UNKNOWN_CODE", "msg": f"MÃ£ Ä‘á» {raw_made} khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i trong barem Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n"})
        else:
            final_made = ""
            anomalies.append({"type": "INVALID_EXAM_CODE", "msg": f"MÃ£ Ä‘á» thi chÆ°a rÃµ rÃ ng hoáº·c tÃ´ lá»—i ({raw_made})"})

        return sbd, raw_made, final_made, anomalies

    def process_sheet(self, image_bgr: np.ndarray, file_name: str, forced_exam_code: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

        # 1. Táº O INK MAP TOÃ€N Cá»¤C (High-performance Gaussian background subtraction)
        bg = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (0, 0), sigmaX=22.0, sigmaY=22.0, borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT)
        ink_map = bg.astype(np.float32) - gray.astype(np.float32)

        # 2. Äá»ŒC HEADER
        sbd, raw_made, detected_made, h_anom = self._read_header_ink(ink_map)
        
        # Náº¿u giÃ¡o viÃªn cÆ°á»¡ng cháº¿ mÃ£ Ä‘á» (sá»­a tay), sá»­ dá»¥ng forced_exam_code
        if forced_exam_code and str(forced_exam_code).strip():
            exam_code = str(forced_exam_code).strip().zfill(4)
        else:
            exam_code = detected_made

        # 3. XÃC Äá»ŠNH PHáº M VI CÃ‚U Há»ŽI (Káº¾ THá»ªA Cáº¤U TRÃšC Äá»¢T THI, KHÃ”NG BAO GIá»œ Bá»Ž SÃ“T PHáº¦N II, III)
        key_dict = self.answer_keys.get(exam_code, {}) if exam_code else {}
        global_p1 = set().union(*[{int(q) for q in k.get("part1", {}).keys()} for k in self.answer_keys.values()]) if self.answer_keys else set()
        global_p2 = set().union(*[{int(q) for q in k.get("part2", {}).keys()} for k in self.answer_keys.values()]) if self.answer_keys else set()
        global_p3 = set().union(*[{int(q) for q in k.get("part3", {}).keys()} for k in self.answer_keys.values()]) if self.answer_keys else set()

        active_p1 = {int(q) for q in key_dict.get("part1", {}).keys()} if key_dict.get("part1") else (global_p1 or set(range(1, 17)))
        active_p2 = {int(q) for q in key_dict.get("part2", {}).keys()} if key_dict.get("part2") else (global_p2 or set(range(1, 3)))
        active_p3 = {int(q) for q in key_dict.get("part3", {}).keys()} if key_dict.get("part3") else (global_p3 or set(range(1, 5)))

        # 4. QUÃ‰T PHáº¦N I (TRáº®C NGHIá»†M 4 Lá»°A CHá»ŒN)
        p1_res = {}
        p1_anom = []
        FILL_P1_THRESH = 16.0
        MARGIN_P1 = 9.0

        for q in self.p1_coords:
            q_num = int(q["question"])
            if q_num not in active_p1:
                continue
            scores = {opt: self._eval_bubble_ink(ink_map, q["choices"][opt][0], q["choices"][opt][1], r_core=8) for opt in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]}
            ranked = sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            top_opt, top_val = ranked[0]
            sec_opt, sec_val = ranked[1]

            if top_val < FILL_P1_THRESH:
                p1_res[q_num] = ""
            elif sec_val >= FILL_P1_THRESH and (top_val - sec_val) < MARGIN_P1:
                # Sá»¬A Lá»–I 1: TÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp gÃ¡n MULTIPLE -> Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng cho Ä‘iá»ƒm cÃ¢u nÃ y
                p1_res[q_num] = "MULTIPLE"
                p1_anom.append({"type": "P1_DOUBLE_FILL", "msg": f"Pháº§n I CÃ¢u {q_num} tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp ({top_opt} vÃ  {sec_opt})"})
            else:
                p1_res[q_num] = top_opt

        # 5. QUÃ‰T PHáº¦N II (ÄÃšNG / SAI Báº¬C THANG)
        p2_res = {}
        p2_anom = []
        FILL_P2_THRESH = 16.0
        MARGIN_P2 = 9.0

        for q in self.p2_coords:
            q_num = int(q["question"])
            if q_num not in active_p2:
                continue
            row_dict = {}
            for rk in ["a", "b", "c", "d"]:
                tx, ty = q["rows"][rk]["true"]
                fx, fy = q["rows"][rk]["false"]
                st = self._eval_bubble_ink(ink_map, tx, ty, r_core=8)
                sf = self._eval_bubble_ink(ink_map, fx, fy, r_core=8)

                if st < FILL_P2_THRESH and sf < FILL_P2_THRESH:
                    row_dict[rk] = ""
                elif st >= FILL_P2_THRESH and sf >= FILL_P2_THRESH:
                    # Sá»¬A Lá»–I 1: Cáº£ Ä vÃ  S cÃ¹ng cÃ³ nÃ©t chÃ¬ -> GÃ¡n ÄS -> TÃ­nh 0 Ä‘iá»ƒm Ã½ nÃ y
                    row_dict[rk] = "ÄS"
                    p2_anom.append({"type": "P2_DOUBLE_FILL", "msg": f"Pháº§n II CÃ¢u {q_num}{rk} tÃ´ cáº£ ÄÃºng vÃ  Sai"})
                elif st >= FILL_P2_THRESH and (st - sf) >= MARGIN_P2:
                    row_dict[rk] = "Ä"
                elif sf >= FILL_P2_THRESH and (sf - st) >= MARGIN_P2:
                    row_dict[rk] = "S"
                elif max(st, sf) >= FILL_P2_THRESH:
                    row_dict[rk] = "Ä" if st > sf else "S"
                    p2_anom.append({"type": "P2_UNCERTAIN", "msg": f"Pháº§n II CÃ¢u {q_num}{rk} tÃ´ má»/chÆ°a rÃµ giá»¯a Ä vÃ  S"})
                else:
                    row_dict[rk] = ""
            p2_res[q_num] = row_dict

        # 6. QUÃ‰T PHáº¦N III (TRáº¢ Lá»œI NGáº®N)
        p3_res, p3_anom = self.p3_reader.read_filtered(gray, ink_map, list(active_p3))

        all_anom = h_anom + p1_anom + p2_anom + p3_anom
        score_res = self._grade(exam_code, p1_res, p2_res, p3_res)

        # PHÃ‚N TÃCH TRáº NG THÃI NGHIá»†P Vá»¤ BÃ€I THI
        has_critical_error = any(
            a["type"] in {"INVALID_EXAM_CODE", "UNKNOWN_CODE", "NO_ANSWER_KEY", "MISSING_SBD"}
            or a["type"].endswith("DOUBLE_FILL")
            or "VIOLATION" in a["type"]
            for a in all_anom
        )

        if score_res.get("status") in {"UNKNOWN_CODE", "NO_ANSWER_KEY"}:
            status_flag = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
        elif has_critical_error:
            status_flag = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
            score_res["status"] = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
        else:
            status_flag = "GRADED"

        sub_res = {
            "file_name": file_name,
            "sbd": sbd,
            "exam_code": exam_code if exam_code else "CHÆ¯A RÃ•",
            "raw_exam_code": raw_made,
            "status": status_flag,
            "anomalies": all_anom,
            "scores": score_res,
            "details": {
                "part1": p1_res,
                "part2": p2_res,
                "part3": p3_res
            }
        }

        # 7. ÄÃ“NG TEM BÃšT Äá»Ž
        graded_bgr = VisualStamper.stamp_graded_sheet(
            image_bgr,
            sub_res,
            self.p1_coords,
            self.p2_coords,
            self.p3_coords,
            key_dict,
            sbd_columns=self.sbd_columns,
            made_columns=self.made_columns
        )
        sub_res["graded_image_bgr"] = graded_bgr

        return sub_res

    def _grade(self, code: str, p1: dict, p2: dict, p3: dict) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        if not self.answer_keys:
            return {"status": "NO_ANSWER_KEY", "p1": 0.0, "p2": 0.0, "p3": 0.0, "total": 0.0}

        code_str = str(code).strip().zfill(4) if code else ""
        if not code_str or code_str not in self.answer_keys:
            return {"status": "UNKNOWN_CODE", "p1": 0.0, "p2": 0.0, "p3": 0.0, "total": 0.0}

        key = self.answer_keys[code_str]
        pts_p1 = float(self.config.get("pts_p1", 0.25))
        raw_p2_rule = self.config.get("pts_p2_rule", {0: 0.0, 1: 0.1, 2: 0.25, 3: 0.5, 4: 1.0})
        p2_rule = {int(k): float(v) for k, v in raw_p2_rule.items()}
        pts_p3 = float(self.config.get("pts_p3", 0.25))

        p1_clean = {int(k): str(v).upper() for k, v in p1.items() if v}
        p2_clean = {int(k): {str(sk).lower(): str(sv).upper() for sk, sv in sdict.items()} for k, sdict in p2.items() if sdict}
        p3_clean = {int(k): v for k, v in p3.items() if v is not None}

        # Cháº¥m Pháº§n I (chá»‰ khá»›p cÃ¡c phÆ°Æ¡ng Ã¡n há»£p lá»‡; "MULTIPLE" tá»± Ä‘á»™ng nháº­n 0Ä‘)
        s_p1 = sum(pts_p1 for q, ans in key.get("part1", {}).items() if p1_clean.get(q) == ans and ans in ["A", "B", "C", "D"])

        # Cháº¥m Pháº§n II (chá»‰ khá»›p phÆ°Æ¡ng Ã¡n Ä hoáº·c S duy nháº¥t)
        s_p2 = 0.0
        for q, sub_map in key.get("part2", {}).items():
            correct_sub = sum(
                1 for sk, tf in sub_map.items()
                if p2_clean.get(q, {}).get(sk) == tf and p2_clean.get(q, {}).get(sk) in ["Ä", "S"]
            )
            s_p2 += p2_rule.get(correct_sub, 0.0)

        # Sá»¬A Lá»–I 8: Cháº¥m Pháº§n III há»— trá»£ cáº£ Dict OMR láº«n sá»‘ thá»±c Float sá»­a tay
        s_p3 = 0.0
        for q, exp_v in key.get("part3", {}).items():
            val_item = p3_clean.get(q)
            if val_item is None:
                continue

            stu_val = None
            if isinstance(val_item, dict):
                if val_item.get("is_valid") and val_item.get("normalized_value") is not None:
                    stu_val = val_item["normalized_value"]
            elif isinstance(val_item, (int, float)):
                stu_val = float(val_item)

            if stu_val is not None and abs(stu_val - float(exp_v)) <= 1e-4:
                s_p3 += pts_p3

        total = round(s_p1 + s_p2 + s_p3, 2)
        return {
            "status": "GRADED",
            "p1": round(s_p1, 2),
            "p2": round(s_p2, 2),
            "p3": round(s_p3, 2),
            "total": total
        }

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
DESKTOP LAUNCHER CHO Há»† THá»NG OMR 2026 (PRODUCTION DESKTOP CLIENT)
TÃ­ch há»£p Self-Healing Bootstrapper: Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng trÃ­ch xuáº¥t tá»a Ä‘á»™ phÃ´i máº«u, file Excel máº«u vÃ  template
tá»« _internal sang working directory trÆ°á»›c khi náº¡p FastAPI Pipeline.

import sys
import os

# Thiáº¿t láº­p báº£ng mÃ£ UTF-8 cho toÃ n bá»™ tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh con vÃ  Uvicorn
os.environ["PYTHONIOENCODING"] = "utf-8"
if sys.platform == "win32":
    try:
        if sys.stdout is not None and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if sys.stderr is not None and hasattr(sys.stderr, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:

from pathlib import Path
import shutil

import threading

# ==============================================================================
# 1. BOOTSTRAPPER: THIáº¾T Láº¬P THÆ¯ Má»¤C LÃ€M VIá»†C & Äá»’NG Bá»˜ Dá»® LIá»†U Tá»° Äá»˜NG
# (Báº®T BUá»˜C CHáº Y TRÆ¯á»šC KHI IMPORT APP.PY)
# ==============================================================================
def get_base_dir() -> Path:
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        return Path(sys.executable).resolve().parent
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent

BASE_DIR = get_base_dir()
os.chdir(BASE_DIR)

RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIR = BASE_DIR / "output"
RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
(RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIR / "graded_sheets").mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
(BASE_DIR / "output/web_uploads").mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
TARGET_TEMPLATES = BASE_DIR / "templates"
TARGET_TEMPLATES.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)

# Äá»“ng bá»™ tÃ i nguyÃªn tá»« gÃ³i Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i PyInstaller (_internal hoáº·c _MEIPASS)
if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
    internal_dirs = [
        Path(getattr(sys, '_MEIPASS', BASE_DIR / "_internal")),
        BASE_DIR / "_internal"
    ]
    for int_dir in internal_dirs:
        if not int_dir.exists():
            continue
        
        # Sao chÃ©p cÃ¡c file tá»a Ä‘á»™ & template máº«u
        src_output = int_dir / "output"
        if src_output.exists():
            for f in src_output.iterdir():
                if f.is_file():
                    dest_f = RUNTIME_OUTPUT_DIR / f.name
                    if not dest_f.exists() or dest_f.stat().st_size == 0:
                        try:
                            shutil.copy2(f, dest_f)
                        except Exception:
                            pass

        # Sao chÃ©p toÃ n bá»™ thÆ° má»¥c templates (bao gá»“m icons, sw.js, manifest.json, cÃ¡c file html)
        src_tpl = int_dir / "templates"
        if src_tpl.exists():
            for item in src_tpl.rglob("*"):
                rel = item.relative_to(src_tpl)
                dest_item = TARGET_TEMPLATES / rel
                if item.is_dir():
                    dest_item.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                elif not dest_item.exists() or dest_item.stat().st_size == 0:
                    dest_item.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
                    try:
                        shutil.copy2(item, dest_item)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

# ==============================================================================
# 2. KHá»žI Táº O MÃY CHá»¦ UVICORN VÃ€ GIAO DIá»†N DESKTOP WEBVIEW
# ==============================================================================
from app import app
import uvicorn
import webview

def check_port_open(host: str, port: int, timeout: float = 0.3) -> bool:
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.settimeout(timeout)
        return s.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0

def find_available_port(default_port: int = 8000) -> int:
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        if s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", default_port)) != 0:
            return default_port
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.bind(("", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]

class ServerWorker(threading.Thread):
    def __init__(self, host="0.0.0.0", port=8000):
        super().__init__(daemon=True)
        self.host = host
        self.port = port
        self.config = uvicorn.Config(
            app=app, 
            host=self.host, 
            port=self.port, 
            log_level="warning"
        )
        self.server = uvicorn.Server(self.config)

    def run(self):
        try:
            self.server.run()
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[!] Lá»—i tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh mÃ¡y chá»§: {e}")

    def stop(self):
        self.server.should_exit = True

def main():
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()
    port = find_available_port(8000)
    
    server = ServerWorker(host="0.0.0.0", port=port)
    server.start()

    # Chá» server má»Ÿ socket hoÃ n táº¥t Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng lá»—i tráº¯ng trang
    for _ in range(40):
        if check_port_open("127.0.0.1", port):
            break
        time.sleep(0.1)

    window = webview.create_window(
        title="Há»† THá»NG QUáº¢N LÃ VÃ€ CHáº¤M THI OMR 2026",
        url=f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}",
        width=1440,
        height=900,
        min_size=(1024, 700),
        text_select=True,
        confirm_close=True
    )

    def on_window_closed():
        server.stop()

    window.events.closed += on_window_closed
    webview.start(gui="edgechromium", debug=False)
    sys.exit(0)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# package_all.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Ká»ŠCH Báº¢N ÄÃ“NG GÃ“I TOÃ€N DIá»†N CHO Há»† THá»NG CHáº¤M THI OMR 2026
Thá»±c hiá»‡n tuáº§n tá»±:
1. Kiá»ƒm tra mÃ´i trÆ°á»ng & cÃ´ng cá»¥ biÃªn dá»‹ch (PyInstaller, Inno Setup).
2. Táº¡o icon app_icon.ico chuáº©n Ä‘a phÃ¢n giáº£i.
3. Cháº¡y PyInstaller biÃªn dá»‹ch ra dist/OMR_Desktop_App.
4. Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh toÃ n váº¹n cá»§a tá»‡p thá»±c thi OMR_System.exe.
5. Gá»i Inno Setup (iscc.exe) Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i thÃ nh file cÃ i Ä‘áº·t Setup_OMR_System_2026.exe.
"""
import os
import sys
import time
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

def print_step(step: str, title: str):
    print("\n" + "=" * 75)
    print(f"[{step}] {title}")
    print("=" * 75)

def find_inno_setup_compiler() -> Path:
    candidates = [
        Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Inno Setup 6\ISCC.exe"),
        Path(r"C:\Program Files\Inno Setup 6\ISCC.exe"),
        Path(r"C:\Program Files (x86)\Inno Setup 5\ISCC.exe"),
        Path(r"C:\Program Files\Inno Setup 5\ISCC.exe"),
    ]
    for p in candidates:
        if p.exists():
            return p
    # Kiá»ƒm tra trong PATH
    which_iscc = shutil.which("iscc.exe") if "shutil" in globals() else None
    if which_iscc:
        return Path(which_iscc)
    return None

def main():
    start_time = time.time()
    base_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
    os.chdir(base_dir)

    print_step("1/5", "KIá»‚M TRA CÃ”NG Cá»¤ VÃ€ MÃ”I TRÆ¯á»œNG BIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH...")
    iscc_path = find_inno_setup_compiler()
    if not iscc_path or not iscc_path.exists():
        print("[!] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y Inno Setup Compiler (ISCC.exe).")
        print("    Vui lÃ²ng cÃ i Ä‘áº·t Inno Setup 6 tá»«: https://jrsoftware.org/isdl.php")
        sys.exit(1)
    print(f"[âœ“] TÃ¬m tháº¥y Inno Setup Compiler: {iscc_path}")

    # Äáº£m báº£o thÆ° má»¥c Ä‘áº§u ra tá»“n táº¡i
    dist_installer = base_dir / "dist_installer"
    dist_installer.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    print_step("2/5", "KHá»žI Táº O BIá»‚U TÆ¯á»¢NG á»¨NG Dá»¤NG (APP_ICON.ICO)...")
    icon_file = base_dir / "app_icon.ico"
    png_icon = base_dir / "templates" / "icons" / "icon-512.png"
    if png_icon.exists():
        try:
            from PIL import Image
            img = Image.open(png_icon)
            img.save(icon_file, format="ICO", sizes=[(16,16), (32,32), (48,48), (64,64), (128,128), (256,256)])
            print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ táº¡o thÃ nh cÃ´ng {icon_file.name} ({icon_file.stat().st_size:,} bytes)")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[!] Cáº£nh bÃ¡o: KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº¡o icon: {e}")

    print_step("3/5", "BIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH á»¨NG Dá»¤NG Báº°NG PYINSTALLER (BUILD_SPEC.PY)...")
    build_py = base_dir / "build_spec.py"
    res = subprocess.run([sys.executable, str(build_py)])
    if res.returncode != 0:
        print(f"\n[!] Tháº¥t báº¡i táº¡i bÆ°á»›c PyInstaller (Exit Code: {res.returncode})")
        sys.exit(res.returncode)

    print_step("4/5", "KIá»‚M TRA TÃNH TOÃ€N Váº¸N Cá»¦A Báº¢N BIÃŠN Dá»ŠCH...")
    target_exe = base_dir / "dist" / "OMR_Desktop_App" / "OMR_System.exe"
    if not target_exe.exists():
        print(f"[!] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tá»‡p thá»±c thi: {target_exe}")
        sys.exit(1)
    exe_size_mb = target_exe.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)
    print(f"[âœ“] Tá»‡p thá»±c thi: {target_exe.name} ({exe_size_mb:.2f} MB)")

    print_step("5/5", "ÄÃ“NG GÃ“I Bá»˜ CÃ€I Äáº¶T SETUP Báº°NG INNO SETUP...")
    iss_file = base_dir / "installer_config.iss"
    if not iss_file.exists():
        print(f"[!] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y ká»‹ch báº£n Inno Setup: {iss_file}")
        sys.exit(1)

    cmd_inno = [str(iscc_path), str(iss_file)]
    res_inno = subprocess.run(cmd_inno)
    if res_inno.returncode != 0:
        print(f"\n[!] Tháº¥t báº¡i khi Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i Inno Setup (Exit Code: {res_inno.returncode})")
        sys.exit(res_inno.returncode)

    installer_exe = dist_installer / "Setup_OMR_System_2026.exe"
    if not installer_exe.exists():
        print(f"[!] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bá»™ cÃ i Ä‘áº·t hoÃ n táº¥t táº¡i: {installer_exe}")
        sys.exit(1)

    installer_size_mb = installer_exe.stat().st_size / (1024 * 1024)
    elapsed = time.time() - start_time

    print("\n" + "=" * 75)
    print("ðŸŽ‰ ÄÃ“NG GÃ“I THÃ€NH CÃ”NG Rá»°C Rá»  Bá»˜ CÃ€I Äáº¶T WINDOWS (.EXE)!")
    print("=" * 75)
    print(f"[*] TÃªn tá»‡p cÃ i Ä‘áº·t : {installer_exe.name}")
    print(f"[*] ÄÆ°á»ng dáº«n Ä‘áº§y Ä‘á»§: {installer_exe.resolve()}")
    print(f"[*] Dung lÆ°á»£ng      : {installer_size_mb:.2f} MB")
    print(f"[*] Thá»i gian xá»­ lÃ½ : {elapsed:.1f} giÃ¢y")
    print("=" * 75)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()



=== FILE: pack_project.py ===
# pack_project.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
CÃ”NG Cá»¤ ÄÃ“NG GÃ“I MÃƒ NGUá»’N Dá»° ÃN OMR THÃ€NH 1 FILE TXT
Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng lá»c bá»: .venv, __pycache__, áº£nh (PNG, JPG), tÃ i liá»‡u (PDF, XLSX).
Chá»‰ gom: Code Python (.py), Giao diá»‡n (.html, .css, .js), Cáº¥u hÃ¬nh tá»a Ä‘á»™ (.json), File cháº¡y (.bat).
"""
from datetime import datetime
import os
from pathlib import Path

# ThÆ° má»¥c vÃ  file cáº§n bá» qua
EXCLUDE_DIRS = {
    ".venv",
    "venv",
    "env",
    ".git",
    "__pycache__",
    ".vscode",
    ".idea",
    "web_uploads",
    "graded_sheets",
    "dist",
    "build",
}

EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS = {
    ".png",
    ".jpg",
    ".jpeg",
    ".pdf",
    ".xlsx",
    ".xls",
    ".pyc",
    ".zip",
    ".tar",
    ".gz",
}

# CÃ¡c Ä‘uÃ´i file cáº§n gom ná»™i dung
INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS = {".py", ".html", ".js", ".css", ".bat", ".json", ".md"}

OUTPUT_FILE = "project_context.txt"


def generate_tree(root_dir: Path, prefix: str = "") -> list:
    """Táº¡o sÆ¡ Ä‘á»“ cÃ¢y thÆ° má»¥c trá»±c quan"""
    lines = []
    items = sorted(
        list(root_dir.iterdir()), key=lambda x: (x.is_file(), x.name.lower())
    )
    items = [
        item
        for item in items
        if item.name not in EXCLUDE_DIRS
        and item.name != OUTPUT_FILE
        and item.suffix.lower() not in EXCLUDE_EXTENSIONS
    ]

    for index, item in enumerate(items):
        is_last = index == len(items) - 1
        connector = "â””â”€â”€ " if is_last else "â”œâ”€â”€ "
        lines.append(f"{prefix}{connector}{item.name}")

        if item.is_dir():
            sub_prefix = prefix + ("    " if is_last else "â”‚   ")
            lines.extend(generate_tree(item, sub_prefix))
    return lines


def pack_project():
    root_path = Path(".")
    packed_files = []

    # QuÃ©t toÃ n bá»™ file thá»a Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_path):
        # Lá»c bá» thÆ° má»¥c khÃ´ng cáº§n thiáº¿t
        dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in EXCLUDE_DIRS]

        for file in sorted(files):
            if file == OUTPUT_FILE:
                continue
            f_path = Path(root) / file
            if f_path.suffix.lower() in INCLUDE_EXTENSIONS:
                packed_files.append(f_path)

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M:%S")

    with open(OUTPUT_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n")
        out.write("BÃO CÃO TOÃ€N DIá»†N MÃƒ NGUá»’N Dá»° ÃN OMR (Bá»˜ GD&ÄT 2025)\n")
        out.write(f"Thá»i gian táº¡o: {timestamp}\n")
        out.write(f"Tá»•ng sá»‘ file mÃ£ nguá»“n & cáº¥u hÃ¬nh: {len(packed_files)}\n")
        out.write("=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        # 1. Ghi cÃ¢y thÆ° má»¥c
        out.write("SÆ  Äá»’ Cáº¤U TRÃšC THÆ¯ Má»¤C:\n")
        out.write("OMR_EXAM_SYSTEM/\n")
        tree_lines = generate_tree(root_path)
        out.write("\n".join(tree_lines))
        out.write("\n\n" + "=" * 80 + "\n\n")

        # 2. Ghi chi tiáº¿t ná»™i dung tá»«ng file
        for idx, f_path in enumerate(packed_files, start=1):
            rel_path = f_path.relative_to(root_path).as_posix()
            banner = f"[{idx}/{len(packed_files)}] Táº¬P TIN: {rel_path}"
            out.write("-" * 80 + "\n")
            out.write(banner + "\n")
            out.write("-" * 80 + "\n")

            try:
                content = f_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
                out.write(content + "\n\n")
            except Exception as e:
                out.write(f"[!] KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c tá»‡p nÃ y: {e}\n\n")

    file_size_kb = Path(OUTPUT_FILE).stat().st_size / 1024
    print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i thÃ nh cÃ´ng: {OUTPUT_FILE}")
    print(f"[*] Tá»•ng sá»‘ file: {len(packed_files)}")
    print(f"[*] Dung lÆ°á»£ng file xuáº¥t: {file_size_kb:.2f} KB")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()


=== FILE: quality_gate.py ===
# quality_gate.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
MODULE TIá»€N KIá»‚M Äá»ŠNH CHáº¤T LÆ¯á»¢NG áº¢NH Äáº¦U VÃ€O (INPUT QUALITY GATE)
Kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»™ nÃ©t vÃ  Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng trÆ°á»›c khi Ä‘Æ°a vÃ o pipeline nháº­n diá»‡n OMR.
"""
from typing import Dict, Any, Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np


class ImageQualityAssessment:
    # NgÆ°á»¡ng Laplacian Variance tá»‘i thiá»ƒu Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng bá»‹ má» nhÃ²e (Motion Blur)
    BLUR_THRESHOLD: float = 60.0
    # Äá»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n tá»‘i thiá»ƒu (Ä‘á»™ lá»‡ch chuáº©n Ä‘á»™ xÃ¡m toÃ n trang)
    CONTRAST_MIN_STD: float = 18.0
    # Äá»™ sÃ¡ng trung bÃ¬nh tá»‘i thiá»ƒu vÃ  tá»‘i Ä‘a [40, 248]
    MIN_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS: float = 40.0
    MAX_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS: float = 248.0
    # Tá»· lá»‡ pixel quÃ¡ tá»‘i (underexposed) hoáº·c quÃ¡ sÃ¡ng (burned out) tá»‘i Ä‘a (cho phÃ©p bÃ n tá»‘i mÃ u 65%)
    MAX_EXTREME_PIXELS_RATIO: float = 0.65

    @classmethod
    def evaluate(cls, image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[bool, Dict[str, Any]]:
        """
        ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ cháº¥t lÆ°á»£ng áº£nh chá»¥p bÃ i thi.
        Tráº£ vá»: (is_valid, metrics_dict)
        """
        if image_bgr is None or image_bgr.size == 0:
            return False, {"error": "áº¢nh rá»—ng hoáº·c khÃ´ng thá»ƒ giáº£i mÃ£."}

        h, w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

        # 1. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ Ä‘á»™ nÃ©t (Sharpness / Blur Detection) qua toÃ¡n tá»­ Laplacian
        laplacian_var = float(cv2.Laplacian(gray, cv2.CV_64F).var())
        is_sharp = laplacian_var >= cls.BLUR_THRESHOLD

        # 2. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ dáº£i tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n (Dynamic Range / Contrast)
        std_dev = float(np.std(gray))
        mean_val = float(np.mean(gray))
        is_contrast_ok = std_dev >= cls.CONTRAST_MIN_STD

        # 3. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ Ä‘á»™ sÃ¡ng trung bÃ¬nh (Mean Brightness)
        is_brightness_ok = cls.MIN_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS <= mean_val <= cls.MAX_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS

        # 4. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ tá»· lá»‡ bÃ³ng Ä‘en gáº¯t vÃ  chÃ¡y sÃ¡ng gáº¯t
        dark_pixels = np.sum(gray < 35)
        bright_pixels = np.sum(gray > 252)
        extreme_ratio = float((dark_pixels + bright_pixels) / (h * w))
        is_lighting_acceptable = extreme_ratio <= cls.MAX_EXTREME_PIXELS_RATIO

        # Tá»•ng há»£p káº¿t quáº£
        reasons = []
        if not is_sharp:
            reasons.append(f"áº¢nh bá»‹ má» nhÃ²e/rung tay (Äá»™ nÃ©t Laplacian: {laplacian_var:.1f} < {cls.BLUR_THRESHOLD})")
        if not is_contrast_ok:
            reasons.append(f"Äá»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n quÃ¡ yáº¿u, áº£nh bá»‹ xá»‰n hoáº·c má» nháº¡t (Std: {std_dev:.1f} < {cls.CONTRAST_MIN_STD})")
        if not is_brightness_ok:
            if mean_val < cls.MIN_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS:
                reasons.append(f"áº¢nh quÃ¡ tá»‘i/thiáº¿u sÃ¡ng nghiÃªm trá»ng (Äá»™ sÃ¡ng TB: {mean_val:.1f} < {cls.MIN_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS})")
            else:
                reasons.append(f"áº¢nh bá»‹ chÃ³i lÃ³a/chÃ¡y sÃ¡ng toÃ n trang (Äá»™ sÃ¡ng TB: {mean_val:.1f} > {cls.MAX_MEAN_BRIGHTNESS})")
        if not is_lighting_acceptable:
            reasons.append(f"VÃ¹ng bÃ³ng Ä‘en hoáº·c chÃ¡y sÃ¡ng quÃ¡ lá»›n ({extreme_ratio * 100:.1f}% diá»‡n tÃ­ch)")

        is_passed = len(reasons) == 0

        return is_passed, {
            "is_passed": is_passed,
            "reasons": reasons,
            "metrics": {
                "sharpness_laplacian": round(laplacian_var, 1),
                "contrast_std": round(std_dev, 1),
                "mean_brightness": round(mean_val, 1),
                "extreme_lighting_ratio": round(extreme_ratio, 3),
                "resolution": f"{w}x{h}"
            }
        }



=== FILE: restore_coordinates.py ===

# 2. Tá»a Ä‘á»™ Pháº§n I (40 cÃ¢u tráº¯c nghiá»‡m)
p1_questions = []
col_x_bases = [
    {"A": 191.0, "B": 264.0, "C": 334.0, "D": 402.0, "y0": 925.5},

y_step_p1 = 42.722
for block_idx, base in enumerate(col_x_bases):
    for r in range(10):

for q_idx, (tx, fx, y0) in enumerate(p2_x_bases, start=1):

    for r_idx, rk in enumerate(["a", "b", "c", "d"]):

        rows_dict[rk] = {

        }
    p2_questions.append({"question": q_idx, "rows": rows_dict})

    print(f"[âœ“] ÄÃ£ khÃ´i phá»¥c thÃ nh cÃ´ng: {file_path}")

# run_desktop.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import multiprocessing
import os
from pathlib import Path
import socket
import sys
import threading
import time
import traceback


def get_base_dir() -> Path:
    """XÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘Ãºng thÆ° má»¥c gá»‘c dÃ¹ cháº¡y mÃ£ nguá»“n trá»±c tiáº¿p hay cháº¡y qua file .exe Ä‘Ã³ng gÃ³i"""
    if getattr(sys, "frozen", False) and hasattr(sys, "_MEIPASS"):
        return Path(sys._MEIPASS)
    return Path(__file__).resolve().parent


# Äáº·t láº¡i thÆ° má»¥c lÃ m viá»‡c vá» Ä‘Ãºng thÆ° má»¥c dá»± Ã¡n
os.chdir(get_base_dir())

try:
    from app import app
    import uvicorn
    import webview
except Exception as e:
    print("\n[!] Lá»–I IMPORT THÆ¯ VIá»†N:")
    traceback.print_exc()
    input("\nNháº¥n Enter Ä‘á»ƒ thoÃ¡t...")
    sys.exit(1)


def find_available_port(default_port: int = 8000) -> int:
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        # Kiá»ƒm tra trÃªn 0.0.0.0
        if s.connect_ex(("127.0.0.1", default_port)) != 0:
            return default_port
    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
        s.bind(("", 0))
        return s.getsockname()[1]


def wait_for_server(host: str, port: int, timeout: float = 8.0) -> bool:
    start_time = time.time()
    while time.time() - start_time < timeout:
        with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
            s.settimeout(0.2)
            if s.connect_ex((host, port)) == 0:
                return True
        time.sleep(0.1)
    return False


class UvicornServerThread(threading.Thread):

    def __init__(self, host: str, port: int):
        super().__init__(daemon=True)
        self.host = host
        self.port = port
        self.config = uvicorn.Config(
            app=app, host=self.host, port=self.port, log_level="warning"
        )
        self.server = uvicorn.Server(self.config)

    def run(self):
        try:
            self.server.run()
        except Exception as ex:
            print(f"[!] Lá»—i mÃ¡y chá»§ Uvicorn: {ex}")

    def stop(self):
        self.server.should_exit = True


def start_app():
    # 0.0.0.0 cho phÃ©p cáº£ mÃ¡y tÃ­nh láº«n Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i trong máº¡ng LAN káº¿t ná»‘i
    server_host = "0.0.0.0"
    port = find_available_port(8000)
    
    # URL hiá»ƒn thá»‹ trÃªn Desktop ná»™i bá»™
    desktop_url = f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"

    server_thread = UvicornServerThread(server_host, port)
    server_thread.start()

    if not wait_for_server("127.0.0.1", port, timeout=8.0):
        print(f"[!] Lá»—i: MÃ¡y chá»§ khÃ´ng thá»ƒ láº¯ng nghe táº¡i cá»•ng {port}")
        input("\nNháº¥n Enter Ä‘á»ƒ thoÃ¡t...")
        sys.exit(1)

    print(f"[*] MÃ¡y chá»§ OMR Ä‘ang má»Ÿ rá»™ng trÃªn toÃ n máº¡ng:")
    print(f"    - Truy cáº­p trÃªn mÃ¡y tÃ­nh : http://127.0.0.1:{port}")
    print(f"    - Truy cáº­p trÃªn Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i: http://192.168.1.48:{port}/scanner")

    window = webview.create_window(
        title="Há»† THá»NG CHáº¤M PHIáº¾U TRáº®C NGHIá»†M - Bá»˜ GD&ÄT 2025",
        url=desktop_url,
        width=1440,
        height=900,
        min_size=(1024, 700),
        resizable=True,
        confirm_close=True,
    )

    def on_closed():
        server_thread.stop()

    window.events.closed += on_closed
    webview.start(gui="edgechromium", debug=False)
    sys.exit(0)

    def on_closed():
        server_thread.stop()

    window.events.closed += on_closed
    webview.start(gui="edgechromium", debug=False)
    sys.exit(0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    multiprocessing.freeze_support()
    start_app()
