# visual_stamper.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
MODULE Váº¼ OVERLAY CHáº¤M BÃšT Äá»Ž TRá»°C TIáº¾P LÃŠN PHIáº¾U BÃ€I LÃ€M (PRODUCTION V6.9)
Quy chuáº©n mÃ u sáº¯c trá»±c quan:
- XANH LÃ (COLOR_GREEN)       : CÃ¢u/Ã½ há»c sinh TÃ” ÄÃšNG.
- Äá»Ž (COLOR_RED)              : Ã” há»c sinh TÃ” SAI hoáº·c TÃ” ÄÃšP vi pháº¡m.
- VÃ€NG Há»” PHÃCH (COLOR_YELLOW): ÄÃ¡p Ã¡n Ä‘Ãºng cá»§a cÃ¡c cÃ¢u Bá»Ž TRá»NG (KHÃ”NG TÃ”).
"""
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import cv2
import re
import numpy as np


class VisualStamper:
    COLOR_GREEN = (0, 180, 0)     # Xanh lÃ¡ (Há»c sinh lÃ m ÄÃšNG)
    COLOR_RED = (0, 0, 220)       # Äá» (Há»c sinh lÃ m SAI / TÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp)
    COLOR_YELLOW = (0, 190, 245)  # VÃ ng há»• phÃ¡ch (Bá»Ž TRá»NG)

    @staticmethod
    def _draw_centered_text(img: np.ndarray, text: str, cx: int, cy: int, font: int, scale: float, color: tuple, thickness: int):
        (tw, th), _ = cv2.getTextSize(text, font, scale, thickness)
        tx = int(round(cx - tw / 2.0))
        ty = int(round(cy + th / 2.0))
        cv2.putText(img, text, (tx, ty), font, scale, color, thickness, cv2.LINE_AA)

    @classmethod
    def _highlight_bubble(cls, canvas: np.ndarray, center: Tuple[int, int], color: tuple, radius: int = 13, alpha: float = 0.35):
        cx, cy = center
        h, w = canvas.shape[:2]
        if cx - radius < 0 or cy - radius < 0 or cx + radius >= w or cy + radius >= h:
            return

        overlay = canvas.copy()
        cv2.circle(overlay, (cx, cy), radius, color, -1, cv2.LINE_AA)
        cv2.addWeighted(overlay, alpha, canvas, 1.0 - alpha, 0, canvas)
        cv2.circle(canvas, (cx, cy), radius, color, 2, cv2.LINE_AA)

    @classmethod
    def stamp_graded_sheet(
        cls,
        aligned_bgr: np.ndarray,
        submission: Dict[str, Any],
        p1_coords: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        p2_coords: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        p3_coords: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        key_dict: Dict[str, Any],
        sbd_columns: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        made_columns: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        canvas = aligned_bgr.copy()
        sc = submission.get("scores", {"total": 0.0, "p1": 0.0, "p2": 0.0, "p3": 0.0})
        details = submission.get("details", {})
        exam_code = str(submission.get("exam_code", "")).strip().zfill(4)
        raw_exam_code = str(submission.get("raw_exam_code", exam_code)).strip()
        sbd_str = str(submission.get("sbd", "????????")).strip()

        RADIUS = 13

        p1_stu = {int(k): str(v).upper() for k, v in details.get("part1", {}).items() if v}
        p2_stu = {int(k): {str(sk).lower(): str(sv).upper() for sk, sv in sdict.items()} for k, sdict in details.get("part2", {}).items() if sdict}
        p3_stu = {int(k): v for k, v in details.get("part3", {}).items() if v is not None}

        p1_keys = {int(k): str(v).upper() for k, v in key_dict.get("part1", {}).items() if str(v).strip()}
        p2_keys = {int(k): {str(sk).lower(): str(sv).upper() for sk, sv in sdict.items()} for k, sdict in key_dict.get("part2", {}).items() if sdict}
        p3_keys = {}
        for k, v in key_dict.get("part3", {}).items():
            if v is not None and str(v).strip() != "":
                try:
                    p3_keys[int(k)] = float(v)
                except ValueError:
                    pass

        # 1. ÄÃ“NG KHUNG ÄIá»‚M
        box_tl, box_br = (65, 185), (410, 440)
        cv2.rectangle(canvas, box_tl, box_br, (255, 255, 255), -1)
        cv2.rectangle(canvas, box_tl, box_br, cls.COLOR_RED, 3)

        cv2.putText(canvas, f"DIEM: {sc.get('total', 0.0):.2f} / 10", (85, 245), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_DUPLEX, 1.15, cls.COLOR_RED, 3, cv2.LINE_AA)
        cv2.putText(canvas, f"P.I   : {sc.get('p1', 0.0):.2f} d", (85, 295), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.75, (0, 0, 180), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
        cv2.putText(canvas, f"P.II  : {sc.get('p2', 0.0):.2f} d", (85, 345), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.75, (0, 0, 180), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
        cv2.putText(canvas, f"P.III : {sc.get('p3', 0.0):.2f} d", (85, 395), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.75, (0, 0, 180), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)
        cv2.putText(canvas, f"Ma de: {exam_code}", (260, 295), cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, 0.75, (0, 100, 0), 2, cv2.LINE_AA)

        # 2. CHáº¤M Sá» BÃO DANH
        if sbd_columns:
            for col_idx, col_data in enumerate(sbd_columns):
                char_digit = sbd_str[col_idx] if col_idx < len(sbd_str) else "?"
                if char_digit == "?" or not char_digit.isdigit():
                    pos_0 = col_data["digits"]["0"]
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_0[0])), int(round(pos_0[1]))), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                else:
                    if char_digit in col_data["digits"]:
                        pos_d = col_data["digits"][char_digit]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_d[0])), int(round(pos_d[1]))), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

        # 3. CHáº¤M MÃƒ Äá»€
        if made_columns:
            is_all_correct = (raw_exam_code == exam_code and "?" not in raw_exam_code and len(raw_exam_code) == 4)
            for col_idx, col_data in enumerate(made_columns):
                raw_char = raw_exam_code[col_idx] if col_idx < len(raw_exam_code) else "?"
                target_char = exam_code[col_idx] if col_idx < len(exam_code) else "?"

                if is_all_correct:
                    if target_char in col_data["digits"]:
                        pos = col_data["digits"][target_char]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos[0])), int(round(pos[1]))), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                else:
                    if raw_char.isdigit() and raw_char in col_data["digits"]:
                        if raw_char != target_char:
                            pos_wrong = col_data["digits"][raw_char]
                            cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_wrong[0])), int(round(pos_wrong[1]))), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                        else:
                            pos_right = col_data["digits"][raw_char]
                            cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_right[0])), int(round(pos_right[1]))), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                    elif raw_char == "?":
                        pos_0 = col_data["digits"]["0"]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_0[0])), int(round(pos_0[1]))), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)

                    if target_char.isdigit() and target_char in col_data["digits"] and target_char != raw_char:
                        pos_target = col_data["digits"][target_char]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(round(pos_target[0])), int(round(pos_target[1]))), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

        # 4. CHáº¤M PHáº¦N I (TRáº®C NGHIá»†M): ÄÃšNG = XANH | SAI = Äá»Ž | KHÃ”NG TÃ” = VÃ€NG
        for q_item in p1_coords:
            q_num = int(q_item["question"])
            if q_num not in p1_keys:
                continue

            correct_ans = p1_keys[q_num]
            stu_ans = p1_stu.get(q_num, "").strip()
            cx, cy = map(int, map(round, q_item["choices"][correct_ans]))

            if not stu_ans or stu_ans == "":
                cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (cx, cy), cls.COLOR_YELLOW, RADIUS)
            elif stu_ans == correct_ans:
                cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (cx, cy), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
            elif stu_ans == "MULTIPLE":
                # TÃ” ÄÃšP: Há»c sinh tÃ´ 2 Ã´ trá»Ÿ lÃªn -> Bá»‹ pháº¡t 0 ÄIá»‚M cÃ¢u nÃ y!
                # Khoanh Äá»Ž vÃ o cÃ¡c Ã´ há»c sinh Ä‘Ã£ tÃ´ vi pháº¡m
                double_opts = []
                for a in submission.get("anomalies", []):
                    if a.get("type") == "P1_DOUBLE_FILL" and f"CÃ¢u {q_num} " in a.get("msg", ""):
                        m = re.findall(r'\b([A-D])\b', a.get("msg", ""))
                        if m:
                            double_opts = m
                        break
                if not double_opts:
                    double_opts = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]

                for opt in double_opts:
                    if opt in q_item["choices"]:
                        ox, oy = map(int, map(round, q_item["choices"][opt]))
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (ox, oy), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                # ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n chuáº©n cá»§a barem
                cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (cx, cy), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS - 4 if correct_ans in double_opts else RADIUS)
            else:
                if stu_ans in q_item["choices"]:
                    wx, wy = map(int, map(round, q_item["choices"][stu_ans]))
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (wx, wy), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (cx, cy), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

        # 5. CHáº¤M PHáº¦N II (ÄÃšNG/SAI): ÄÃšNG = XANH | SAI = Äá»Ž | KHÃ”NG TÃ” = VÃ€NG
        for q_item in p2_coords:
            q_num = int(q_item["question"])
            if q_num not in p2_keys:
                continue

            sub_map = p2_keys[q_num]
            stu_sub_map = p2_stu.get(q_num, {})

            for rk in ["a", "b", "c", "d"]:
                if rk not in sub_map:
                    continue
                exp_tf = sub_map[rk]
                stu_tf = stu_sub_map.get(rk, "").strip()

                tx, ty = map(int, map(round, q_item["rows"][rk]["true"]))
                fx, fy = map(int, map(round, q_item["rows"][rk]["false"]))

                correct_pos = (tx, ty) if exp_tf == "Ä" else (fx, fy)
                wrong_pos = (fx, fy) if exp_tf == "Ä" else (tx, ty)

                if not stu_tf or stu_tf == "":
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, correct_pos, cls.COLOR_YELLOW, RADIUS)
                elif stu_tf == exp_tf:
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, correct_pos, cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                else:
                    if stu_tf in ["Ä", "S"]:
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, wrong_pos, cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                    elif stu_tf == "ÄS":
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (tx, ty), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (fx, fy), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, correct_pos, cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

        # 6. CHáº¤M PHáº¦N III (TRáº¢ Lá»œI NGáº®N): ÄÃšNG = XANH | SAI = Äá»Ž | KHÃ”NG TÃ” = VÃ€NG
        for q_item in p3_coords:
            q_num = int(q_item["question"])
            if q_num not in p3_keys:
                continue

            exp_v = p3_keys[q_num]
            stu_item = p3_stu.get(q_num)

            is_valid = False
            stu_v = None
            raw_str = ""
            stu_digits_list = []

            if isinstance(stu_item, dict):
                is_valid = stu_item.get("is_valid", False)
                stu_v = stu_item.get("normalized_value")
                raw_str = stu_item.get("raw_string", "")
                stu_digits_list = stu_item.get("digits", [[], [], [], []])
            elif isinstance(stu_item, (int, float)):
                stu_v = float(stu_item)
                is_valid = True
                raw_str = str(stu_item)

            is_correct = (is_valid and stu_v is not None and abs(stu_v - exp_v) <= 1e-4)
            is_blank = (not raw_str or raw_str.replace("_", "").strip() == "") and not is_correct

            col_xs = [q_item["digits"]["0"][c][0] for c in range(4)]
            box_cx = int(round(np.mean(col_xs)))
            score_y = int(round(q_item["sign"][1])) - 40

            if abs(exp_v - round(exp_v)) < 1e-6:
                exp_str = str(int(round(exp_v)))
            else:
                exp_str = f"{exp_v:.4f}".rstrip('0').rstrip('.')

            has_exp_minus = exp_v < 0
            val_part = exp_str.lstrip("-")
            comma_idx = val_part.find(".") if "." in val_part else -1
            clean_digits = val_part.replace(".", "")

            if is_correct:
                if has_exp_minus:
                    sx, sy = map(int, map(round, q_item["sign"]))
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (sx, sy), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                if comma_idx in [1, 2]:
                    cpos = q_item["comma_positions"][comma_idx - 1]
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(cpos[0]), int(cpos[1])), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                for c_i in range(min(4, len(clean_digits))):
                    d_char = clean_digits[c_i]
                    if d_char in q_item["digits"]:
                        pos = q_item["digits"][d_char][c_i]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(pos[0]), int(pos[1])), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

            elif is_blank:
                if has_exp_minus:
                    sx, sy = map(int, map(round, q_item["sign"]))
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (sx, sy), cls.COLOR_YELLOW, RADIUS)
                if comma_idx in [1, 2]:
                    cpos = q_item["comma_positions"][comma_idx - 1]
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(cpos[0]), int(cpos[1])), cls.COLOR_YELLOW, RADIUS)
                for c_i in range(min(4, len(clean_digits))):
                    d_char = clean_digits[c_i]
                    if d_char in q_item["digits"]:
                        pos = q_item["digits"][d_char][c_i]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(pos[0]), int(pos[1])), cls.COLOR_YELLOW, RADIUS)

            else:
                for c_i, hits in enumerate(stu_digits_list):
                    for d_char in hits:
                        if d_char in q_item["digits"]:
                            pos = q_item["digits"][d_char][c_i]
                            cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(pos[0]), int(pos[1])), cls.COLOR_RED, RADIUS)
                if has_exp_minus:
                    sx, sy = map(int, map(round, q_item["sign"]))
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (sx, sy), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                if comma_idx in [1, 2]:
                    cpos = q_item["comma_positions"][comma_idx - 1]
                    cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(cpos[0]), int(cpos[1])), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)
                for c_i in range(min(4, len(clean_digits))):
                    d_char = clean_digits[c_i]
                    if d_char in q_item["digits"]:
                        pos = q_item["digits"][d_char][c_i]
                        cls._highlight_bubble(canvas, (int(pos[0]), int(pos[1])), cls.COLOR_GREEN, RADIUS)

        return canvas

    @staticmethod
    def export_all_to_pdf(graded_bgr_list: List[np.ndarray], output_pdf_path: Path):
        """HÃ m dá»± phÃ²ng xuáº¥t PDF - Tuyá»‡t Ä‘á»‘i khÃ´ng dÃ¹ng kÃ½ tá»± Unicode láº¡ Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh charmap crash."""
        from PIL import Image

        pil_images = []
        for bgr in graded_bgr_list:
            rgb = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2RGB)
            pil_images.append(Image.fromarray(rgb))

        if pil_images:
            output_pdf_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
            pil_images[0].save(output_pdf_path, save_all=True, append_images=pil_images[1:], resolution=200.0)
            print(f"[OK] Da xuat thanh cong tep PDF: {output_pdf_path.name}")


=== FILE: archive_backup\align_sheet.py ===

import argparse
from pathlib import Path

import cv2
import numpy as np

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")


# ==========================================
# DOC ANH JPG, PNG HOAC PDF
# ==========================================

def load_image(path):

    if not path.exists():
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong tim thay file: {path}"
        )

    if path.suffix.lower() == ".pdf":

        import fitz

        with fitz.open(path) as document:

            if len(document) == 0:
                raise ValueError("PDF khong co trang.")

            page = document[0]

            pix = page.get_pixmap(
                matrix=fitz.Matrix(3, 3),
                alpha=False
            )

            rgb = np.frombuffer(
                pix.samples,
                dtype=np.uint8
            ).reshape(
                pix.height,
                pix.width,
                pix.n
            )

            return cv2.cvtColor(
                rgb,
                cv2.COLOR_RGB2BGR
            )

    image = cv2.imread(str(path))

    if image is None:
        raise ValueError("Khong doc duoc anh.")

    return image


# ==========================================
# TIM 4 O VUONG DEN TAI 4 GOC PHIEU
# ==========================================


def find_corner_markers(image):

    height, width = image.shape[:2]

    # 1. Chuyen anh sang anh xam
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        image,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    # 2. Tang do tuong phan cho anh photocopy nhat
    clahe = cv2.createCLAHE(
        clipLimit=2.5,
        tileGridSize=(8, 8)
    )

    enhanced = clahe.apply(gray)

    # 3. Nhi phan hoa thich nghi
    # Khong phu thuoc vao mot nguong do den co dinh
    block_size = int(min(width, height) * 0.025)

    if block_size % 2 == 0:
        block_size += 1

    block_size = max(31, min(block_size, 151))

    binary = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(
        enhanced,
        255,
        cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C,
        cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV,
        block_size,
        7
    )

    # 4. Noi cac diem muc bi dut do photocopy
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(
        cv2.MORPH_RECT,
        (3, 3)
    )

    binary = cv2.morphologyEx(
        binary,
        cv2.MORPH_CLOSE,
        kernel
    )

    # Luu anh nhi phan de kiem tra
    cv2.imwrite(
        "output/marker_threshold_debug.png",
        binary
    )

    # 5. Tim cac vung lien thong
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(
        binary,
        cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
        cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE
    )

    candidates = []

    for contour in contours:

        area = cv2.contourArea(contour)

        x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(contour)

        if w <= 0 or h <= 0:
            continue

        ratio = w / h
        fill_ratio = area / (w * h)
        area_ratio = area / (width * height)

        # Loc cac vung gan hinh vuong
        if not (0.55 <= ratio <= 1.55):
            continue

        # Chap nhan ca o photocopy nhat
        if fill_ratio < 0.35:
            continue

        # Kich thuoc tuong doi cua o dinh vi
        if not (0.00001 <= area_ratio <= 0.002):
            continue

        if not (0.004 <= w / width <= 0.055):
            continue

        if not (0.003 <= h / height <= 0.045):
            continue

        cx = x + w / 2
        cy = y + h / 2

        candidates.append((cx, cy, w, h))

    # 6. Vi tri mong doi cua 4 goc
    expected = {
        "TL": (0.032, 0.018),
        "TR": (0.973, 0.018),
        "BR": (0.973, 0.970),
        "BL": (0.032, 0.970)
    }

    # Gioi han vung tim kiem
    zones = {
        "TL": lambda x, y:
            x < 0.20 * width and y < 0.15 * height,

        "TR": lambda x, y:
            x > 0.80 * width and y < 0.15 * height,

        "BR": lambda x, y:
            x > 0.80 * width and y > 0.85 * height,

        "BL": lambda x, y:
            x < 0.20 * width and y > 0.85 * height
    }

    result = []

    # 7. Tim diem tot nhat o tung goc
    for name in ["TL", "TR", "BR", "BL"]:

        ex, ey = expected[name]

        options = [
            p for p in candidates
            if zones[name](p[0], p[1])
        ]

        if not options:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Khong tim thay diem dinh vi {name}. "
                "Kiem tra anh marker_threshold_debug.png"
            )

        best = min(
            options,
            key=lambda p:
                (p[0] / width - ex) ** 2
                + (p[1] / height - ey) ** 2
        )

        result.append((best[0], best[1]))

    return np.asarray(result, dtype=np.float32)



# ==========================================
# CAN CHINH PHIEU
# ==========================================

def align_sheet(input_path):

    # Doc anh mau chuan
    template = load_image(TEMPLATE_PATH)

    # Doc anh bai lam
    source = load_image(input_path)

    template_h, template_w = template.shape[:2]

    # Tim 4 diem goc tren anh bai lam
    source_points = find_corner_markers(source)

    # Tim 4 diem goc tren mau chuan
    target_points = find_corner_markers(template)

    names = ["TL", "TR", "BR", "BL"]

    print("\nTOA DO 4 DIEM GOC:")

    for name, point in zip(names, source_points):

        print(
            f"{name}: "
            f"x={point[0]:.1f}, "
            f"y={point[1]:.1f}"
        )

    # Tinh ma tran bien doi phoi canh
    matrix = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(
        source_points,
        target_points
    )

    # Dua anh ve dung kich thuoc mau chuan
    aligned = cv2.warpPerspective(
        source,
        matrix,
        (template_w, template_h),
        flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC,
        borderMode=cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT,
        borderValue=(255, 255, 255)
    )

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    # Luu anh da can chinh
    output_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{input_path.stem}_aligned.png"

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(output_path),
        aligned
    )

    # Luu anh danh dau 4 diem tren anh nguon
    debug = source.copy()

    for name, point in zip(names, source_points):

        x, y = map(
            int,
            np.round(point)
        )

        cv2.circle(
            debug,
            (x, y),
            12,
            (0, 0, 255),
            3
        )

        cv2.putText(
            debug,
            name,
            (x + 12, y - 10),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.8,
            (0, 0, 255),
            2
        )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{input_path.stem}_debug.png"

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n================================")
    print("CAN CHINH PHIEU THANH CONG!")
    print("Kich thuoc:", template_w, "x", template_h)
    print("Anh da can chinh:", output_path.resolve())
    print("Anh kiem tra:", debug_path.resolve())
    print("================================")


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "input",
        nargs="?",
        default=str(TEMPLATE_PATH),
        help="Duong dan anh hoac PDF bai lam"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        align_sheet(Path(args.input))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)

# benchmark_test.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
BENCHMARK TEST: SO SÃNH PHÆ¯Æ NG PHÃP ÄO TÃN HIá»†U OMR TRÃŠN BÃ€I THI THáº¬T
1. PhÆ°Æ¡ng phÃ¡p cÅ©: Relative Darkness (Tá»· lá»‡ tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n vÃ nh - lÃµi)
2. PhÆ°Æ¡ng phÃ¡p má»›i: Ink Map (Trá»« ná»n vi phÃ¢n Gaussian Blur diá»‡n rá»™ng)
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import numpy as np


def run_benchmark():
    # 1. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tÃ¬m áº£nh bÃ i thi Ä‘Ã£ náº¯n pháº³ng trong cÃ¡c thÆ° má»¥c
    search_paths = [Path("output/web_uploads"), Path("output"), Path("samples")]

    img_path = None
    for folder in search_paths:
        if folder.exists():
            for p in folder.glob("*aligned*.png"):
                img_path = p
                break
            if not img_path:
                for p in folder.glob("*.jpg"):
                    img_path = p
                    break
        if img_path:
            break

    if not img_path or not img_path.exists():
        print("[!] KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y áº£nh bÃ i thi máº«u trong output/web_uploads hoáº·c output/.")
        print("[*] HÆ°á»›ng dáº«n: HÃ£y quÃ©t thá»­ 1 bÃ i thi trÃªn web trÆ°á»›c Ä‘á»ƒ sinh file aligned, sau Ä‘Ã³ cháº¡y láº¡i test nÃ y.")
        return

    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"[*] ÄANG CHáº Y BENCHMARK TRÃŠN áº¢NH: {img_path.name}")
    print("=" * 80)

    bgr = cv2.imread(str(img_path))
    if bgr is None:
        print(f"[!] KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ náº¡p áº£nh: {img_path}")
        return

    gray = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape

    # 2. XÃ¢y dá»±ng ma tráº­n Ink Map (Gaussian Background Subtraction)
    bg = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (0, 0), sigmaX=22, sigmaY=22)
    ink_map = bg.astype(np.float32) - gray.astype(np.float32)

    r_core, r_in, r_out = 9, 17, 22
    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r_out:r_out + 1, -r_out:r_out + 1]
    dist_sq = xx * xx + yy * yy
    core_mask = dist_sq <= (r_core ** 2)
    ring_mask = (dist_sq >= (r_in ** 2)) & (dist_sq <= (r_out ** 2))

    def eval_bubble(cx, cy):
        x, y = int(round(cx)), int(round(cy))
        if x - r_out < 0 or y - r_out < 0 or x + r_out >= w or y - r_out >= h:
            return 0.0, 0.0

        # Äo theo phÆ°Æ¡ng phÃ¡p CÅ¨ (Relative Darkness)
        patch_gray = gray[y - r_out:y + r_out + 1, x - r_out:x + r_out + 1].astype(np.float32)
        c_vals = patch_gray[core_mask]
        r_vals = patch_gray[ring_mask]
        if c_vals.size == 0 or r_vals.size == 0:
            score_old = 0.0
        else:
            ref = np.percentile(r_vals, 70)
            score_old = float((ref - np.mean(c_vals)) / ref) if ref > 35 else 0.0
        score_old = max(0.0, score_old)

        # Äo theo phÆ°Æ¡ng phÃ¡p Má»šI (Ink Map)
        core_ink = ink_map[y - r_core:y + r_core + 1, x - r_core:x + r_core + 1]
        yy_c, xx_c = np.ogrid[-r_core:r_core + 1, -r_core:r_core + 1]
        mask_c = (xx_c * xx_c + yy_c * yy_c) <= (r_core ** 2)
        score_new = float(np.mean(core_ink[mask_c]))

        return score_old, score_new

    # =========================================================================
    # A. SO SÃNH KHU Vá»°C HEADER (MÃƒ Äá»€ THI - Dá»„ Bá»Š Lá»–I CHÃŒ Má»œ)
    # =========================================================================
    header_path = Path("output/header_coordinates.json")
    if header_path.exists():
        with open(header_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            h_data = json.load(f)

        print("\n[A] Äá»I SOÃT Má»¤C 8 - MÃƒ Äá»€ THI (4 Cá»˜T):")
        print("%-12s | %-22s | %-18s | %s" % ("Vá»‹ trÃ­", "CÅ¨: Relative Darkness", "Má»šI: Ink Map", "Nháº­n Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘á»™ tin cáº­y"))
        print("-" * 80)

        for col_idx, col in enumerate(h_data["made_columns"], start=1):
            for d in range(10):
                cx, cy = col["digits"][str(d)]
                s_old, s_new = eval_bubble(cx, cy)
                if s_old >= 0.08 or s_new >= 10.0:
                    status = ">> CÃ“ TÃ” (Ráº¥t rÃµ)" if s_new >= 20.0 else ("? ChÃ¬ má» (ÄÃ£ báº¯t Ä‘Æ°á»£c)" if s_new >= 12.0 else "Ã” tráº¯ng / Nhiá»…u")
                    print("%-12s | %-22.3f | %-18.2f | %s" % (f"Cá»™t {col_idx} sá»‘ {d}", s_old, s_new, status))

    # =========================================================================
    # B. SO SÃNH PHáº¦N I (TRáº®C NGHIá»†M 5 CÃ‚U Äáº¦U TIÃŠN)
    # =========================================================================
    p1_path = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
    if p1_path.exists():
        with open(p1_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            p1_data = json.load(f)["questions"]

        print("\n[B] Äá»I SOÃT PHáº¦N I (5 CÃ‚U Äáº¦U TIÃŠN):")
        print("%-12s | %-22s | %-18s | %s" % ("CÃ¢u há»i", "CÅ¨: Relative Darkness", "Má»šI: Ink Map", "Nháº­n Ä‘á»‹nh"))
        print("-" * 80)
        for q in p1_data[:5]:
            q_num = q["question"]
            for opt in ["A", "B", "C", "D"]:
                cx, cy = q["choices"][opt]
                s_old, s_new = eval_bubble(cx, cy)
                if s_old >= 0.07 or s_new >= 10.0:
                    status = "ÄÆ¯á»¢C CHá»ŒN" if s_new >= 20.0 else "Nhiá»…u / Táº©y xÃ³a"
                    print("%-12s | %-22.3f | %-18.2f | %s" % (f"CÃ¢u {q_num} [{opt}]", s_old, s_new, status))

    print("\n" + "=" * 80)
    print("[âœ“] BENCHMARK HOÃ€N Táº¤T!")
    print("QUY Táº®C PHÃ‚N TÃCH TÃN HIá»†U Äá»ŠNH LÆ¯á»¢NG:")
    print(" - Ã” tráº¯ng (Blank):       CÅ© = 0.00 - 0.08  | Má»›i = 0.0 - 5.0")
    print(" - Váº¿t táº©y xÃ³a (Erased):   CÅ© = 0.08 - 0.12  | Má»›i = 6.0 - 12.0 (Khá»­ nháº§m thÃ nh cÃ´ng)")
    print(" - Ã” tÃ´ chÃ¬ tháº­t (Filled): CÅ© = 0.14 - 0.25  | Má»›i = 22.0 - 65.0 (TÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n gáº¥p 3 - 4 láº§n!)")
    print("=" * 80)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_benchmark()


=== FILE: archive_backup\bubble_detector.py ===
# bubble_detector.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
THUáº¬T TOÃN ÄO Äá»˜ TÃ” Ã” TRÃ’N KHÃNG BÃ“NG Äá»” & Tá»° THÃCH á»¨NG BÃšT CHÃŒ NHáº T
Há»— trá»£ Dynamic Row Contrast vÃ  tÆ°Æ¡ng thÃ­ch ngÆ°á»£c toÃ n bá»™ tham sá»‘ cÅ©/má»›i.
"""
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np


class BubbleDetector:
    def __init__(
        self,
        core_radius: int = 9,       # BÃ¡n kÃ­nh lÃµi bÃºt chÃ¬ r < 12.8 px
        ring_inner: int = 17,       # VÃ nh ná»n ngoÃ i viá»n in r > 15.3 px
        ring_outer: int = 22        # VÃ nh ngoÃ i an toÃ n
    ):
        self.r_core = core_radius
        self.r_in = ring_inner
        self.r_out = ring_outer

    def compute_relative_darkness(self, gray_img: np.ndarray, cx: float, cy: float) -> float:
        """
        TÃ­nh Ä‘á»™ Ä‘áº­m tÆ°Æ¡ng Ä‘á»‘i cá»§a lÃµi so vá»›i ná»n giáº¥y tráº¯ng bao quanh.
        """
        x_base = int(round(cx))
        y_base = int(round(cy))
        h, w = gray_img.shape

        search_r = 4
        if (x_base - search_r < 0 or y_base - search_r < 0 or
            x_base + search_r >= w or y_base + search_r >= h):
            best_x, best_y = x_base, y_base
        else:
            patch_s = gray_img[y_base - search_r : y_base + search_r + 1,
                               x_base - search_r : x_base + search_r + 1]
            min_y, min_x = np.unravel_index(np.argmin(patch_s), patch_s.shape)
            best_x = x_base - search_r + min_x
            best_y = y_base - search_r + min_y

        r = self.r_out
        if best_x - r < 0 or best_y - r < 0 or best_x + r >= w or best_y + r >= h:
            return 0.0

        patch = gray_img[best_y - r : best_y + r + 1, best_x - r : best_x + r + 1].astype(np.float32)
        yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r : r + 1, -r : r + 1]
        dist_sq = xx * xx + yy * yy

        core_mask = dist_sq <= (self.r_core ** 2)
        ring_mask = (dist_sq >= (self.r_in ** 2)) & (dist_sq <= (self.r_out ** 2))

        core_vals = patch[core_mask]
        ring_vals = patch[ring_mask]

        if core_vals.size == 0 or ring_vals.size == 0:
            return 0.0

        core_mean = float(np.mean(core_vals))
        ring_ref = float(np.percentile(ring_vals, 70))

        if ring_ref < 35.0:
            return 0.0

        relative_darkness = (ring_ref - core_mean) / ring_ref
        return max(0.0, float(relative_darkness))

    def classify_mcq_row(
        self,
        gray_img: np.ndarray,
        coords: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]],
        min_signal: float = 0.095,
        contrast_margin: float = 0.055,
        fill_thresh: Optional[float] = None,
        margin_thresh: Optional[float] = None,
        **kwargs: Any
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        PhÃ¢n loáº¡i Pháº§n I. TÆ°Æ¡ng thÃ­ch cáº£ min_signal láº«n fill_thresh.
        """
        if fill_thresh is not None:
            min_signal = fill_thresh
        if margin_thresh is not None:
            contrast_margin = margin_thresh

        scores = {}
        for opt, (x, y) in coords.items():
            scores[opt] = round(self.compute_relative_darkness(gray_img, x, y), 3)

        ranked = sorted(scores.items(), key=lambda item: item[1], reverse=True)
        top_opt, top_score = ranked[0]
        second_opt, second_score = ranked[1]

        if top_score < min_signal:
            return {"answer": "", "status": "BLANK", "scores": scores}

        if second_score >= min_signal and (top_score - second_score) < contrast_margin:
            return {"answer": "MULTIPLE", "status": "DOUBLE_FILL", "scores": scores}

        if (top_score - second_score) >= contrast_margin or (top_score >= 1.8 * max(second_score, 0.04)):
            return {"answer": top_opt, "status": "VALID", "scores": scores}

        return {"answer": "", "status": "UNCERTAIN", "scores": scores}

    def classify_true_false_row(
        self,
        gray_img: np.ndarray,
        true_coord: Tuple[float, float],
        false_coord: Tuple[float, float],
        min_signal: float = 0.145,       # NgÆ°á»¡ng tá»‘i thiá»ƒu cá»§a Ã´ tÃ´ chÃ¬ tháº­t (váº¿t chÃ¬ nháº¡t nháº¥t lÃ  0.177)
        contrast_margin: float = 0.055,  # ChÃªnh lá»‡ch tá»‘i thiá»ƒu giá»¯a 2 Ã´
        fill_thresh: Optional[float] = None,
        margin_thresh: Optional[float] = None,
        **kwargs: Any
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        PhÃ¢n loáº¡i Pháº§n II (ÄÃºng/Sai) chuáº©n xÃ¡c, chá»‘ng báº«y nhiá»…u ná»n giáº¥y xÃ¡m.
        """
        if fill_thresh is not None:
            min_signal = fill_thresh
        if margin_thresh is not None:
            contrast_margin = margin_thresh

        s_true = round(self.compute_relative_darkness(gray_img, true_coord[0], true_coord[1]), 3)
        s_false = round(self.compute_relative_darkness(gray_img, false_coord[0], false_coord[1]), 3)
        
        max_s = max(s_true, s_false)
        diff = abs(s_true - s_false)

        # 1. Náº¿u cáº£ 2 Ã´ Ä‘á»u khÃ´ng Ä‘áº¡t ngÆ°á»¡ng nÃ©t chÃ¬ (chá»‰ lÃ  ná»n giáº¥y) -> Bá»Ž TRá»NG
        if max_s < min_signal:
            return {"answer": "", "status": "BLANK", "score_true": s_true, "score_false": s_false}

        # 2. Náº¿u cÃ³ váº¿t tÃ´ nhÆ°ng cáº£ 2 Ã´ Ä‘áº­m gáº§n báº±ng nhau -> TÃ” ÄÃšP
        if diff < contrast_margin and min(s_true, s_false) >= (min_signal * 0.85):
            return {"answer": "BOTH", "status": "DOUBLE_FILL", "score_true": s_true, "score_false": s_false}

        # 3. Ã” há»£p lá»‡
        ans = "Ä" if s_true > s_false else "S"
        return {"answer": ans, "status": "VALID", "score_true": s_true, "score_false": s_false}


=== FILE: archive_backup\detect_markers.py ===

import cv2
from pathlib import Path

# Duong dan anh phieu mau
IMAGE_PATH = Path("output") / "answer_sheet_master.png"
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")


def detect_markers():
    image = cv2.imread(str(IMAGE_PATH))

    if image is None:
        print("LOI: Khong doc duoc anh phieu mau!")
        return

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    # Nhi phan hoa
    _, binary = cv2.threshold(
        gray, 70, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV
    )

    # Tim cac vung den
    contours, _ = cv2.findContours(
        binary,
        cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL,
        cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE
    )

    markers = []

    for contour in contours:
        area = cv2.contourArea(contour)

        if area < 100 or area > 3000:
            continue

        x, y, w, h = cv2.boundingRect(contour)

        ratio = w / h

        if not (0.75 <= ratio <= 1.25):
            continue

        rectangular_area = w * h
        fill_ratio = area / rectangular_area

        if fill_ratio < 0.65:
            continue

        markers.append((x, y, w, h))

    # Sap xep tu tren xuong duoi
    markers.sort(key=lambda item: (item[1], item[0]))

    print("TOA DO CAC DIEM DINH VI:")

    for index, (x, y, w, h) in enumerate(markers, start=1):
        center_x = x + w / 2
        center_y = y + h / 2

        print(
            f"Diem {index}: "
            f"x={center_x:.1f}, "
            f"y={center_y:.1f}, "
            f"w={w}, h={h}"
        )

    # Ve cac diem dinh vi len anh
    result = image.copy()

    for x, y, w, h in markers:
        cv2.rectangle(
            result,
            (x, y),
            (x + w, y + h),
            (0, 255, 0),
            3
        )

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    output_path = OUTPUT_DIR / "detected_markers.png"
    cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), result)

    print("--------------------------------")
    print("NHAN DIEN DIEM DINH VI HOAN TAT")
    print("Tong so vung nghi la diem dinh vi:", len(markers))
    print("Anh ket qua:", output_path.resolve())
    print("--------------------------------")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    detect_markers()



=== FILE: archive_backup\detect_part1.py ===

import cv2
import json
import csv
import argparse
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

FILL_THRESHOLD = 55
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 28
BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5

CHOICES = ["A", "B", "C", "D"]


# ==========================================
# DOC GIA TRI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    r = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    roi = gray[
        y-r:y+r+1,
        x-r:x+r+1
    ]

    if roi.shape != (2*r+1, 2*r+1):
        raise ValueError("O dap an nam ngoai anh.")

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= r**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN O TO
# ==========================================

def detect_part1(image_path):

    # Doc anh bai lam da can chinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc anh phieu mau trang
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do 160 o
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh bai lam khong khop phieu mau. "
            "Hay chay align_sheet.py truoc."
        )

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    questions = coordinate_data["questions"]

    if len(questions) != 40:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 40 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    results = []
    debug = student.copy()

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN I")
    print("=" * 65)

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        scores = {}

        # Doc do den cua 4 phuong an
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]

            reference_value = get_center_value(
                template_gray, x, y
            )

            student_value = get_center_value(
                student_gray, x, y
            )

            # Do muc do toi hon so voi phieu mau
            score = reference_value - student_value

            scores[choice] = round(
                max(0, score), 1
            )

        # Tim cac phuong an duoc to ro
        
        # ======================================
        # PHAN LOAI DAP AN THEO TUONG QUAN
        # ======================================

        ranked = sorted(
            scores.items(),
            key=lambda item: item[1],
            reverse=True
        )

        top_choice, top_score = ranked[0]
        second_choice, second_score = ranked[1]

        # Lay 2 diem thap nhat lam muc nen
        lowest_two = sorted(scores.values())[:2]
        baseline = float(np.mean(lowest_two))

        top_margin = top_score - baseline
        second_margin = second_score - baseline

        MARK_MARGIN = 55
        REVIEW_MARGIN = 30

        filled = []
        uncertain = []

        if top_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:

            if second_margin >= MARK_MARGIN:
                answer = "MULTIPLE"
                status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

                filled = [
                    choice
                    for choice in CHOICES
                    if scores[choice] - baseline >= MARK_MARGIN
                ]

            else:
                answer = top_choice
                status = "VALID"
                filled = [top_choice]

        elif top_margin >= REVIEW_MARGIN:

            answer = "REVIEW"
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
            uncertain = [top_choice]

        else:

            answer = ""
            status = "BLANK"

        
        # ======================================
        # LUU KET QUA CUA TUNG CAU
        # ======================================

        results.append({
            "question": q_number,
            "answer": answer,
            "status": status,
            "scores": scores.copy()
        })

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number:02d}: "
            f"{answer if answer else 'BO TRONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # Danh dau cac o tren anh kiem tra
        for choice in CHOICES:

            x, y = question["choices"][choice]
            x, y = int(x), int(y)

            if choice in filled:
                color = (0, 0, 255)       # Do: da to

            elif choice in uncertain:
                color = (0, 165, 255)     # Cam: can kiem tra

            else:
                color = (150, 150, 150)   # Xam: bo trong

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                9,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        # Ghi dap an nhan dang
        first_x, first_y = question["choices"]["D"]

        cv2.putText(
            debug,
            answer if answer else "-",
            (int(first_x) + 12, int(first_y) + 4),
            cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
            0.4,
            (255, 0, 0),
            1
        )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA CSV VA JSON
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.csv"
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1.json"
    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part1_debug.png"

    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "Cau",
            "Dap an",
            "Trang thai",
            "Diem A",
            "Diem B",
            "Diem C",
            "Diem D"
        ])

        for item in results:

            writer.writerow([
                item["question"],
                item["answer"],
                item["status"],
                item["scores"]["A"],
                item["scores"]["B"],
                item["scores"]["C"],
                item["scores"]["D"]
            ])

    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("THONG KE NHAN DIEN")
    print("Nhan dang mot dap an:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu dap an:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDa luu:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default="output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png",

    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        detect_part1(Path(args.image))

    except Exception as error:

        print("\nLOI:", error)

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part2_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_STUDENT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

CHOICES = ["true", "false"]
ROWS = ["a", "b", "c", "d"]

# Nguong thu nghiem tren phieu photocopy
FILL_THRESHOLD = 70
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 58

BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5


# ==========================================
# DOC DO TOI TAI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    radius = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    x1 = x - radius
    y1 = y - radius
    x2 = x + radius + 1
    y2 = y + radius + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do nam ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[
        -radius:radius + 1,
        -radius:radius + 1
    ]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= radius**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN II
# ==========================================

def detect_part2(image_path):

    # Doc anh bai lam da can chinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh bai lam: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc anh phieu mau trang
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    # Kiem tra kich thuoc
    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh khong khop phieu mau. "
            "Hay can chinh lai truoc."
        )

    # Doc file toa do
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 8:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 8 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    # Chuyen sang anh xam
    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    debug = student.copy()

    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN II")
    print("=" * 65)

    # ======================================
    # DOC 8 CAU X 4 Y
    # ======================================

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        for row_label in ROWS:

            row_data = question["rows"][row_label]

            scores = {}

            # Doc hai o DUNG va SAI
            for choice in CHOICES:

                x, y = row_data[choice]

                reference_value = get_center_value(
                    template_gray, x, y
                )

                student_value = get_center_value(
                    student_gray, x, y
                )

                # So sanh voi phieu mau
                score = reference_value - student_value

                scores[choice] = round(
                    max(0, score), 1
                )

            true_score = scores["true"]
            false_score = scores["false"]


            
            # ==================================
            # PHAN LOAI DUNG / SAI
            # ==================================

            true_score = scores["true"]
            false_score = scores["false"]

            if (
                true_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and false_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "MULTIPLE"
                status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

            elif (
                true_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and false_score < REVIEW_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "DUNG"
                status = "VALID"

            elif (
                false_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                and true_score < REVIEW_THRESHOLD
            ):
                answer = "SAI"
                status = "VALID"

            elif max(true_score, false_score) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                answer = "REVIEW"
                status = "UNCERTAIN"

            else:
                answer = ""
                status = "BLANK"


            # ==================================
            # LUU KET QUA
            # ==================================

            results.append({
                "question": q_number,
                "statement": row_label,
                "answer": answer,
                "status": status,
                "true_score": true_score,
                "false_score": false_score
            })

            print(
                f"Cau {q_number:02d} - {row_label}: "
                f"{answer if answer else 'BO TRONG':10s} "
                f"| Dung={true_score:5.1f} "
                f"Sai={false_score:5.1f}"
            )

            # ==================================
            # VE KET QUA LEN ANH
            # ==================================

            for choice in CHOICES:

                x, y = row_data[choice]

                x = int(round(x))
                y = int(round(y))

                score = scores[choice]

                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 0, 255)

                elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 165, 255)

                else:
                    color = (150, 150, 150)

                cv2.circle(
                    debug,
                    (x, y),
                    9,
                    color,
                    2
                )

            # Ghi ket qua ben phai o Sai
            false_x, false_y = row_data["false"]

            display_answer = (
                answer if answer else "-"
            )

            cv2.putText(
                debug,
                display_answer,
                (int(false_x) + 12, int(false_y) + 4),
                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX,
                0.35,
                (255, 0, 0),
                1
            )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    # ======================================
    # LUU FILE KET QUA
    # Tao ten rieng de tranh ghi de CSV dang mo
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"
    )

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_{timestamp}.csv"
    )

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_{timestamp}.json"
    )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part2_debug_{timestamp}.png"
    )

    # Luu CSV
    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.writer(f)

        writer.writerow([
            "Cau",
            "Y",
            "Dap an",
            "Trang thai",
            "Diem Dung",
            "Diem Sai"
        ])

        for item in results:

            writer.writerow([
                item["question"],
                item["statement"],
                item["answer"],
                item["status"],
                item["true_score"],
                item["false_score"]
            ])

    # Luu JSON
    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Luu anh kiem tra
    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 65)
    print("THONG KE NHAN DIEN PHAN II")

    print("Nhan dang mot lua chon:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To ca hai:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU KET QUA:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# ==========================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# ==========================================

if __name__ == "__main__":

    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",

    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:

        print("\nLOI:", error)



=== FILE: archive_backup\detect_part3.py ===

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong thu nghiem cho diem tuong phan cuc bo
# Diem moi khac thang diem cu (khong so sanh truc tiep 90/65 cu).
FILL_THRESHOLD = 32
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 15

# Chenh lech toi thieu giua chu so cao nhat va chu so thu hai
DIGIT_MARGIN = 12

# Ban kinh vung do:
# - vung loi nho ben trong tam o tron
# - vung nen vong ngoai, tranh duong vien in cua o tron
CORE_RADIUS = 3
RING_INNER_RADIUS = 9
RING_OUTER_RADIUS = 11


# ==========================================
# DO TUONG PHAN CUC BO - GIAM ANH HUONG BONG
# ==========================================

def get_local_contrast(gray, x, y):
    """
    Do su khac biet giua loi o tron va nen xung quanh.

    BÃ³ng Ä‘á»• diá»‡n rá»™ng thÆ°á»ng lÃ m tá»‘i cáº£ lÃµi vÃ  ná»n xung quanh.
    Láº¥y hiá»‡u ná»n - lÃµi giÃºp giáº£m áº£nh hÆ°á»Ÿng cá»§a thay Ä‘á»•i Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng
    Ä‘á»“ng Ä‘á»u trong vÃ¹ng nhá».

    VÃ¹ng lÃµi trÃ¡nh Ä‘Æ°á»ng viá»n vÃ²ng trÃ²n.
    VÃ¹ng ná»n lÃ  vÃ nh khÄƒn bÃªn ngoÃ i Ä‘Æ°á»ng viá»n.
    """

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    outer = RING_OUTER_RADIUS

    x1 = x - outer
    y1 = y - outer
    x2 = x + outer + 1
    y2 = y + outer + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2].astype(np.float32)

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-outer:outer + 1, -outer:outer + 1]
    distance_sq = xx**2 + yy**2

    core_mask = distance_sq <= CORE_RADIUS**2

    ring_mask = (
        (distance_sq >= RING_INNER_RADIUS**2)
        & (distance_sq <= RING_OUTER_RADIUS**2)
    )

    core_pixels = roi[core_mask]
    ring_pixels = roi[ring_mask]

    if core_pixels.size == 0 or ring_pixels.size == 0:
        raise ValueError("Khong du pixel de do tuong phan cuc bo.")

    # Trung binh loi o va trung vi nen vong ngoai
    core_mean = float(np.mean(core_pixels))
    ring_median = float(np.median(ring_pixels))

    return ring_median - core_mean


def get_score(student_gray, template_gray, position):
    """
    Diem = tuong phan cuc bo tren bai hoc sinh
           - tuong phan cuc bo tren phieu mau.

    O duoc to se co loi o toi hon nen gan no.
    Bong dien thoai lam toi ca loi va nen, nen duoc giam anh huong.
    """

    x, y = position

    template_contrast = get_local_contrast(
        template_gray, x, y
    )

    student_contrast = get_local_contrast(
        student_gray, x, y
    )

    score = student_contrast - template_contrast

    return round(float(score), 1)


# ==========================================
# PHAN LOAI MOT O NHI PHAN
# ==========================================

def classify_single(score):

    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"

    elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return "BLANK"


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN HAI O DAU PHAY
# ==========================================

def classify_comma(scores):

    marked = [
        i for i, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:

        other = 1 - marked[0]

        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)

        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN 10 CHU SO TRONG MOT COT
# ==========================================

def classify_digit(scores):

    ranked = sorted(
        enumerate(scores),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]

    second_digit, second_score = ranked[1]

    # Kiem tra nhieu chu so duoc to
    marked = [
        digit
        for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    margin = top_score - second_score

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN
    ):
        return str(top_digit)

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN III
# ==========================================

def detect_part3(image_path):

    # Doc anh hoc sinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc phieu mau
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh khong khop phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 6 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    debug = student.copy()

    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN III")
    print("=" * 70)

    # ======================================
    # XU LY TUNG CAU
    # ======================================

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU AM
        # ----------------------------------

        sign_score = get_score(
            student_gray,
            template_gray,
            question["sign"]
        )

        sign_status = classify_single(
            sign_score
        )

        sign = ""

        if sign_status == "MARKED":
            sign = "-"

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU PHAY
        # ----------------------------------

        comma_scores = []

        for position in question["comma_positions"]:

            score = get_score(
                student_gray,
                template_gray,
                position
            )

            comma_scores.append(score)

        comma_result = classify_comma(
            comma_scores
        )

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC 4 COT CHU SO
        # ----------------------------------

        digit_columns = []
        digit_scores_all = []

        for column in range(4):

            scores = []

            # Doc 10 chu so tu 0 den 9
            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                score = get_score(
                    student_gray,
                    template_gray,
                    position
                )

                scores.append(score)

            digit_result = classify_digit(scores)

            digit_columns.append(digit_result)

            digit_scores_all.append(scores)

        # ----------------------------------
        # XAC DINH TRANG THAI CAU HOI
        # ----------------------------------

        has_multiple = (
            comma_result == "MULTIPLE"
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        has_review = (
            sign_status == "REVIEW"
            or comma_result == "REVIEW"
            or "REVIEW" in digit_columns
        )

        digit_values = [
            d for d in digit_columns
            if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE")
        ]

        # Chuoi 4 cot, giu vi tri trong de kiem tra
        raw_digits = "".join(
            d if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE") else "_"
            for d in digit_columns
        )

        has_digit_error = (
            "REVIEW" in digit_columns
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        if has_multiple:

            status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

        elif has_review:

            status = "UNCERTAIN"

        elif not digit_values:

            status = "BLANK"

        else:

            status = "VALID"

        # Chua tu dong chen dau phay vao so
        # De tranh dien giai sai vi tri dau phay
        result = {
            "question": q_number,
            "sign": sign,
            "sign_status": sign_status,
            "sign_score": sign_score,
            "comma_option": comma_result,
            "comma_scores": comma_scores,
            "digit_1": digit_columns[0],
            "digit_2": digit_columns[1],
            "digit_3": digit_columns[2],
            "digit_4": digit_columns[3],
            "raw_digits": raw_digits,
            "digit_scores": digit_scores_all,
            "status": status
        }

        results.append(result)

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number}: "
            f"{sign}{raw_digits} "
            f"| Dau phay={comma_result or 'KHONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # ==================================
        # VE KET QUA LEN ANH
        # ==================================

        # Ve dau am
        sx, sy = map(
            int,
            map(round, question["sign"])
        )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255)
            if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255)
            if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )

        cv2.circle(
            debug,
            (sx, sy),
            9,
            sign_color,
            2
        )

        # Ve dau phay
        for index, position in enumerate(
            question["comma_positions"]
        ):

            x, y = map(
                int,
                map(round, position)
            )

            score = comma_scores[index]

            color = (
                (0, 0, 255)
                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255)
                if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)
            )

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                8,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ve 240 o chu so
        for column in range(4):

            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                x, y = map(
                    int,
                    map(round, position)
                )

                score = digit_scores_all[column][digit]

                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 0, 255)

                elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 165, 255)

                else:
                    color = (150, 150, 150)

                cv2.circle(
                    debug,
                    (x, y),
                    7,
                    color,
                    2
                )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"
    )

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.csv"
    )

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.json"
    )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_debug_{timestamp}.png"
    )

    # Luu CSV
    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=list(results[0].keys())
        )

        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    # Luu JSON
    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Luu anh debug
    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("THONG KE PHAN III")
    print("Nhan dang hop le:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu o:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())

=== FILE: archive_backup\detect_part3_backup.py ===

import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# ==========================================
# CAU HINH
# ==========================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")

DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path(
    "output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"
)

OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong thu nghiem
FILL_THRESHOLD = 90
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 65

# Nguong chenh lech giua hai chu so
DIGIT_MARGIN = 25

BUBBLE_RADIUS = 5


# ==========================================
# DOC DO TOI TAI TAM O TRON
# ==========================================

def get_center_value(gray, x, y):

    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))

    r = BUBBLE_RADIUS

    x1 = x - r
    y1 = y - r
    x2 = x + r + 1
    y2 = y + r + 1

    if (
        x1 < 0 or y1 < 0
        or x2 > gray.shape[1]
        or y2 > gray.shape[0]
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Toa do ngoai anh: ({x}, {y})"
        )

    roi = gray[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r+1, -r:r+1]

    mask = xx**2 + yy**2 <= r**2

    return float(np.median(roi[mask]))


def get_score(student_gray, template_gray, position):

    x, y = position

    reference = get_center_value(
        template_gray, x, y
    )

    student = get_center_value(
        student_gray, x, y
    )

    return round(
        max(0, reference - student),
        1
    )


# ==========================================
# PHAN LOAI MOT O NHI PHAN
# ==========================================

def classify_single(score):

    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"

    elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return "BLANK"


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN HAI O DAU PHAY
# ==========================================

def classify_comma(scores):

    marked = [
        i for i, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:

        other = 1 - marked[0]

        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)

        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN 10 CHU SO TRONG MOT COT
# ==========================================

def classify_digit(scores):

    ranked = sorted(
        enumerate(scores),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]

    second_digit, second_score = ranked[1]

    # Kiem tra nhieu chu so duoc to
    marked = [
        digit
        for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    margin = top_score - second_score

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN
    ):
        return str(top_digit)

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# ==========================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN III
# ==========================================

def detect_part3(image_path):

    # Doc anh hoc sinh
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))

    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh: {image_path}"
        )

    # Doc phieu mau
    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))

    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            "Khong tim thay anh phieu mau."
        )

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh khong khop phieu mau."
        )

    # Doc toa do
    with open(
        COORDINATE_PATH,
        "r",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])

    if len(questions) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 6 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        student,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(
        template,
        cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY
    )

    debug = student.copy()

    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN III")
    print("=" * 70)

    # ======================================
    # XU LY TUNG CAU
    # ======================================

    for question in questions:

        q_number = question["question"]

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU AM
        # ----------------------------------

        sign_score = get_score(
            student_gray,
            template_gray,
            question["sign"]
        )

        sign_status = classify_single(
            sign_score
        )

        sign = ""

        if sign_status == "MARKED":
            sign = "-"

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC DAU PHAY
        # ----------------------------------

        comma_scores = []

        for position in question["comma_positions"]:

            score = get_score(
                student_gray,
                template_gray,
                position
            )

            comma_scores.append(score)

        comma_result = classify_comma(
            comma_scores
        )

        # ----------------------------------
        # DOC 4 COT CHU SO
        # ----------------------------------

        digit_columns = []
        digit_scores_all = []

        for column in range(4):

            scores = []

            # Doc 10 chu so tu 0 den 9
            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                score = get_score(
                    student_gray,
                    template_gray,
                    position
                )

                scores.append(score)

            digit_result = classify_digit(scores)

            digit_columns.append(digit_result)

            digit_scores_all.append(scores)

        # ----------------------------------
        # XAC DINH TRANG THAI CAU HOI
        # ----------------------------------

        has_multiple = (
            comma_result == "MULTIPLE"
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        has_review = (
            sign_status == "REVIEW"
            or comma_result == "REVIEW"
            or "REVIEW" in digit_columns
        )

        digit_values = [
            d for d in digit_columns
            if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE")
        ]

        # Chuoi 4 cot, giu vi tri trong de kiem tra
        raw_digits = "".join(
            d if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE") else "_"
            for d in digit_columns
        )

        has_digit_error = (
            "REVIEW" in digit_columns
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        if has_multiple:

            status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"

        elif has_review:

            status = "UNCERTAIN"

        elif not digit_values:

            status = "BLANK"

        else:

            status = "VALID"

        # Chua tu dong chen dau phay vao so
        # De tranh dien giai sai vi tri dau phay
        result = {
            "question": q_number,
            "sign": sign,
            "sign_status": sign_status,
            "sign_score": sign_score,
            "comma_option": comma_result,
            "comma_scores": comma_scores,
            "digit_1": digit_columns[0],
            "digit_2": digit_columns[1],
            "digit_3": digit_columns[2],
            "digit_4": digit_columns[3],
            "raw_digits": raw_digits,
            "status": status
        }

        results.append(result)

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number}: "
            f"{sign}{raw_digits} "
            f"| Dau phay={comma_result or 'KHONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # ==================================
        # VE KET QUA LEN ANH
        # ==================================

        # Ve dau am
        sx, sy = map(
            int,
            map(round, question["sign"])
        )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255)
            if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255)
            if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )

        cv2.circle(
            debug,
            (sx, sy),
            9,
            sign_color,
            2
        )

        # Ve dau phay
        for index, position in enumerate(
            question["comma_positions"]
        ):

            x, y = map(
                int,
                map(round, position)
            )

            score = comma_scores[index]

            color = (
                (0, 0, 255)
                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255)
                if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)
            )

            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (x, y),
                8,
                color,
                2
            )

        # Ve 240 o chu so
        for column in range(4):

            for digit in range(10):

                position = (
                    question["digits"][str(digit)][column]
                )

                x, y = map(
                    int,
                    map(round, position)
                )

                score = digit_scores_all[column][digit]

                if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 0, 255)

                elif score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
                    color = (0, 165, 255)

                else:
                    color = (150, 150, 150)

                cv2.circle(
                    debug,
                    (x, y),
                    7,
                    color,
                    2
                )

    # ======================================
    # THONG KE
    # ======================================

    valid = sum(
        r["status"] == "VALID"
        for r in results
    )

    blank = sum(
        r["status"] == "BLANK"
        for r in results
    )

    multiple = sum(
        r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        for r in results
    )

    uncertain = sum(
        r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN"
        for r in results
    )

    # ======================================
    # LUU KET QUA
    # ======================================

    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem

    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime(
        "%Y%m%d_%H%M%S"
    )

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.csv"
    )

    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_{timestamp}.json"
    )

    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / (
        f"{stem}_part3_debug_{timestamp}.png"
    )

    # Luu CSV
    with open(
        csv_path,
        "w",
        newline="",
        encoding="utf-8-sig"
    ) as f:

        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=list(results[0].keys())
        )

        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    # Luu JSON
    with open(
        json_path,
        "w",
        encoding="utf-8"
    ) as f:

        json.dump(
            results,
            f,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=4
        )

    # Luu anh debug
    cv2.imwrite(
        str(debug_path),
        debug
    )

    print("\n" + "=" * 70)
    print("THONG KE PHAN III")
    print("Nhan dang hop le:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu o:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())
