=== FILE: archive_backup\detect_part3_shadowfix.py ===
import cv2
import json
import csv
import numpy as np

from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# =========================================================
# CAU HINH PHAN III
# =========================================================

TEMPLATE_PATH = Path("output/answer_sheet_master.png")
COORDINATE_PATH = Path("output/part3_coordinates.json")
DEFAULT_IMAGE = Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("output")

# Nguong moi: diem duoc tinh tu high-pass + tam o da tinh chinh.
# Khong dung lai nguong 90/65 cua phien ban cu.
FILL_THRESHOLD = 16
REVIEW_THRESHOLD = 10
DIGIT_MARGIN = 10

# Kich thuoc hinh hoc tren anh xem thu nghiem 1434 px rong.
# Anh trong du an duoc chuan hoa ve 1769 px rong, nen tu dong scale.
REFERENCE_PREVIEW_WIDTH = 1434.0

# Ban kinh cua vong tron va vung loi, tinh theo scale anh.
CORE_RADIUS_BASE = 3.0
RING_INNER_BASE = 7.2
RING_OUTER_BASE = 10.8
CENTER_SEARCH_BASE = 8.0
ILLUMINATION_SIGMA_BASE = 18.0

CHOICES = list(range(10))


# =========================================================
# TAO BAN DO GRADIENT DE TIM LAI TAM O TRON
# =========================================================

def build_gradient_response(gray, scale):
    """Tao phan hoi vien tron tu gradient, giam anh huong bong nen."""
    smoothed = cv2.GaussianBlur(
        gray,
        (0, 0),
        sigmaX=max(0.8, 1.0 * scale),
        sigmaY=max(0.8, 1.0 * scale)
    )

    gx = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 1, 0, ksize=3)
    gy = cv2.Sobel(smoothed, cv2.CV_32F, 0, 1, ksize=3)
    magnitude = cv2.magnitude(gx, gy)

    ring_inner = RING_INNER_BASE * scale
    ring_outer = RING_OUTER_BASE * scale
    radius = int(np.ceil(ring_outer))

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-radius:radius + 1, -radius:radius + 1]
    distance = np.sqrt(xx * xx + yy * yy)

    ring_mask = (
        (distance >= ring_inner)
        & (distance <= ring_outer)
    ).astype(np.float32)

    total = float(ring_mask.sum())
    if total <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Khong tao duoc mat na vong tron.")

    kernel = ring_mask / total

    response = cv2.filter2D(
        magnitude,
        cv2.CV_32F,
        kernel,
        borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT
    )

    return response


def refine_center(response, x, y, scale):
    """
    Tim lai tam o tron quanh toa do hieu chuan.
    Dung do manh gradient tren vong vien, khong dung do toi o loi.
    """
    x = int(round(x))
    y = int(round(y))
    search = int(round(CENTER_SEARCH_BASE * scale))

    h, w = response.shape

    x1 = max(0, x - search)
    x2 = min(w, x + search + 1)
    y1 = max(0, y - search)
    y2 = min(h, y + search + 1)

    patch = response[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    yy, xx = np.mgrid[y1:y2, x1:x2]

    # Uu tien tam gan toa do chuan neu hai vi tri co gradient gan nhau.
    distance_penalty = 0.4 * np.sqrt(
        (xx - x) ** 2 + (yy - y) ** 2
    )

    adjusted = patch - distance_penalty

    iy, ix = np.unravel_index(
        np.argmax(adjusted),
        adjusted.shape
    )

    return int(x1 + ix), int(y1 + iy)


# =========================================================
# BU SANG NEN VA DO MUC TO O LOI
# =========================================================

def build_ink_map(gray, scale):
    """
    Gaussian blur uoc luong nen sang thay doi cham.
    Nen - anh goc tao ban do muc toi cuc bo:
    - bong dien thoai dien rong duoc giam anh huong;
    - vet but trong loi o tao gia tri duong ro hon.
    """
    sigma = ILLUMINATION_SIGMA_BASE * scale

    background = cv2.GaussianBlur(
        gray,
        (0, 0),
        sigmaX=sigma,
        sigmaY=sigma,
        borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT
    )

    ink_map = (
        background.astype(np.float32)
        - gray.astype(np.float32)
    )

    return ink_map


def disk_mean(ink_map, x, y, radius):
    x = int(x)
    y = int(y)
    r = max(1, int(round(radius)))

    h, w = ink_map.shape

    if (
        x - r < 0 or y - r < 0
        or x + r >= w or y + r >= h
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            f"Vung do vuot ngoai anh tai ({x}, {y})."
        )

    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r:r + 1, -r:r + 1]
    mask = xx * xx + yy * yy <= r * r

    patch = ink_map[
        y - r:y + r + 1,
        x - r:x + r + 1
    ]

    return float(np.mean(patch[mask]))


def get_score(
    student_ink,
    template_ink,
    student_response,
    template_response,
    position,
    scale
):
    """
    Diem = muc toi cuc bo tren bai hoc sinh
           - muc toi cuc bo tren phieu mau.

    Truoc khi do, tu dong tinh chinh tam o tren anh hoc sinh va anh mau.
    """
    x, y = position

    student_center = refine_center(
        student_response, x, y, scale
    )

    template_center = refine_center(
        template_response, x, y, scale
    )

    radius = CORE_RADIUS_BASE * scale

    student_value = disk_mean(
        student_ink,
        student_center[0],
        student_center[1],
        radius
    )

    template_value = disk_mean(
        template_ink,
        template_center[0],
        template_center[1],
        radius
    )

    score = student_value - template_value

    return (
        round(float(score), 1),
        student_center,
        template_center
    )


# =========================================================
# PHAN LOAI
# =========================================================

def classify_single(score):
    if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD:
        return "MARKED"
    if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"
    return "BLANK"


def classify_comma(scores):
    marked = [
        index for index, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) >= 2:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    if len(marked) == 1:
        other = 1 - marked[0]
        if scores[other] < REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
            return str(marked[0] + 1)
        return "REVIEW"

    if max(scores) >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


def classify_digit(scores):
    ranked = sorted(
        enumerate(scores),
        key=lambda item: item[1],
        reverse=True
    )

    top_digit, top_score = ranked[0]
    second_score = ranked[1][1]

    marked = [
        digit for digit, score in enumerate(scores)
        if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
    ]

    if len(marked) > 1:
        return "MULTIPLE"

    margin = top_score - second_score

    if (
        top_score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
        and margin >= DIGIT_MARGIN
    ):
        return str(top_digit)

    if top_score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD:
        return "REVIEW"

    return ""


# =========================================================
# NHAN DIEN PHAN III
# =========================================================

def detect_part3(image_path):
    student = cv2.imread(str(image_path))
    if student is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh bai lam: {image_path}"
        )

    template = cv2.imread(str(TEMPLATE_PATH))
    if template is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(
            f"Khong doc duoc anh mau: {TEMPLATE_PATH}"
        )

    if student.shape[:2] != template.shape[:2]:
        raise ValueError(
            "Kich thuoc anh bai lam khong khop anh mau. "
            "Hay chay align_sheet.py truoc."
        )

    with open(COORDINATE_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        coordinate_data = json.load(f)

    questions = coordinate_data.get("questions", [])
    if len(questions) != 6:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Can 6 cau, hien co {len(questions)} cau."
        )

    # Anh mau du an rong 1769 px; anh trong tin nhan co the duoc
    # nen xuong 1434 px. Scale nay giu dung kich thuoc hinh hoc.
    scale = student.shape[1] / REFERENCE_PREVIEW_WIDTH

    student_gray = cv2.cvtColor(student, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    template_gray = cv2.cvtColor(template, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    # Ban do muc toi sau bu sang
    student_ink = build_ink_map(student_gray, scale)
    template_ink = build_ink_map(template_gray, scale)

    # Ban do gradient de tu can tam cac vong tron
    student_response = build_gradient_response(student_gray, scale)
    template_response = build_gradient_response(template_gray, scale)

    debug = student.copy()
    results = []

    print("\nKET QUA NHAN DIEN PHAN III - BU SANG + TU CAN TAM")
    print("=" * 76)
    print(
        f"Scale={scale:.3f} | Fill={FILL_THRESHOLD} | "
        f"Review={REVIEW_THRESHOLD}"
    )

    for question in questions:
        q_number = question["question"]

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # DAU AM
        # -------------------------------------------------
        sign_score, sign_center, _ = get_score(
            student_ink,
            template_ink,
            student_response,
            template_response,
            question["sign"],
            scale
        )

        sign_status = classify_single(sign_score)
        sign = "-" if sign_status == "MARKED" else ""

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # DAU PHAY
        # -------------------------------------------------
        comma_scores = []
        comma_centers = []

        for position in question["comma_positions"]:
            score, center, _ = get_score(
                student_ink,
                template_ink,
                student_response,
                template_response,
                position,
                scale
            )
            comma_scores.append(score)
            comma_centers.append(center)

        comma_result = classify_comma(comma_scores)

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # BON COT CHU SO
        # -------------------------------------------------
        digit_columns = []
        digit_scores_all = []
        digit_centers_all = []

        for column in range(4):
            scores = []
            centers = []

            for digit in range(10):
                position = question["digits"][str(digit)][column]

                score, center, _ = get_score(
                    student_ink,
                    template_ink,
                    student_response,
                    template_response,
                    position,
                    scale
                )

                scores.append(score)
                centers.append(center)

            digit_columns.append(classify_digit(scores))
            digit_scores_all.append(scores)
            digit_centers_all.append(centers)

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # TRANG THAI
        # -------------------------------------------------
        has_multiple = (
            comma_result == "MULTIPLE"
            or "MULTIPLE" in digit_columns
        )

        has_review = (
            sign_status == "REVIEW"
            or comma_result == "REVIEW"
            or "REVIEW" in digit_columns
        )

        digit_values = [
            d for d in digit_columns
            if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE")
        ]

        raw_digits = "".join(
            d if d not in ("", "REVIEW", "MULTIPLE") else "_"
            for d in digit_columns
        )

        if has_multiple:
            status = "MULTIPLE_MARKS"
        elif has_review:
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
        elif not digit_values and sign_status != "MARKED" and not comma_result:
            status = "BLANK"
        elif not digit_values:
            status = "UNCERTAIN"
        else:
            status = "VALID"

        result = {
            "question": q_number,
            "sign": sign,
            "sign_status": sign_status,
            "sign_score": sign_score,
            "comma_option": comma_result,
            "comma_scores": comma_scores,
            "digit_1": digit_columns[0],
            "digit_2": digit_columns[1],
            "digit_3": digit_columns[2],
            "digit_4": digit_columns[3],
            "raw_digits": raw_digits,
            "digit_scores": digit_scores_all,
            "status": status
        }

        results.append(result)

        print(
            f"Cau {q_number}: {sign}{raw_digits} "
            f"| Dau phay={comma_result or 'KHONG'} "
            f"| {status}"
        )

        # -------------------------------------------------
        # VE ANH KIEM TRA
        # -------------------------------------------------
        def draw_point(center, color, radius):
            cv2.circle(
                debug,
                (int(center[0]), int(center[1])),
                radius,
                color,
                2
            )

        sign_color = (
            (0, 0, 255) if sign_status == "MARKED"
            else (0, 165, 255) if sign_status == "REVIEW"
            else (150, 150, 150)
        )
        draw_point(sign_center, sign_color, max(6, int(9 * scale)))

        for index, center in enumerate(comma_centers):
            score = comma_scores[index]
            color = (
                (0, 0, 255) if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                else (0, 165, 255) if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                else (150, 150, 150)
            )
            draw_point(center, color, max(6, int(8 * scale)))

        for column in range(4):
            for digit in range(10):
                score = digit_scores_all[column][digit]
                center = digit_centers_all[column][digit]

                color = (
                    (0, 0, 255) if score >= FILL_THRESHOLD
                    else (0, 165, 255) if score >= REVIEW_THRESHOLD
                    else (150, 150, 150)
                )

                draw_point(
                    center,
                    color,
                    max(5, int(7 * scale))
                )

    # =====================================================
    # THONG KE
    # =====================================================
    valid = sum(r["status"] == "VALID" for r in results)
    blank = sum(r["status"] == "BLANK" for r in results)
    multiple = sum(r["status"] == "MULTIPLE_MARKS" for r in results)
    uncertain = sum(r["status"] == "UNCERTAIN" for r in results)

    # =====================================================
    # LUU KET QUA - TEN FILE CO DONG HO
    # =====================================================
    OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    stem = Path(image_path).stem
    timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S")

    csv_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_{timestamp}.csv"
    json_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_{timestamp}.json"
    debug_path = OUTPUT_DIR / f"{stem}_part3_shadowfix_debug_{timestamp}.png"

    with open(csv_path, "w", newline="", encoding="utf-8-sig") as f:
        writer = csv.DictWriter(
            f,
            fieldnames=list(results[0].keys())
        )
        writer.writeheader()
        writer.writerows(results)

    with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(results, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=4)

    cv2.imwrite(str(debug_path), debug)

    print("\n" + "=" * 76)
    print("THONG KE PHAN III")
    print("Nhan dang hop le:", valid)
    print("Bo trong:", blank)
    print("To nhieu o:", multiple)
    print("Can kiem tra:", uncertain)

    print("\nDA LUU:")
    print(csv_path.resolve())
    print(json_path.resolve())
    print(debug_path.resolve())


# =========================================================
# CHUONG TRINH CHINH
# =========================================================

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse

    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()

    parser.add_argument(
        "--image",
        default=str(DEFAULT_IMAGE),
        help="Anh bai lam da can chinh"
    )

    args = parser.parse_args()

    try:
        detect_part3(Path(args.image))
    except Exception as error:
        print("\nLOI:", error)



=== FILE: archive_backup\render_template.py ===

import fitz
from pathlib import Path

# Tim file PDF mau trong thu muc du an
pdf_files = list(Path(".").glob("*.pdf"))

if not pdf_files:
    print("LOI: Khong tim thay file PDF mau!")
else:
    pdf_path = pdf_files[0]
    output_dir = Path("output")
    output_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)

    print("Dang doc file:", pdf_path.name)

    # Mo file PDF
    document = fitz.open(pdf_path)

    # Chi xu ly trang dau tien
    page = document[0]

    # Tang do phan giai anh len 3 lan
    matrix = fitz.Matrix(3, 3)

    # Chuyen trang PDF thanh anh
    pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
        matrix=matrix,
        alpha=False
    )

    # Luu anh PNG
    output_path = output_dir / "answer_sheet_master.png"
    pixmap.save(output_path)

    print("--------------------------------")
    print("CHUYEN DOI THANH CONG!")
    print("So trang PDF:", len(document))
    print("Kich thuoc anh:", pixmap.width, "x", pixmap.height)
    print("Anh duoc luu tai:", output_path.resolve())
    print("--------------------------------")

    document.close()



=== FILE: archive_backup\test_bubble_detector.py ===
# test_bubble_detector.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
KIá»‚M CHá»¨NG THá»°C NGHIá»†M THUáº¬T TOÃN BUBBLE DETECTOR KHÃNG BÃ“NG Äá»”
"""
import json
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import numpy as np

from bubble_detector import BubbleDetector


def run_benchmark():
    detector = BubbleDetector(core_radius=9, ring_inner=17, ring_outer=22)

    part1_coord_path = Path("output/part1_coordinates.json")
    part2_coord_path = Path("output/part2_coordinates.json")

    if not part1_coord_path.exists() or not part2_coord_path.exists():
        print("[!] Lá»—i: ChÆ°a tÃ¬m tháº¥y file tá»a Ä‘á»™ part1_coordinates.json hoáº·c part2_coordinates.json.")
        return

    with open(part1_coord_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        p1_data = json.load(f)["questions"]
    with open(part2_coord_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        p2_data = json.load(f)["questions"]

    test_images = [
        Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_01_aligned.png"),
        Path("output/bai_kiem_tra_02_aligned.png"),
    ]

    print("=" * 80)
    print("BENCHMARK: KIá»‚M CHá»¨NG CHá»ˆ Sá» RELATIVE DARKNESS TRÃŠN Dá»® LIá»†U THá»°C Táº¾")
    print("=" * 80)

    for img_path in test_images:
        if not img_path.exists():
            print(f"[!] Bá» qua (chÆ°a cÃ³ file): {img_path}")
            continue

        img_bgr = cv2.imread(str(img_path))
        gray = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        print(f"\n[*] Äang Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡: {img_path.name} (KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c: {gray.shape[1]}x{gray.shape[0]})")

        filled_scores = []
        blank_scores = []

        # 1. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ Pháº§n I (40 cÃ¢u) sá»­ dá»¥ng tham sá»‘ tá»‘i Æ°u (min_signal=0.095)
        p1_results = []
        for q_item in p1_data:
            q_num = q_item["question"]
            res = detector.classify_mcq_row(gray, q_item["choices"], min_signal=0.095, contrast_margin=0.055)
            p1_results.append((q_num, res["answer"], res["status"], res["scores"]))

            for opt, score in res["scores"].items():
                if res["answer"] == opt and res["status"] == "VALID":
                    filled_scores.append(score)
                elif res["answer"] == "":
                    blank_scores.append(score)

        print("  --- Káº¿t quáº£ Pháº§n I (16 cÃ¢u Ä‘áº§u) ---")
        for q_num, ans, status, scores in p1_results[:16]:
            print(f"  CÃ¢u {q_num:02d}: ÄÃ¡p Ã¡n=[{ans or ' '}] | Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i={status:<11} | Scores={scores}")

        # 2. ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ Pháº§n II (8 cÃ¢u x 4 Ã½)
        p2_results = []
        for q_item in p2_data:
            q_num = q_item["question"]
            row_res = {}
            for row_k in ["a", "b", "c", "d"]:
                true_pt = tuple(q_item["rows"][row_k]["true"])
                false_pt = tuple(q_item["rows"][row_k]["false"])
                eval_res = detector.classify_true_false_row(gray, true_pt, false_pt, min_signal=0.095, contrast_margin=0.055)
                row_res[row_k] = eval_res
                if eval_res["answer"] == "Ä":
                    filled_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])
                elif eval_res["answer"] == "S":
                    filled_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                else:
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_true"])
                    blank_scores.append(eval_res["score_false"])

            p2_results.append((q_num, row_res))

        print("\n  --- Káº¿t quáº£ Pháº§n II (CÃ¢u 1 vÃ  CÃ¢u 2) ---")
        for q_num, row_dict in p2_results[:2]:
            row_str = " | ".join([f"{k}:{v['answer'] or '-'}(T:{v['score_true']},F:{v['score_false']})" for k, v in row_dict.items()])
            print(f"  CÃ¢u {q_num}: {row_str}")

        # Thá»‘ng kÃª phÃ¢n phá»‘i
        if filled_scores and blank_scores:
            print("\n  --- THá»NG KÃŠ PHÃ‚N TÃCH GIÃ TRá»Š (SEPARATION MARGIN) ---")
            print(f"  Ã” ÄÃƒ TÃ”    : Min = {min(filled_scores):.3f} | Mean = {np.mean(filled_scores):.3f} | Max = {max(filled_scores):.3f}")
            print(f"  Ã” Bá»Ž TRá»NG : Min = {min(blank_scores):.3f} | Mean = {np.mean(blank_scores):.3f} | Max = {max(blank_scores):.3f}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_benchmark()

=== FILE: scratch\add_smart_paste_endpoint.py ===
import re

with open("app.py", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    content = f.read()

target = """        save_to_disk()
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "exam_name": exam_data["exam_name"],
            "codes_loaded": list(exam_data["answer_keys"].keys()),
            "exam_info": exam["exam_info"]
        }
    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i file Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: {err}")"""

replacement = """        save_to_disk()
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "exam_name": exam_data["exam_name"],
            "codes_loaded": list(exam_data["answer_keys"].keys()),
            "exam_info": exam["exam_info"]
        }
    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i file Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: {err}")


@app.post("/api/smart-paste-answer-key")
async def smart_paste_answer_key(payload: SmartPasteRequest):
    global pipeline
    if not payload.text or not payload.text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¡n ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n.")

    try:
        parsed_keys = ExamManager.smart_parse_text_keys(payload.text, default_made=payload.default_made or "0001")
        if not parsed_keys:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c cÃ¢u há»i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n nÃ o. HÃ£y kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng dÃ¡n (VD: 1.A 2.B hoáº·c 1A 2B).")

        exam = get_current_exam()
        if not exam.get("exam_data"):
            exam["exam_data"] = {
                "exam_name": f"{exam['exam_info'].get('subject', 'Äá» thi')} - {exam['exam_info'].get('period', 'Kiá»ƒm tra')}",
                "subject": exam["exam_info"].get("subject", "Sinh há»c"),
                "period": exam["exam_info"].get("period", "Giá»¯a ká»³ I"),
                "answer_keys": {},
                "scoring_rules": {
                    "part1": {"points_per_question": 0.25},
                    "part2": {
                        "type": "graduated_moet",
                        "table": {"1": 0.1, "2": 0.25, "3": 0.5, "4": 1.0}
                    },
                    "part3": {"points_per_question": 0.25}
                }
            }

        # Cáº­p nháº­t cÃ¡c mÃ£ Ä‘á» má»›i parsed vÃ o answer_keys
        exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"].update(parsed_keys)
        exam["exam_info"]["is_configured"] = True

        pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=exam["exam_data"])

        # TÃ¡i cháº¥m cÃ¡c bÃ i thi cÃ³ trong danh sÃ¡ch
        re_graded_count = 0
        for sub_id, sub in exam.get("submissions", {}).items():
            sub_code = sub.get("exam_code", "")
            if sub_code in exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"]:
                aligned_p = None
                for p in [GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png", UPLOAD_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.png", GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_aligned.jpg"]:
                    if p.exists():
                        aligned_p = p
                        break

                if aligned_p and aligned_p.exists():
                    img = cv2.imread(str(aligned_p))
                    if img is not None:
                        updated = pipeline.process_sheet(img, sub.get("file_name", ""), forced_exam_code=sub_code)
                        updated["submission_id"] = sub_id
                        updated["original_filename"] = sub.get("file_name", "")
                        
                        graded_path = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_graded.jpg"
                        cv2.imwrite(str(graded_path), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, 90])
                        
                        preview_webp = GRADED_DIR / f"{sub_id}_preview.webp"
                        cv2.imwrite(str(preview_webp), updated["graded_image_bgr"], [cv2.IMWRITE_WEBP_QUALITY, 80])
                        
                        del updated["graded_image_bgr"]
                        exam["submissions"][sub_id] = updated
                        re_graded_count += 1

        save_to_disk()
        return {
            "status": "SUCCESS",
            "codes_loaded": list(parsed_keys.keys()),
            "total_codes": list(exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"].keys()),
            "re_graded_count": re_graded_count,
            "details": {
                c: {
                    "part1": len(k.get("part1", {})),
                    "part2": len(k.get("part2", {})),
                    "part3": len(k.get("part3", {}))
                } for c, k in parsed_keys.items()
            },
            "exam_info": exam["exam_info"]
        }
    except HTTPException:
        raise
    except Exception as err:
        traceback.print_exc()
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Lá»—i xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n dÃ¡n: {err}")"""

if target in content:
    content = content.replace(target, replacement, 1)
    with open("app.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(content)
    print("SUCCESS: Endpoint added.")
else:
    print("ERROR: Target not found.")



=== FILE: scratch\check_orientation.py ===
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
import cv2
import numpy as np
from scratch.orig_align_sheet import rotate_cardinal, detect_fiducial_squares, find_adaptive_4_corners

img = cv2.imread("samples/bai_kiem_tra_01.jpg")
print("Original shape:", img.shape)

# Let's check ink distribution in angle 0 vs angle 180
gray0 = cv2.cvtColor(img, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
cands0 = detect_fiducial_squares(gray0)
corners0 = find_adaptive_4_corners(cands0, gray0.shape[1], gray0.shape[0])

# Top 30% vs Bot 30%
if corners0 is not None:
    tl, tr, br, bl = corners0
    min_y = int(min(tl[1], tr[1]))
    max_y = int(max(bl[1], br[1]))
    sheet_h = max_y - min_y
    top_part = gray0[min_y : min_y + int(sheet_h * 0.3), :]
    bot_part = gray0[max_y - int(sheet_h * 0.3) : max_y, :]
    top_ink = np.sum(top_part < 100)
    bot_ink = np.sum(bot_part < 100)
    print(f"Angle 0: top_ink={top_ink}, bot_ink={bot_ink}, ratio={top_ink/max(1, bot_ink):.2f}")

gray180 = cv2.cvtColor(rotate_cardinal(img, 180), cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
cands180 = detect_fiducial_squares(gray180)
corners180 = find_adaptive_4_corners(cands180, gray180.shape[1], gray180.shape[0])
if corners180 is not None:
    tl, tr, br, bl = corners180
    min_y = int(min(tl[1], tr[1]))
    max_y = int(max(bl[1], br[1]))
    sheet_h = max_y - min_y
    top_part = gray180[min_y : min_y + int(sheet_h * 0.3), :]
    bot_part = gray180[max_y - int(sheet_h * 0.3) : max_y, :]
    top_ink = np.sum(top_part < 100)
    bot_ink = np.sum(bot_part < 100)
    print(f"Angle 180: top_ink={top_ink}, bot_ink={bot_ink}, ratio={top_ink/max(1, bot_ink):.2f}")

=== FILE: scratch\compare_norm_ink.py ===
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
import cv2
import numpy as np
from align_sheet_v2 import normalize_illumination

im = cv2.imread("output/web_uploads/9e965acc_aligned.png")

# Case A: Without normalize_illumination (Original pipeline behavior)
gray_a = cv2.cvtColor(im, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
bg_a = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_a, (0, 0), sigmaX=22.0, sigmaY=22.0)
ink_a = bg_a.astype(np.float32) - gray_a.astype(np.float32)

# Case B: With normalize_illumination
norm_bgr = normalize_illumination(im)
gray_b = cv2.cvtColor(norm_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
bg_b = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray_b, (0, 0), sigmaX=22.0, sigmaY=22.0)
ink_b = bg_b.astype(np.float32) - gray_b.astype(np.float32)

print("Case A (Original) ink_map mean:", np.mean(ink_a), "max:", np.max(ink_a), "std:", np.std(ink_a))
print("Case B (With norm) ink_map mean:", np.mean(ink_b), "max:", np.max(ink_b), "std:", np.std(ink_b))

# Let's test _read_header_ink under both cases
from full_grading_pipeline import FullGradingPipeline
pipeline = FullGradingPipeline()

sbd_a, r_made_a, d_made_a, anom_a = pipeline._read_header_ink(ink_a, im)
print("\nCase A (Original):")
print("SBD:", sbd_a, "MÃ£ Ä‘á»:", d_made_a, "Anomalies:", len(anom_a), [x['msg'] for x in anom_a])

sbd_b, r_made_b, d_made_b, anom_b = pipeline._read_header_ink(ink_b, im)
print("\nCase B (With norm):")
print("SBD:", sbd_b, "MÃ£ Ä‘á»:", d_made_b, "Anomalies:", len(anom_b), [x['msg'] for x in anom_b])

=== FILE: scratch\debug_orig_steps.py ===
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
import cv2
import numpy as np
import time
from pathlib import Path

from scratch.orig_align_sheet import (
    auto_orient_sheet_robust,
    detect_fiducial_markers_legacy,
    select_best_5,
    detect_a4_paper_contour,
    CANONICAL_WIDTH,
    CANONICAL_HEIGHT,
    TPL_LEFT_5,
    TPL_RIGHT_5
)

raw = cv2.imread("output/web_uploads/9e965acc_raw.png")
print("1. Testing auto_orient_sheet_robust...")
t0 = time.time()
image_bgr, corners_4 = auto_orient_sheet_robust(raw)
t1 = time.time()
print(f"   Done in {t1-t0:.3f}s. Corners:", corners_4 is not None)

gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
h, w = gray.shape

print("2. Testing detect_fiducial_markers_legacy...")
t0 = time.time()
lc, rc, _ = detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray)
t1 = time.time()
print(f"   Done in {t1-t0:.3f}s. lc len: {len(lc)}, rc len: {len(rc)}")

if len(lc) <= 15:
    t0 = time.time()
    src_left = select_best_5(lc, min_span=h * 0.50)
    t1 = time.time()
    print(f"   select_best_5 left done in {t1-t0:.3f}s:", src_left is not None)
else:
    print(f"   [!] lc has {len(lc)} candidates! select_best_5 would try combinations({len(lc)}, 5) = hang!")

# But look at Táº¦NG 2 in orig_align_sheet:
# If Táº¦NG 1 fails or is skipped:
# Táº¦NG 2: Náº®N 4 Má»C GÃ“C OMR THÃCH á»¨NG
print("3. Testing Táº¦NG 2 with corners_4...")
if corners_4 is not None:
    tpl_corners = np.array([
        TPL_LEFT_5[0], TPL_RIGHT_5[0],
        TPL_RIGHT_5[4], TPL_LEFT_5[4]
    ], dtype=np.float32)
    H_corners = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(corners_4, tpl_corners)
    canvas = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H_corners, (CANONICAL_WIDTH, CANONICAL_HEIGHT), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
    print("   âœ“ Canvas generated by Táº¦NG 2! Shape:", canvas.shape)
    cv2.imwrite("scratch/test_tang2_aligned.png", canvas)

    # Let's compare test_tang2_aligned.png with 9e965acc_aligned.png!
    im_ref = cv2.imread("output/web_uploads/9e965acc_aligned.png")
    diff = np.abs(canvas.astype(np.float32) - im_ref.astype(np.float32))
    print(f"   Diff vs 9e965acc_aligned.png: mean diff = {np.mean(diff):.3f}")



=== FILE: scratch\debug_p3.py ===
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))

import cv2
import numpy as np
from align_sheet_v2 import align_sheet_4band
from full_grading_pipeline import Part3ShadowfixReader, FullGradingPipeline

def analyze_sheet(img_name, desc):
    print("=" * 80)
    print(f"ANALYZING: {desc} ({img_name})")
    print("=" * 80)
    img_path = Path(r"C:\Users\DinhDuyLinh\Desktop\11b") / img_name
    aligned = align_sheet_4band(img_path, Path(f"scratch/debug_{img_name}"))
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(aligned, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)

    bg = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (51, 51), 0)
    diff = cv2.subtract(bg, gray)
    ink_map = cv2.normalize(diff, None, 0, 255, cv2.NORM_MINMAX)

    p3_file = json.load(open("output/part3_coordinates.json", encoding="utf-8"))
    p3_coords = p3_file["questions"]
    reader = Part3ShadowfixReader(p3_coords, scale=1.0)
    resp = reader._build_gradient_response(gray)

    res, anoms = reader.read_filtered(gray, ink_map, active_q_nums=[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6])
    print("RESULT FROM read_filtered:")
    for q_num, val in res.items():
        print(f"  Cau {q_num}: raw='{val['raw_string']}', norm={val['normalized_value']}, valid={val['is_valid']}, digits={val['digits']}, anoms={val['anomalies']}")

    # Chi tiáº¿t tá»«ng bubble cho cÃ¡c cÃ¢u
    for q in p3_coords:
        q_num = int(q["question"])
        print(f"\n--- CHI TIET CAU {q_num} ---")
        # Sign
        s_val, _ = reader._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, q["sign"][0], q["sign"][1])
        print(f"  Sign (-): {s_val:.2f}")
        for idx, cpos in enumerate(q["comma_positions"]):
            c_val, _ = reader._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, cpos[0], cpos[1])
            print(f"  Comma {idx+1}: {c_val:.2f}")
        for col in range(4):
            scores = []
            for d in range(10):
                val, pt = reader._refine_and_score(ink_map, resp, q["digits"][str(d)][col][0], q["digits"][str(d)][col][1])
                scores.append((d, round(val, 2)))
            ranked = sorted(scores, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
            print(f"  Col {col+1}: top 4 = {ranked[:4]}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    analyze_sheet("1793866080261_1554388614628998194_1096305833999352470_70dcf339630643e28bbf84c4e7235384.jpg", "BAI 3 (Cau 1 to 2134)")
    analyze_sheet("1793866080504_1554388614628998194_1096305833999352470_aac0e872b9ff341dd8dcee9463cd053c.jpg", "BAI 5 (Cau 3 to 10)")

=== FILE: scratch\fix_exam_manager.py ===
with open("exam_manager.py", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    lines = f.readlines()

start_idx = None
for idx, l in enumerate(lines):
    if "def _parse_single_key_block(" in l:
        start_idx = idx
        break

if start_idx is not None:
    new_code = '''    @classmethod
    def _parse_single_key_block(cls, text: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        import re
        p1, p2, p3 = {}, {}, {}
        norm = text.upper().replace("Ã", "Ä")
        
        # Sáº¯p xáº¿p cÃ¡c cá»¥m tá»« dÃ i trÆ°á»›c (III rá»“i II rá»“i I) Ä‘á»ƒ trÃ¡nh khá»›p tiá»n tá»‘ nháº§m láº«n
        sec_regex = r'(?i)(?:^|\\n)\\s*(PHáº¦N\\s*III\\b|PHAN\\s*3\\b|TLN\\b|PHáº¦N\\s*II\\b|PHAN\\s*2\\b|DS\\b|ÄS\\b|PHáº¦N\\s*I\\b|PHAN\\s*1\\b|TN\\b)\\s*[:\\-\\.]?'
        matches = list(re.finditer(sec_regex, norm))
        
        p1_text, p2_text, p3_text = "", "", ""
        if matches:
            for idx, m in enumerate(matches):
                label = m.group(1).upper()
                start = m.end()
                end = matches[idx+1].start() if idx+1 < len(matches) else len(norm)
                chunk = norm[start:end]
                if any(k in label for k in ["III", "3", "TLN"]):
                    p3_text += "\\n" + chunk
                elif any(k in label for k in ["II", "2", "DS", "ÄS"]):
                    p2_text += "\\n" + chunk
                elif any(k in label for k in ["I", "1", "TN"]):
                    p1_text += "\\n" + chunk
        else:
            lines_text = [l.strip() for l in norm.split("\\n") if l.strip()]
            for line in lines_text:
                if re.search(r'[ÄS]', line) and not re.search(r'[A-D]', line):
                    p2_text += "\\n" + line
                elif re.search(r'\\d+(?:[.,]\\d+)?', line) and not re.search(r'[A-DÄS]', line):
                    p3_text += "\\n" + line
                else:
                    p1_text += "\\n" + line

        # 1. Pháº§n I
        p1_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\\s;,]|cÃ¢u\\s*)(\\d{1,2})[\\.:\\)\\-\\s]*([ABCD])\\b', p1_text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p1_items:
            for q_str, ans in p1_items:
                q_num = int(q_str)
                if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P1:
                    p1[q_num] = ans.upper()
        else:
            tokens = re.findall(r'\\b[ABCD]+\\b', p1_text)
            flat_chars = "".join(tokens)
            for idx, char in enumerate(flat_chars[:cls.MAX_P1], start=1):
                p1[idx] = char

        # 2. Pháº§n II: Há»— trá»£ linh hoáº¡t cáº£ khá»‘i cÃ¢u 'CÃ¢u 1: a) Ä, b) S...' vÃ  dáº¡ng láº·p '1a: Ä, 1b: S...'
        q_blocks = re.split(r'(?i)(?:^|\\n)\\s*(?:cÃ¢u\\s*)?(\\d{1,2})\\s*[:\\.\\)]', p2_text)
        if len(q_blocks) > 1:
            for i in range(1, len(q_blocks), 2):
                try:
                    q_num = int(q_blocks[i])
                    if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        block = q_blocks[i+1]
                        subs = re.findall(r'([ABCDa-d])[\\.:\\)\\-\\s]*([ÄS])', block)
                        if subs:
                            p2[q_num] = {k.lower(): v for k, v in subs}
                except (ValueError, IndexError):
                    pass

        if not p2:
            p2_sub_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\\s;,]|cÃ¢u\\s*)(\\d{1,2})\\s*([ABCDa-d])[\\.:\\)\\-\\s]*([ÄS])\\b', p2_text, re.IGNORECASE)
            if p2_sub_items:
                for q_str, sub_key, val in p2_sub_items:
                    q_num = int(q_str)
                    if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        if q_num not in p2:
                            p2[q_num] = {}
                        p2[q_num][sub_key.lower()] = val.upper()

        if not p2:
            for line in p2_text.split('\\n'):
                line = line.strip()
                m = re.match(r'^(?:cÃ¢u\\s*)?(\\d{1,2})[\\.:\\)\\-\\s]*([ÄS\\s]{4,})', line, re.IGNORECASE)
                if m:
                    q_num = int(m.group(1))
                    chars = [c for c in m.group(2) if c in "ÄS"]
                    if len(chars) == 4 and 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P2:
                        p2[q_num] = {"a": chars[0], "b": chars[1], "c": chars[2], "d": chars[3]}
            if not p2:
                all_ds = [c for c in p2_text if c in "ÄS"]
                num_rows = min(len(all_ds) // 4, cls.MAX_P2)
                for r_idx in range(num_rows):
                    chunk = all_ds[r_idx*4 : (r_idx+1)*4]
                    p2[r_idx + 1] = {"a": chunk[0], "b": chunk[1], "c": chunk[2], "d": chunk[3]}

        # 3. Pháº§n III
        p3_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\\s;,]|cÃ¢u\\s*)(\\d{1,2})[:\\)\\.\\s]+(-?\\d+(?:[.,]\\d+)?)\\b', p3_text, re.IGNORECASE)
        if p3_items:
            for q_str, val_str in p3_items:
                q_num = int(q_str)
                if 1 <= q_num <= cls.MAX_P3:
                    try:
                        p3[q_num] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
                    except ValueError:
                        pass
        else:
            num_tokens = re.findall(r'-?\\d+(?:[.,]\\d+)?', p3_text)
            for idx, val_str in enumerate(num_tokens[:cls.MAX_P3], start=1):
                try:
                    p3[idx] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
                except ValueError:
                    pass

        return {"part1": p1, "part2": p2, "part3": p3}
'''
    final_content = "".join(lines[:start_idx]) + new_code
    with open("exam_manager.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(final_content)
    print("SUCCESS: Replaced from start_idx", start_idx)
else:
    print("ERROR: Start index not found")

target = """@app.post("/api/smart-paste-answer-key")
async def smart_paste_answer_key(payload: SmartPasteRequest):
    global pipeline
    if not payload.text or not payload.text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¡n ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n.")

async def smart_paste_answer_key(payload: SmartPasteRequest):
    global pipeline
    if not payload.text or not payload.text.strip():
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¡n ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n.")

    try:
        parsed_result = ExamManager.smart_parse_text_keys(payload.text, default_made=payload.default_made or "0001")
        parsed_keys = parsed_result.get("answer_keys", {})
        if not parsed_keys:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c cÃ¢u há»i hoáº·c Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n nÃ o. HÃ£y kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng dÃ¡n (VD: 1.A 2.B hoáº·c 1A 2B).")

        exam = get_current_exam()
        if not exam.get("exam_data"):
            exam["exam_data"] = {
                "exam_name": f"{exam['exam_info'].get('subject', 'Äá» thi')} - {exam['exam_info'].get('period', 'Kiá»ƒm tra')}",
                "subject": exam["exam_info"].get("subject", "Sinh há»c"),
                "period": exam["exam_info"].get("period", "Giá»¯a ká»³ I"),
                "config": parsed_result.get("config", {"part1_max": 18, "part2_max": 4, "part3_max": 6}),
                "answer_keys": {},
                "scoring_rules": {

        # Cáº­p nháº­t cÃ¡c mÃ£ Ä‘á» má»›i parsed vÃ o answer_keys
        exam["exam_data"]["answer_keys"].update(parsed_keys)"""

=== FILE: scratch\orig_align_sheet.py ===
# align_sheet_v2.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
MODULE Náº®N PHáº²NG PHá»I Cáº¢NH ÄA Táº¦NG OMR CHUáº¨N Bá»˜ GD&ÄT 2025 (PHIÃŠN Báº¢N CAMERA DI Äá»˜NG CHUYÃŠN NGHIá»†P)
TÃ­ch há»£p:
1. Bá»™ lá»c má»‘c vuÃ´ng tá»± thÃ­ch á»©ng (Adaptive 4-Corner OMR Quad Finder) cho Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i chá»¥p nghiÃªng, lá»‡ch gÃ³c.
2. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xoay 4 hÆ°á»›ng (0Â°, 90Â°, 180Â°, 270Â°) dá»±a trÃªn Ä‘áº·c tÃ­nh hÃ¬nh há»c Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng vÃ  phÃ¢n bá»‘ má»±c.
3. Táº§ng dá»± phÃ²ng nháº­n diá»‡n viá»n giáº¥y A4 (Document Boundary Contour Fallback).
4. Kháº¯c phá»¥c triá»‡t Ä‘á»ƒ lá»—i charmap UnicodeEncodeError trÃªn Windows .exe.
"""
from itertools import combinations
import os
from pathlib import Path
import sys
from typing import List, Optional, Tuple
import cv2
import numpy as np

# Báº£o vá»‡ luá»“ng Console stdout chá»‘ng lá»—i charmap trÃªn Windows .exe
if sys.platform == "win32":
    try:
        if sys.stdout is not None and hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
        if sys.stderr is not None and hasattr(sys.stderr, "reconfigure"):
            sys.stderr.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except Exception:
        pass

CANONICAL_WIDTH = 1769
CANONICAL_HEIGHT = 2526

# Tá»a Ä‘á»™ 5 cáº·p má»‘c chuáº©n trÃªn phÃ´i Master (1769 x 2526)
TPL_LEFT_5 = np.array([
    [56.7, 45.9],      # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn trÃ¡i
    [58.9, 819.2],     # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [58.9, 1371.1],    # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [55.6, 1723.7],    # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [55.6, 2449.0],    # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i trÃ¡i
], dtype=np.float32)

TPL_RIGHT_5 = np.array([
    [1720.9, 44.8],    # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn pháº£i
    [1720.9, 819.2],   # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [1721.5, 1371.1],  # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [1721.5, 1723.7],  # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [1721.5, 2449.0],  # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i pháº£i
], dtype=np.float32)

# Tá»· lá»‡ khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chuáº©n theo trá»¥c dá»c
EXPECTED_Y_RATIOS = (TPL_LEFT_5[:, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1]) / (TPL_LEFT_5[4, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1])


def rotate_cardinal(image: np.ndarray, angle: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Xoay ma tráº­n áº£nh chÃ­nh xÃ¡c theo cÃ¡c gÃ³c vuÃ´ng."""
    if angle == 90:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE)
    elif angle == 180:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_180)
    elif angle == 270:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE)
    return image


def detect_fiducial_squares(gray: np.ndarray) -> List[Tuple[float, float, float, float]]:
    """
    QuÃ©t tÃ¬m cÃ¡c á»©ng viÃªn má»‘c vuÃ´ng Ä‘en trÃªn toÃ n trang, ká»ƒ cáº£ khi chá»¥p camera cÃ³ ná»n bÃ n.
    Sá»­ dá»¥ng cv2.RETR_LIST Ä‘á»ƒ khÃ´ng bá»‹ máº¥t cÃ¡c má»‘c náº±m bÃªn trong viá»n giáº¥y.
    """
    h, w = gray.shape
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (3, 3), 0)
    
    # Káº¿t há»£p hai ngÆ°á»¡ng thÃ­ch á»©ng Ä‘á»ƒ báº¯t má»‘c trong má»i Ä‘iá»u kiá»‡n Ã¡nh sÃ¡ng
    thresh1 = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_GAUSSIAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 31, 7)
    thresh2 = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, 25, 6)
    thresh = cv2.bitwise_or(thresh1, thresh2)

    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (3, 3))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(thresh, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)
    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(closed, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)

    cands = []
    min_dim = min(h, w)
    min_side = max(6, int(round(min_dim * 0.005)))
    max_side = max(50, int(round(min_dim * 0.08)))

    for c in cnts:
        x, y, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if cw < min_side or ch < min_side or cw > max_side or ch > max_side:
            continue
        aspect = cw / float(ch)
        if 0.65 <= aspect <= 1.55:
            area = cv2.contourArea(c)
            extent = area / float(cw * ch)
            if extent > 0.45:
                cands.append((float(x + cw / 2.0), float(y + ch / 2.0), float(cw), float(ch)))
    return cands


def find_adaptive_4_corners(cands: List[Tuple[float, ...]], w: int, h: int) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """
    Äá»‹nh vá»‹ 4 má»‘c gÃ³c OMR (TL, TR, BR, BL) thÃ­ch á»©ng 100% vá»›i áº£nh chá»¥p Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i.
    KhÃ´ng phá»¥ thuá»™c vÃ o vá»‹ trÃ­ Ä‘áº·t giáº¥y trÃªn bÃ n hay khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch cáº§m mÃ¡y.
    """
    if len(cands) < 4:
        return None

    pts = np.array([[c[0], c[1]] for c in cands], dtype=np.float32)
    hull_idx = cv2.convexHull(pts, returnPoints=False)
    hull = pts[hull_idx.flatten()]
    if len(hull) < 4:
        return None

    # Tá»‘i Æ°u tá»‘c Ä‘á»™: giá»¯ láº¡i tá»‘i Ä‘a 18 Ä‘iá»ƒm cá»±c trá»‹ ngoÃ i cÃ¹ng
    if len(hull) > 18:
        ctr = np.mean(hull, axis=0)
        dists = np.linalg.norm(hull - ctr, axis=1)
        hull = hull[np.argsort(dists)[-18:]]

    best_quad = None
    best_score = -1.0

    for comb in combinations(range(len(hull)), 4):
        quad = hull[list(comb)]
        ctr = np.mean(quad, axis=0)
        angles = np.arctan2(quad[:, 1] - ctr[1], quad[:, 0] - ctr[0])
        quad = quad[np.argsort(angles)]

        # Sáº¯p xáº¿p theo thá»© tá»±: TL, TR, BR, BL
        tl_idx = min(range(4), key=lambda i: quad[i][0]**2 + quad[i][1]**2)
        quad = np.roll(quad, -tl_idx, axis=0)
        p_tl, p_tr, p_br, p_bl = quad

        # Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ­nh lá»“i vÃ  chiá»u khÃ´ng gian
        if p_tr[0] <= p_tl[0] or p_br[1] <= p_tr[1] or p_bl[1] <= p_tl[1] or p_br[0] <= p_bl[0]:
            continue

        w_top = np.linalg.norm(p_tr - p_tl)
        w_bot = np.linalg.norm(p_br - p_bl)
        h_l = np.linalg.norm(p_bl - p_tl)
        h_r = np.linalg.norm(p_br - p_tr)

        avg_w = (w_top + w_bot) / 2.0
        avg_h = (h_l + h_r) / 2.0
        if avg_w < 100 or avg_h < 150:
            continue

        ratio = avg_h / avg_w
        # Tá»· lá»‡ lÃ½ tÆ°á»Ÿng giá»¯a chiá»u cao vÃ  chiá»u rá»™ng cá»§a 4 má»‘c OMR lÃ  ~1.444 (cho phÃ©p gÃ³c nghiÃªng Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i 1.10 - 1.80)
        if not (1.10 <= ratio <= 1.80):
            continue
        if abs(w_top - w_bot) / avg_w > 0.38 or abs(h_l - h_r) / avg_h > 0.38:
            continue

        area = avg_w * avg_h
        score = area / (1.0 + abs(ratio - 1.444) * 6.0)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_quad = np.array([p_tl, p_tr, p_br, p_bl], dtype=np.float32)

    return best_quad


def auto_orient_sheet_robust(image_bgr: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, Optional[np.ndarray]]:
    """
    Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng phÃ¡t hiá»‡n hÆ°á»›ng chá»¥p vÃ  xoay Ä‘Ãºng chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng (0Â°).
    Kiá»ƒm tra cáº£ 4 gÃ³c vuÃ´ng (0Â°, 90Â°, 180Â°, 270Â°).
    """
    raw_h, raw_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    test_angles = [0, 90, 180, 270] if raw_h >= raw_w else [90, 270, 0, 180]

    for angle in test_angles:
        rotated = rotate_cardinal(image_bgr, angle)
        cur_gray = cv2.cvtColor(rotated, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        cur_h, cur_w = cur_gray.shape
        cands = detect_fiducial_squares(cur_gray)
        corners = find_adaptive_4_corners(cands, cur_w, cur_h)

        if corners is not None:
            # Kiá»ƒm tra chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng: pháº§n Ä‘áº§u Ä‘á» thi (chá»©a SBD & MÃ£ Ä‘á») luÃ´n cÃ³ nhiá»u má»±c Ä‘en hÆ¡n pháº§n chÃ¢n
            tl, tr, br, bl = corners
            min_y = int(min(tl[1], tr[1]))
            max_y = int(max(bl[1], br[1]))
            sheet_h = max_y - min_y
            if sheet_h > 100:
                top_part = cur_gray[min_y : min_y + int(sheet_h * 0.3), :]
                bot_part = cur_gray[max_y - int(sheet_h * 0.3) : max_y, :]
                top_ink = np.sum(top_part < 100)
                bot_ink = np.sum(bot_part < 100)
                if top_ink >= bot_ink * 0.70:
                    if angle != 0:
                        print(f"[OK] Phat hien anh xoay {angle} do -> Da xoay ve chieu dung (0 do).")
                    return rotated, corners

    return image_bgr, None


def detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[List[Tuple[float, ...]], List[Tuple[float, ...]], List[Tuple[float, float]]]:
    """HÃ m quÃ©t má»‘c pháº³ng nguyÃªn báº£n (dÃ nh cho báº£n scan mÃ¡y tÃ­nh)."""
    h, w = gray.shape
    expected_side = max(9, int(round(0.0183 * w)))

    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (3, 3), 0)
    block_size = max(15, (expected_side * 3) | 1)
    thresh = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(blur, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, block_size, 7)
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (3, 3))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(thresh, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, kernel)

    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(closed, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    min_side = expected_side * 0.50
    max_side = expected_side * 1.95

    left_cands, right_cands, all_cands = [], [], []
    for c in cnts:
        x, y, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if min_side <= cw <= max_side and min_side <= ch <= max_side:
            aspect = cw / float(ch)
            area = cv2.contourArea(c)
            extent = area / float(cw * ch)
            if 0.60 <= aspect <= 1.55 and extent > 0.45:
                cx = x + cw / 2.0
                cy = y + ch / 2.0
                all_cands.append((cx, cy))
                if cx < w * 0.30:
                    left_cands.append((cx, cy, float(cw), float(ch)))
                elif cx > w * 0.70:
                    right_cands.append((cx, cy, float(cw), float(ch)))

    left_cands.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    right_cands.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    return left_cands, right_cands, all_cands


def select_best_5(cands: List[Tuple[float, ...]], min_span: float) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Khá»›p tá»• há»£p 5 má»‘c theo tá»· lá»‡ khoáº£ng cÃ¡ch báº¥t Ä‘á»‘i xá»©ng chuáº©n."""
    if len(cands) < 5:
        return None
    best_pts = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    for comb in combinations(range(len(cands)), 5):
        pts = [cands[i] for i in comb]
        xs = np.array([p[0] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)
        ys = np.array([p[1] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)

        span_y = ys[-1] - ys[0]
        if span_y < min_span:
            continue

        ratios = (ys - ys[0]) / span_y
        ratio_err = float(np.max(np.abs(ratios - EXPECTED_Y_RATIOS)))
        if ratio_err > 0.052:
            continue

        poly = np.polyfit(ys, xs, 1)
        x_pred = np.polyval(poly, ys)
        x_err = float(np.max(np.abs(xs - x_pred)))

        score = ratio_err * 1000.0 + x_err * 2.0
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_pts = np.array([[p[0], p[1]] for p in pts], dtype=np.float32)

    return best_pts


def detect_a4_paper_contour(gray: np.ndarray) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
    """Táº§ng dá»± phÃ²ng 3: Nháº­n diá»‡n 4 gÃ³c cá»§a tá» giáº¥y A4 trÃªn ná»n bÃ n."""
    h, w = gray.shape
    blur = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (5, 5), 0)
    _, thresh = cv2.threshold(blur, 0, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY + cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(thresh, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    cnts = sorted(cnts, key=cv2.contourArea, reverse=True)

    min_area = w * h * 0.15
    for c in cnts[:5]:
        if cv2.contourArea(c) < min_area:
            break
        peri = cv2.arcLength(c, True)
        approx = cv2.approxPolyDP(c, 0.02 * peri, True)
        if len(approx) == 4:
            pts = approx.reshape(4, 2).astype(np.float32)
            ctr = np.mean(pts, axis=0)
            angles = np.arctan2(pts[:, 1] - ctr[1], pts[:, 0] - ctr[0])
            pts = pts[np.argsort(angles)]
            tl_idx = min(range(4), key=lambda i: pts[i][0]**2 + pts[i][1]**2)
            pts = np.roll(pts, -tl_idx, axis=0)
            return pts
    return None


def align_sheet_4band(image_path: Path, output_path: Path) -> np.ndarray:
    """Náº¯n pháº³ng phá»‘i cáº£nh Ä‘a táº§ng sau khi Ä‘Ã£ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chuáº©n hÃ³a chiá»u Ä‘á»©ng."""
    raw_bgr = cv2.imread(str(image_path))
    if raw_bgr is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y file: {image_path}")

    out_w, out_h = CANONICAL_WIDTH, CANONICAL_HEIGHT
    tpl_corners = np.array([
        TPL_LEFT_5[0], TPL_RIGHT_5[0],
        TPL_RIGHT_5[4], TPL_LEFT_5[4]
    ], dtype=np.float32)

    # 1. Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng xoay vÃ  tÃ¬m 4 má»‘c gÃ³c OMR thÃ­ch á»©ng vá»›i camera di Ä‘á»™ng
    image_bgr, corners_4 = auto_orient_sheet_robust(raw_bgr)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(image_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape

    # Táº¦NG 1: Náº®N 4 Dáº¢I KHI Äá»¦ 10 Má»C (DÃ€NH CHO Báº¢N SCAN PHáº²NG)
    lc, rc, _ = detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray)
    src_left = select_best_5(lc, min_span=h * 0.50)
    src_right = select_best_5(rc, min_span=h * 0.50)

    if src_left is not None and src_right is not None:
        canvas = np.zeros((out_h, out_w, 3), dtype=np.uint8)
        band_bounds = [
            (0, int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[1, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[1, 1])), int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[2, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[2, 1])), int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[3, 1]))),
            (int(round(TPL_LEFT_5[3, 1])), out_h),
        ]
        for b_idx in range(4):
            src_quad = np.array([
                src_left[b_idx], src_right[b_idx],
                src_right[b_idx + 1], src_left[b_idx + 1]
            ], dtype=np.float32)
            dst_quad = np.array([
                TPL_LEFT_5[b_idx], TPL_RIGHT_5[b_idx],
                TPL_RIGHT_5[b_idx + 1], TPL_LEFT_5[b_idx + 1]
            ], dtype=np.float32)
            H = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(src_quad, dst_quad)
            warped_band = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
            y0, y1 = band_bounds[b_idx]
            canvas[y0:y1, :] = warped_band[y0:y1, :]

        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas)
        return canvas

    # Táº¦NG 2: Náº®N 4 Má»C GÃ“C OMR THÃCH á»¨NG (DÃ€NH CHO CAMERA DI Äá»˜NG Cáº¦M TAY)
    if corners_4 is not None:
        H_corners = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(corners_4, tpl_corners)
        canvas = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H_corners, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas)
        return canvas

    # Táº¦NG 3: Dá»° PHÃ’NG VIá»€N GIáº¤Y A4 TRÃŠN Ná»€N BÃ€N
    paper_corners = detect_a4_paper_contour(gray)
    if paper_corners is not None:
        dst_a4 = np.array([
            [0, 0], [out_w, 0],
            [out_w, out_h], [0, out_h]
        ], dtype=np.float32)
        H_paper = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(paper_corners, dst_a4)
        canvas_a4 = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H_paper, (out_w, out_h), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
        output_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True, parents=True)
        cv2.imwrite(str(output_path), canvas_a4)
        return canvas_a4

    raise RuntimeError(
        "ALIGNMENT_FAILED: KhÃ´ng nháº­n diá»‡n Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»§ 4 má»‘c Ä‘á»‹nh vá»‹ cá»§a bÃ i thi. "
        "HÃ£y giá»¯ Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i hÆ°á»›ng tháº³ng vÃ o bÃ i thi vÃ  chá»¥p Ä‘á»§ 4 gÃ³c phiáº¿u."
    )

    f.write(proc.stdout)

# 2. Get align_sheet_v2.py from HEAD~1
proc = subprocess.run([git_cmd, "show", "HEAD~1:align_sheet_v2.py"], capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8")
align_code = proc.stdout

# Add the safeguard: only run select_best_5 if candidates <= 15 to prevent combination hang
target = """    lc, rc, _ = detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray)
    src_left = select_best_5(lc, min_span=h * 0.50)
    src_right = select_best_5(rc, min_span=h * 0.50)"""

replacement = """    lc, rc, _ = detect_fiducial_markers_legacy(gray)
    if len(lc) <= 15 and len(rc) <= 15:
        src_left = select_best_5(lc, min_span=h * 0.50)
        src_right = select_best_5(rc, min_span=h * 0.50)
    else:
        src_left, src_right = None, None"""

if target in align_code:
    align_code = align_code.replace(target, replacement, 1)
    print("Added safeguard against combinations hang to align_sheet_v2.py")
else:
    print("Target not found in align_code")

with open("align_sheet_v2.py", "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    f.write(align_code)
print("Restored align_sheet_v2.py from HEAD~1 with safeguard")

import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
import subprocess
import cv2

import sys, cv2, time
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np

if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

CANONICAL_WIDTH = 1769
CANONICAL_HEIGHT = 2526

TPL_LEFT_5 = np.array([
    [57.0, 47.0],      # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn trÃ¡i
    [59.0, 820.0],     # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [59.0, 1371.0],    # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [56.5, 1724.0],    # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [56.5, 2450.0],    # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i trÃ¡i
], dtype=np.float32)

TPL_RIGHT_5 = np.array([
    [1721.5, 46.0],    # M0: GÃ³c trÃªn pháº£i
    [1722.0, 820.0],   # M1: Äáº§u Pháº§n I
    [1722.0, 1371.0],  # M2: Äáº§u Pháº§n II
    [1722.0, 1724.0],  # M3: Äáº§u Pháº§n III
    [1722.0, 2449.5],  # M4: GÃ³c dÆ°á»›i pháº£i
], dtype=np.float32)

EXPECTED_Y_RATIOS = (TPL_LEFT_5[:, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1]) / (TPL_LEFT_5[4, 1] - TPL_LEFT_5[0, 1])

def rotate_cardinal(image: np.ndarray, angle: int) -> np.ndarray:
    if angle == 90:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE)
    elif angle == 180:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_180)
    elif angle == 270:
        return cv2.rotate(image, cv2.ROTATE_90_COUNTERCLOCKWISE)
    return image

def normalize_illumination(img_bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    CÃ¢n báº±ng sÃ¡ng pháº³ng (Flat-field illumination correction):
    Æ¯á»›c lÆ°á»£ng ná»n giáº¥y á»Ÿ Ä‘á»™ phÃ¢n giáº£i tháº¥p, lÃ m má»‹n rá»“i chia Ä‘á»u toÃ n áº£nh.
    Khá»­ sáº¡ch 100% bÃ³ng Ä‘á»• tay/Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i vÃ  nguá»“n sÃ¡ng bÃ n há»c khÃ´ng Ä‘á»u.
    """
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape
    sw, sh = max(8, w // 24), max(8, h // 24)
    small = cv2.resize(gray, (sw, sh), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    k = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (5, 5))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(small, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, k)
    blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(closed, (5, 5), 0)
    bg = cv2.resize(blurred, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
    
    b, g, r = cv2.split(img_bgr)
    b_norm = cv2.divide(b, bg, scale=240.0)
    g_norm = cv2.divide(g, bg, scale=240.0)
    r_norm = cv2.divide(r, bg, scale=240.0)
    return cv2.merge([b_norm, g_norm, r_norm])

def find_extreme_squares_fast(gray: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Äá»‹nh vá»‹ 4 má»‘c gÃ³c cá»±c biÃªn cá»±c nhanh (O(N)), khÃ´ng tá»• há»£p,
    sá»­ dá»¥ng máº·t náº¡ bao lá»“i giáº¥y A4 Ä‘á»ƒ loáº¡i bá» nhiá»…u mÃ©p bÃ n.
    """
    h, w = gray.shape
    block = max(15, min(61, round(min(w, h) / 10) | 1))
    binary = cv2.adaptiveThreshold(gray, 255, cv2.ADAPTIVE_THRESH_MEAN_C, cv2.THRESH_BINARY_INV, block, 16)
    
    # 1. Táº¡o máº·t náº¡ bao lá»“i vÃ¹ng giáº¥y A4 (khá»­ nhiá»…u ná»n bÃ n tá»‘i)
    paper_mask = np.zeros_like(gray)
    _, otsu = cv2.threshold(gray, 0, 255, cv2.THRESH_BINARY | cv2.THRESH_OTSU)
    k9 = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (9, 9))
    otsu_closed = cv2.morphologyEx(otsu, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, k9)
    cnts_p, _ = cv2.findContours(otsu_closed, cv2.RETR_EXTERNAL, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    if cnts_p:
        largest = max(cnts_p, key=cv2.contourArea)
        if cv2.contourArea(largest) > w * h * 0.20:
            hull_p = cv2.convexHull(largest)
            cv2.fillConvexPoly(paper_mask, hull_p, 255)
            binary = cv2.bitwise_and(binary, paper_mask)

    # 2. TÃ¬m táº¥t cáº£ cÃ¡c á»©ng viÃªn Ã´ vuÃ´ng Ä‘áº·c
    cnts, _ = cv2.findContours(binary, cv2.RETR_LIST, cv2.CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE)
    max_side = min(w, h) * 0.08
    min_side = max(5, int(round(min(w, h) * 0.005)))
    
    squares = []
    for c in cnts:
        x, y, cw, ch = cv2.boundingRect(c)
        if cw < min_side or ch < min_side or cw > max_side or ch > max_side:
            continue
        aspect = cw / float(ch)
        if not (0.65 <= aspect <= 1.55):
            continue
        cell = binary[y:y+ch, x:x+cw]
        pixels = cv2.countNonZero(cell)
        density = pixels / float(cw * ch)
        if pixels < 12 or density < 0.60:
            continue
        hull = cv2.convexHull(c)
        h_area = cv2.contourArea(hull)
        if h_area > 0 and (cv2.contourArea(c) / h_area) < 0.75:
            continue
        m = cv2.moments(c)
        if m['m00'] > 0:
            squares.append({'x': m['m10'] / m['m00'], 'y': m['m01'] / m['m00'], 'area': pixels, 'w': cw, 'h': ch})

    if len(squares) < 4:
        return None
    
    # 3. Gom cá»¥m kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c: má»‘c gÃ³c OMR lÃ  nhÃ³m Ã´ vuÃ´ng chuáº©n lá»›n nháº¥t
    areas = sorted([sq['area'] for sq in squares])
    ref = areas[int(len(areas) * 0.80)]
    kept = [sq for sq in squares if ref * 0.40 <= sq['area'] <= ref * 2.5]
    if len(kept) < 4:
        kept = squares

    # 4. Chiáº¿u 4 hÆ°á»›ng cá»±c biÃªn: O(N) hoÃ n toÃ n khÃ´ng láº·p tá»• há»£p
    tl = max(kept, key=lambda s: -(s['x'] + s['y']))
    br = max(kept, key=lambda s: (s['x'] + s['y']))
    tr = max(kept, key=lambda s: (s['x'] - s['y']))
    bl = max(kept, key=lambda s: (s['y'] - s['x']))

    pts = np.array([[tl['x'], tl['y']], [tr['x'], tr['y']], [br['x'], br['y']], [bl['x'], bl['y']]], dtype=np.float32)
    
    # Kiá»ƒm tra hÃ¬nh há»c quad
    w_top = np.linalg.norm(pts[1] - pts[0])
    w_bot = np.linalg.norm(pts[2] - pts[3])
    h_l = np.linalg.norm(pts[3] - pts[0])
    h_r = np.linalg.norm(pts[2] - pts[1])
    avg_w = (w_top + w_bot) / 2.0
    avg_h = (h_l + h_r) / 2.0
    if avg_w < 100 or avg_h < 150:
        return None
    ratio = avg_h / avg_w
    if not (1.05 <= ratio <= 1.85):
        return None
    return pts

def auto_orient_fast(image_bgr: np.ndarray):
    raw_h, raw_w = image_bgr.shape[:2]
    test_angles = [0, 180, 90, 270] if raw_h >= raw_w else [90, 270, 0, 180]
    
    for angle in test_angles:
        rotated = rotate_cardinal(image_bgr, angle)
        cur_gray = cv2.cvtColor(rotated, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
        corners = find_extreme_squares_fast(cur_gray)
        if corners is not None:
            tl, tr, br, bl = corners
            min_y = int(min(tl[1], tr[1]))
            max_y = int(max(bl[1], br[1]))
            sheet_h = max_y - min_y
            if sheet_h > 100:
                top_part = cur_gray[min_y : min_y + int(sheet_h * 0.3), :]
                bot_part = cur_gray[max_y - int(sheet_h * 0.3) : max_y, :]
                top_ink = np.sum(top_part < 100)
                bot_ink = np.sum(bot_part < 100)
                if top_ink >= bot_ink * 0.65:
                    return rotated, corners, angle
    return image_bgr, None, 0

print("Testing alignment engine...")
for name in ['bai_kiem_tra_01.jpg', 'bai_kiem_tra_02.jpg', 'bai_kiem_tra_03.jpg']:
    t0 = time.time()
    raw = cv2.imread(f'samples/{name}')
    oriented, corners, angle = auto_orient_fast(raw)
    elapsed = time.time() - t0
    print(f"{name}: angle={angle}deg, corners={corners is not None}, time={elapsed:.3f}s")

import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
import asyncio
import json

from app import smart_paste_answer_key, SmartPasteRequest, DB_DATA, get_current_exam

async def main():
    text = """
    MÃ£ Ä‘á»: 0504
    PHáº¦N I:
    1.A 2.B 3.C 4.D 5.A 6.B 7.C 8.D 9.A 10.B
    11.C 12.D 13.A 14.B 15.C 16.D 17.A 18.B
    PHáº¦N II:
    CÃ¢u 1: a) Ä, b) S, c) Ä, d) S
    CÃ¢u 2: a) S, b) Ä, c) S, d) Ä
    CÃ¢u 3: a) Ä, b) Ä, c) S, d) S
    CÃ¢u 4: a) S, b) S, c) Ä, d) Ä
    PHáº¦N III:
    CÃ¢u 1: -14
    CÃ¢u 2: 3,14
    CÃ¢u 3: 42
    """
    
    req = SmartPasteRequest(text=text, default_made="0504")
    print("Calling smart_paste_answer_key...", flush=True)
    res = await smart_paste_answer_key(req)
    print("Result:", flush=True)
    print(json.dumps(res, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), flush=True)
    assert res["status"] == "SUCCESS"
    assert "0504" in res["codes_loaded"]
    print(f"PASSED smart_paste_answer_key test! Re-graded {res.get('re_graded_count')} submissions.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    asyncio.run(main())

import sys, cv2, json
from pathlib import Path

import numpy as np

def normalize_illumination(img_bgr):
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(img_bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    h, w = gray.shape
    sw, sh = max(8, w // 24), max(8, h // 24)
    small = cv2.resize(gray, (sw, sh), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
    k = cv2.getStructuringElement(cv2.MORPH_RECT, (5, 5))
    closed = cv2.morphologyEx(small, cv2.MORPH_CLOSE, k)
    blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(closed, (5, 5), 0)
    bg = cv2.resize(blurred, (w, h), interpolation=cv2.INTER_LINEAR)
    
    b, g, r = cv2.split(img_bgr)
    b_norm = cv2.divide(b, bg, scale=240.0)
    g_norm = cv2.divide(g, bg, scale=240.0)
    r_norm = cv2.divide(r, bg, scale=240.0)
    return cv2.merge([b_norm, g_norm, r_norm])

import sys, cv2, json
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np

if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")

root_dir = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(root_dir))

from align_sheet_v2 import normalize_illumination

with open('output/exams_db.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    db = json.load(f)
exam_id = db.get('active_exam_id', 'default_exam')
exam_data = db['exams'][exam_id].get('exam_data', {})

with open('output/header_coordinates.json', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    header_data = json.load(f)
sbd_columns = header_data['sbd_columns']
made_columns = header_data['made_columns']

def eval_bubble_ink(ink_map: np.ndarray, cx: float, cy: float, r_core: int = 8) -> float:
    xi, yi = int(round(cx)), int(round(cy))
    h, w = ink_map.shape
    if xi - r_core < 0 or yi - r_core < 0 or xi + r_core >= w or yi + r_core >= h:
        return 0.0
    patch = ink_map[yi - r_core : yi + r_core + 1, xi - r_core : xi + r_core + 1]
    yy, xx = np.ogrid[-r_core : r_core + 1, -r_core : r_core + 1]
    mask = (xx * xx + yy * yy) <= (r_core * r_core)
    return float(np.mean(patch[mask]))

def decode_grid_robust(ink_map, cols, grid_name="SBD"):
    res = []
    anomalies = []
    FILL_HIGH = 24.0
    FILL_MIN = 20.0
    DOUBLE_THRESH = 22.0
    MARGIN = 7.0

    for col_idx, col in enumerate(cols, start=1):
        scores = [eval_bubble_ink(ink_map, col["digits"][str(d)][0], col["digits"][str(d)][1], r_core=8) for d in range(10)]
        ranked = sorted(enumerate(scores), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        top_d, top_s = ranked[0]
        sec_d, sec_s = ranked[1]

        # Kiá»ƒm tra tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp thá»±c sá»±: cáº£ 2 Ã´ Ä‘á»u pháº£i cÃ³ váº¿t chÃ¬ tháº­t (>= 22.0) vÃ  chÃªnh lá»‡ch nhá» (< 6.0)
        if top_s >= FILL_HIGH and sec_s >= DOUBLE_THRESH and (top_s - sec_s) < 6.0:
            res.append(f"[{top_d}{sec_d}]")
            anomalies.append({
                "type": f"{grid_name}_DOUBLE_FILL",
                "msg": f"{grid_name} Cá»™t {col_idx} tÃ´ Ä‘Ãºp ({top_d} vÃ  {sec_d})"
            })
        elif top_s >= FILL_MIN and (top_s - sec_s) >= MARGIN:
            res.append(str(top_d))
        elif top_s >= 30.0 and (top_s - sec_s) >= 8.0:
            res.append(str(top_d))
        else:
            # Cá»™t chÆ°a tÃ´ hoáº·c chÃ¬ quÃ¡ má» khÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘Æ°á»£c
            res.append("?")
    return "".join(res), anomalies

for fname in ['0bea7e29_aligned.png', '1b312875_aligned.png', '300ab73a_aligned.png']:
    img = cv2.imread('output/web_uploads/' + fname)
    norm = normalize_illumination(img)
    gray = cv2.cvtColor(norm, cv2.COLOR_BGR2GRAY)
    bg = cv2.GaussianBlur(gray, (0, 0), sigmaX=22.0, sigmaY=22.0, borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT)
    ink_map = bg.astype(np.float32) - gray.astype(np.float32)

    sbd, sbd_anom = decode_grid_robust(ink_map, sbd_columns, "SBD")
    made, made_anom = decode_grid_robust(ink_map, made_columns, "MADE")
    print(fname, f"-> SBD: '{sbd}' (anom={len(sbd_anom)}) | Made: '{made}' (anom={len(made_anom)})")

    db = json.load(f)

    TPL_LEFT_5[0], TPL_RIGHT_5[0],
    TPL_RIGHT_5[4], TPL_LEFT_5[4]
], dtype=np.float32)

test_files = [
    Path("samples/bai_kiem_tra_01.jpg"),
    Path("samples/bai_kiem_tra_02.jpg"),
    Path("samples/bai_kiem_tra_03.jpg"),
    Path("output/web_uploads/9e965acc_raw.png")
]

# Add more raw files from web_uploads
for p in Path("output/web_uploads").glob("*_raw.png"):
    if p not in test_files:
        test_files.append(p)
        if len(test_files) >= 8:
            break

print(f"Testing {len(test_files)} files with restored find_adaptive_4_corners...")
for p in test_files:
    if not p.exists():
        continue
    raw = cv2.imread(str(p))
    t0 = time.time()
    image_bgr, corners_4 = auto_orient_sheet_robust(raw)
    t1 = time.time()
    if corners_4 is None:
        print(f"âœ— FAILED corners on {p.name}")
        continue
    H = cv2.getPerspectiveTransform(corners_4, tpl_corners)
    aligned = cv2.warpPerspective(image_bgr, H, (CANONICAL_WIDTH, CANONICAL_HEIGHT), flags=cv2.INTER_CUBIC)
    t2 = time.time()
    
    # Grade!
    res = pipeline.process_sheet(aligned, p.name)
    t3 = time.time()
    print(f"âœ“ {p.name}: corners in {t1-t0:.3f}s, warp in {t2-t1:.3f}s, grade in {t3-t2:.3f}s | SBD={res['sbd']}, MÃ£ Ä‘á»={res['exam_code']}, Score={res['scores'].get('total')}, Anomalies={len(res['anomalies'])}")



=== FILE: scratch\test_smart_paste.py ===
import re, json, sys
if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
from typing import Dict, Any

def parse_smart_keys(text: str, default_made: str = "0001", num_p1: int = 40, num_p2: int = 8, num_p3: int = 6) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:
    raw = str(text or "").replace("\r", "\n")
    # TÃ¡ch theo tá»«ng mÃ£ Ä‘á» náº¿u cÃ³ nhiá»u mÃ£ Ä‘á» trong 1 láº§n dÃ¡n
    made_blocks = re.split(r'(?i)(?:^|\n)\s*(?:MÃƒ Äá»€|MÃƒ DE|MA DE|Äá»€|DE)\s*[:#\s]*(\d+)', raw)
    
    result = {}
    if len(made_blocks) > 1 and made_blocks[1].isdigit():
        i = 1
        while i < len(made_blocks):
            code = made_blocks[i].strip().zfill(4)
            content = made_blocks[i+1] if i+1 < len(made_blocks) else ""
            result[code] = _parse_single_key_block(content, num_p1, num_p2, num_p3)
            i += 2
    else:
        lines = [l.strip() for l in raw.strip().split("\n") if l.strip()]
        first_line = lines[0] if lines else ""
        code = default_made.zfill(4)
        body = raw
        if re.match(r'^\d{3,4}$', first_line):
            code = first_line.zfill(4)
            body = "\n".join(lines[1:])
        result[code] = _parse_single_key_block(body, num_p1, num_p2, num_p3)
        
    return result

def _parse_single_key_block(text: str, max_p1: int = 40, max_p2: int = 8, max_p3: int = 6) -> Dict[str, Any]:
    p1 = {}
    p2 = {}
    p3 = {}
    
    norm = text.upper().replace("Ã", "Ä")
    
    # 1. TÃ¬m vá»‹ trÃ­ phÃ¢n Ä‘oáº¡n náº¿u cÃ³ nhÃ£n rÃµ rÃ ng
    sec_regex = r'(?i)(?:^|\n)\s*(PHáº¦N\s*I|PHAN\s*1|TN\b|PHáº¦N\s*II|PHAN\s*2|DS\b|ÄS\b|PHáº¦N\s*III|PHAN\s*3|TLN\b)\s*[:\-\.]?'
    matches = list(re.finditer(sec_regex, norm))
    
    p1_text = ""
    p2_text = ""
    p3_text = ""
    
    if matches:
        for idx, m in enumerate(matches):
            label = m.group(1).upper()
            start = m.end()
            end = matches[idx+1].start() if idx+1 < len(matches) else len(norm)
            chunk = norm[start:end]
            if any(k in label for k in ["I", "1", "TN"]):
                p1_text += " " + chunk
            elif any(k in label for k in ["II", "2", "DS", "ÄS"]):
                p2_text += " " + chunk
            elif any(k in label for k in ["III", "3", "TLN"]):
                p3_text += " " + chunk
    else:
        # Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng nháº­n diá»‡n cáº¥u trÃºc dÃ²ng
        lines = [l.strip() for l in norm.split("\n") if l.strip()]
        for line in lines:
            if re.search(r'[ÄS]', line) and not re.search(r'[A-D]', line):
                p2_text += "\n" + line
            elif re.search(r'\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?', line) and not re.search(r'[A-DÄS]', line):
                p3_text += "\n" + line
            else:
                p1_text += "\n" + line

    # ==========================
    # PHáº¦N I (TRáº®C NGHIá»†M)
    # ==========================
    p1_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,])(\d{1,2})[\.:\)\-\s]*([ABCD])\b', p1_text)
    if p1_items:
        for q_str, ans in p1_items:
            q_num = int(q_str)
            if 1 <= q_num <= max_p1:
                p1[q_num] = ans
    else:
        tokens = re.findall(r'\b[ABCD]+\b', p1_text)
        flat_chars = "".join(tokens)
        for idx, char in enumerate(flat_chars[:max_p1], start=1):
            p1[idx] = char

    # ==========================
    # PHáº¦N II (ÄÃšNG / SAI)
    # ==========================
    p2_sub_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,])(\d{1,2})\s*([ABCDa-d])[\.:\)\-\s]*([ÄS])\b', p2_text)
    if p2_sub_items:
        for q_str, sub_key, val in p2_sub_items:
            q_num = int(q_str)
            if 1 <= q_num <= max_p2:
                if q_num not in p2:
                    p2[q_num] = {}
                p2[q_num][sub_key.lower()] = val
    else:
        for line in p2_text.split('\n'):
            line = line.strip()
            m = re.match(r'^(\d{1,2})[\.:\)\-\s]*([ÄS\s]{4,})', line)
            if m:
                q_num = int(m.group(1))
                chars = [c for c in m.group(2) if c in "ÄS"]
                if len(chars) == 4 and 1 <= q_num <= max_p2:
                    p2[q_num] = {"a": chars[0], "b": chars[1], "c": chars[2], "d": chars[3]}
        
        # Náº¿u chÆ°a cÃ³, láº¥y toÃ n bá»™ kÃ½ tá»± Ä, S vÃ  chia theo cá»¥m 4
        if not p2:
            all_ds = [c for c in p2_text if c in "ÄS"]
            num_rows = min(len(all_ds) // 4, max_p2)
            for r_idx in range(num_rows):
                chunk = all_ds[r_idx*4 : (r_idx+1)*4]
                p2[r_idx + 1] = {"a": chunk[0], "b": chunk[1], "c": chunk[2], "d": chunk[3]}

    # ==========================
    # PHáº¦N III (TRáº¢ Lá»œI NGáº®N)
    # ==========================
    p3_items = re.findall(r'(?:^|[\s;,])(\d{1,2})[:\)\.\s]+(-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?)\b', p3_text)
    if p3_items:
        for q_str, val_str in p3_items:
            q_num = int(q_str)
            if 1 <= q_num <= max_p3:
                try:
                    p3[q_num] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
                except ValueError:
                    pass
    else:
        num_tokens = re.findall(r'-?\d+(?:[.,]\d+)?', p3_text)
        for idx, val_str in enumerate(num_tokens[:max_p3], start=1):
            try:
                p3[idx] = float(val_str.replace(",", "."))
            except ValueError:
                pass

    return {"part1": p1, "part2": p2, "part3": p3}

# Test Cases
t1 = """
MÃ£ Ä‘á»: 0501
1A 2B 3C 4D 5A 6B 7C 8D 9A 10B 11C 12D 13A 14B 15C 16D
1: Ä S Ä S
2: S Ä S Ä
1: 2,5
2: -14
3: 0,75
"""
res1 = parse_smart_keys(t1)
print("Test 1 Result:", json.dumps(res1, ensure_ascii=False))

t2 = """
0504
TN: B C A A C D C C B A C C C D A B
DS: Ä Ä Ä Ä   Ä Ä Ä Ä
TLN: 214; 214; 1322
"""
res2 = parse_smart_keys(t2)
print("Test 2 Result:", json.dumps(res2, ensure_ascii=False))

        setTimeout(async () => {
          closeModal("smartPasteModal");
          await refreshSession();
        }, 1200);
      } catch (err) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 block";
        fb.innerText = `Lá»—i: ${err.message}`;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = `<span>Nháº­n Diá»‡n & Náº¡p ÄÃ¡p Ãn</span>`;
      }
    }

    function previewBubbleCrop(src, msg) {
      const modal = document.createElement("div");
      modal.className = "fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4";
      modal.onclick = () => modal.remove();
      modal.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 max-w-xs w-full shadow-2xl text-center" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
          <h3 class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800 uppercase mb-2">Chi Tiáº¿t Ã” Báº¥t ThÆ°á»ng</h3>
          <img src="${src}" class="w-44 h-44 mx-auto object-cover rounded-xl border-2 border-rose-400 shadow-md mb-2" />
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-700 font-bold mb-3">${msg}</p>
          <button onclick="this.closest('.fixed').remove()" class="px-4 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg shadow">ÄÃ³ng</button>
        </div>
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
    }"""

    html = html.replace(header_target, header_replacement, 1)
else:
    errs.append("header_target not found")

    html = html.replace(modal_target, modal_smart_paste, 1)
else:
    errs.append("modal_target not found")

    html = html.replace(anomaly_target, anomaly_replacement, 1)
else:
    errs.append("anomaly_target not found")

if js_target in html:
    html = html.replace(js_target, js_replacement, 1)
else:
    errs.append("js_target not found")

    print("ERRORS:", errs)

        f.write(html)

# 3. ThÃªm JS functions

replacement_js = """    function openSmartPasteMobile() {
      document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteText").value = "";
      const fb = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteFeedback");
      fb.className = "hidden";
      fb.innerText = "";
      document.getElementById("mobileSmartPasteModal").classList.remove("hidden");

    function closeSmartPasteMobile() {
      document.getElementById("mobileSmartPasteModal").classList.add("hidden");
    }

    async function submitSmartPasteMobile() {
      const text = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteText").value.trim();
      const defaultMade = document.getElementById("mobDefaultMade").value.trim() || "0001";
      const fb = document.getElementById("mobSmartPasteFeedback");

      btn.disabled = true;
