      fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-indigo-950 text-indigo-300 border border-indigo-800 block";
      fb.innerText = "Äang nháº­n diá»‡n cÃº phÃ¡p...";

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/smart-paste-answer-key", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text: text, default_made: defaultMade })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error(data.detail || "Lá»—i xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n");

        fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800 block";

        showNotificationToast(`ÄÃ£ náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n mÃ£ Ä‘á» ${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}!`, false);

        setTimeout(() => {
          closeSmartPasteMobile();
          syncSessionData();
        }, 1200);
      } catch (err) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2 rounded bg-rose-950 text-rose-300 border border-rose-800 block";
        fb.innerText = "Lá»—i: " + err.message;

        btn.innerText = "Náº¡p ÄÃ¡p Ãn";
      }

errs = []

    html = html.replace(target_btn, replacement_btn, 1)

    errs.append("target_btn not found")

    html = html.replace(target_modal, replacement_modal, 1)
else:
    errs.append("target_modal not found")

    html = html.replace(target_js, replacement_js, 1)
else:
    errs.append("target_js not found")

    print("ERRORS:", errs)

        f.write(html)

=== FILE: scratch\verify_restored.py ===
import sys
import os
sys.path.insert(0, os.path.abspath("."))
sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace')
from pathlib import Path
import cv2
import json

from align_sheet_v2 import align_sheet_4band
from full_grading_pipeline import FullGradingPipeline

# Load exam data
with open("output/exams_db.json", "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
    db = json.load(f)
exam_data = db["exams"]["exam_20261105_163133"].get("exam_data")
pipeline = FullGradingPipeline(exam_data=exam_data)

# Test aligning 9e965acc_raw.png
raw_p = Path("output/web_uploads/9e965acc_raw.png")
out_p = Path("scratch/re_aligned_9e.png")

aligned = align_sheet_4band(raw_p, out_p)
print("Aligned shape:", aligned.shape)

res = pipeline.process_sheet(aligned, "9e965acc_test.png")
print("=== RESTORED PIPELINE RESULT ===")
print("SBD:", res["sbd"], "MÃ£ Ä‘á»:", res["exam_code"])
print("Scores:", res["scores"])
print("Anomalies:", len(res["anomalies"]), [a["msg"] for a in res["anomalies"]])

assert res["exam_code"] == "0503", f"Expected 0503, got {res['exam_code']}"
assert res["scores"]["total"] == 4.0, f"Expected 4.0, got {res['scores']['total']}"
print("\n>>> VERIFICATION SUCCESS: Sheet graded accurately with Score 4.0 and MÃ£ Ä‘á» 0503! <<<")

          ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u bÃ i thi.
        </div>
      </div>
    </aside>

    <!-- Cá»˜T 2: Báº¢NG Äá»I SOÃT & ÄIá»€U CHá»ˆNH ÄIá»‚M -->
    <section class="col-span-12 md:col-span-5 lg:col-span-5 bg-white rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-200 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden">

    <!-- Cá»˜T 3: Báº¢N QUÃ‰T PHIáº¾U BÃ€I LÃ€M ÄÃƒ CHáº¤M -->
    <section class="col-span-12 md:col-span-4 lg:col-span-5 bg-slate-900 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-slate-800 flex flex-col h-full overflow-hidden">

            Xem áº¢nh Gá»‘c â†—
          </button>

      </div>

      <div id="sheetViewer" class="flex-1 overflow-auto p-3 flex justify-center items-start bg-slate-900">

        </div>
        <img id="gradedSheetImg" src="" alt="Báº£n quÃ©t phiáº¿u bÃ i lÃ m Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m" class="hidden transition-transform origin-top duration-150 rounded shadow-2xl max-w-full" />
      </div>

  <script>
    let currentSession = null;
    let currentSubmissions = [];
    let selectedSubId = null;
    let currentSheetZoom = 1.0;
    let isBatchCancelled = false;

    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", async () => {
      await refreshSession();

      const savedSubId = localStorage.getItem("omr_active_sub_id");
      if (savedSubId && currentSubmissions.some(s => s.submission_id === savedSubId)) {
        selectSubmission(savedSubId);
      } else if (currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
        selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[0].submission_id);
      }
    });

    async function refreshSession() {
      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        currentSession = await res.json();
        currentSubmissions = currentSession.submissions || [];

        const info = currentSession.exam_info;
        if (info) {
          document.getElementById("examHeaderTitle").innerText = `${info.subject.toUpperCase()} - ${info.period.toUpperCase()} (${info.class_name})`;
          document.getElementById("examHeaderSub").innerText = `NÄƒm há»c: ${info.school_year}`;

          const badge = document.getElementById("examStatusBadge");
          if (info.is_configured) {
            badge.className = "text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-bold bg-emerald-900 text-emerald-300";
            badge.innerText = "ÄÃ£ sáºµn sÃ ng cháº¥m";
          } else {
            badge.className = "text-[9px] px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-bold bg-amber-900 text-amber-300";
            badge.innerText = "ChÆ°a cáº¥u hÃ¬nh Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n";
          }
        }

        renderExamHistory(currentSession.all_exams || []);
        renderList();

        if (selectedSubId) {
          const stillExists = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === selectedSubId);
          if (stillExists) selectSubmission(selectedSubId);
          else {
            selectedSubId = null;
            clearReviewViews();
          }
        } else if (currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
          selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[0].submission_id);
        } else {
          clearReviewViews();
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("Lá»—i Ä‘á»“ng bá»™ dá»¯ liá»‡u phiÃªn:", e);
      }
    }

    function clearReviewViews() {
      document.getElementById("reviewContainer").innerHTML = `
        <div class="text-center text-slate-400 py-36 text-xs">
          ChÆ°a chá»n bÃ i thi.<br>Nháº¥n <b>+ Náº¡p BÃ i Thi</b> Ä‘á»ƒ báº¯t Ä‘áº§u.
        </div>`;
      const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
      const emptyNotice = document.getElementById("sheetEmptyNotice");
      sheetImg.classList.add("hidden");
      emptyNotice.classList.remove("hidden");
      localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
    }

    function openModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.remove("hidden"); }
    function closeModal(id) { document.getElementById(id).classList.add("hidden"); }

    function openSmartPasteModal() {
      document.getElementById("smartPasteText").value = "";
      const fb = document.getElementById("smartPasteFeedback");
      fb.className = "hidden";
      fb.innerText = "";
      openModal("smartPasteModal");
    }

    async function submitSmartPaste() {
      const text = document.getElementById("smartPasteText").value.trim();
      const defaultMade = document.getElementById("smartPasteDefaultMade").value.trim() || "0001";
      const fb = document.getElementById("smartPasteFeedback");
      const btn = document.getElementById("btnSubmitSmartPaste");

      if (!text) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 block";
        fb.innerText = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¡n ná»™i dung Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n.";
        return;
      }

      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerText = "Äang phÃ¢n tÃ­ch...";
      fb.className = "text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-indigo-50 text-indigo-700 border border-indigo-200 block";
      fb.innerText = "Äang nháº­n diá»‡n cÃº phÃ¡p vÃ  náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n...";

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/smart-paste-answer-key", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ text: text, default_made: defaultMade })
        });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (!res.ok) {
          throw new Error(data.detail || "Lá»—i náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n dÃ¡n");
        }

        fb.className = "text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-700 border border-emerald-200 block";
        fb.innerHTML = `<b>ThÃ nh cÃ´ng!</b> ÄÃ£ náº¡p mÃ£ Ä‘á»: <b>${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}</b>. ÄÃ£ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng cháº¥m láº¡i <b>${data.re_graded_count}</b> bÃ i thi.`;

        setTimeout(async () => {
          closeModal("smartPasteModal");
          await refreshSession();
        }, 1200);
      } catch (err) {
        fb.className = "text-xs p-2.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-50 text-rose-700 border border-rose-200 block";
        fb.innerText = `Lá»—i: ${err.message}`;
      } finally {
        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerHTML = `<span>Nháº­n Diá»‡n & Náº¡p ÄÃ¡p Ãn</span>`;
      }
    }

    function previewBubbleCrop(src, msg) {
      const modal = document.createElement("div");
      modal.className = "fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4";
      modal.onclick = () => modal.remove();
      modal.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-white rounded-2xl p-5 max-w-xs w-full shadow-2xl text-center" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">
          <h3 class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-800 uppercase mb-2">Chi Tiáº¿t Ã” Báº¥t ThÆ°á»ng</h3>
          <img src="${src}" class="w-44 h-44 mx-auto object-cover rounded-xl border-2 border-rose-400 shadow-md mb-2" />
          <p class="text-xs text-slate-700 font-bold mb-3">${msg}</p>
          <button onclick="this.closest('.fixed').remove()" class="px-4 py-1.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded-lg shadow">ÄÃ³ng</button>
        </div>
      `;
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
    }

    function renderExamHistory(allExams) {
      const container = document.getElementById("examHistoryList");
      if (!allExams || allExams.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center py-8 text-xs text-slate-400">ChÆ°a cÃ³ Ä‘á»£t kiá»ƒm tra nÃ o.</div>`;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = allExams.map(ex => `
        <div class="p-3 rounded-xl border transition flex justify-between items-center ${ex.is_active ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-500 ring-1 ring-indigo-500' : 'bg-white hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-200'}">
          <div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span class="font-bold text-xs text-slate-900">${ex.title}</span>
              ${ex.is_active ? `<span class="bg-indigo-600 text-white text-[9px] px-2 py-0.2 rounded-full font-bold">Hiá»‡n táº¡i</span>` : ''}
            </div>
            <div class="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              Khá»Ÿi táº¡o: <b>${ex.created_at || 'Máº·c Ä‘á»‹nh'}</b> | ÄÃ£ cháº¥m: <b class="text-emerald-700">${ex.submission_count} bÃ i</b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            ${!ex.is_active ? `
              <button onclick="switchExamSession('${ex.id}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-indigo-900 hover:bg-indigo-800 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-semibold shadow">
                Má»Ÿ Äá»£t
              </button>
            ` : ''}
            <button onclick="deleteExamSession('${ex.id}', '${ex.title}')" class="px-2.5 py-1 bg-rose-100 hover:bg-rose-200 text-rose-700 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold transition" title="XÃ³a Ä‘á»£t kiá»ƒm tra nÃ y">
              XÃ³a
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      `).join("");
    }

    async function deleteExamSession(examId, examTitle) {
      if (!confirm(`XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a Ä‘á»£t kiá»ƒm tra: "${examTitle}"?\nDá»¯ liá»‡u bÃ i lÃ m trong Ä‘á»£t nÃ y sáº½ bá»‹ xÃ³a khá»i há»‡ thá»‘ng.`)) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/delete-exam/${examId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        if (res.ok) {
          selectedSubId = null;
          localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          await refreshSession();
        } else {
          const err = await res.json();
          alert("Lá»—i: " + (err.detail || "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a Ä‘á»£t nÃ y"));
        }
      } catch (e) {
        alert("Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i: " + e.message);
      }
    }

    async function switchExamSession(examId) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/switch-exam/${examId}`, { method: "POST" });
        if (res.ok) {
          closeModal("examHistoryModal");
          selectedSubId = null;
          localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          await refreshSession();
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lá»—i chuyá»ƒn Ä‘á»£t kiá»ƒm tra: " + err.message);
      }
    }

    async function submitCreateExam() {
      const payload = {
        subject: document.getElementById("inpSubject").value,
        period: document.getElementById("inpPeriod").value,
        class_name: document.getElementById("inpClass").value,
        school_year: document.getElementById("inpYear").value
      };
      await fetch("/api/create-exam", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
      });
      closeModal("createExamModal");
      selectedSubId = null;
      localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
      await refreshSession();
    }

    document.getElementById("keyPicker").addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", file);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/upload-answer-key", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        const data = await res.json();
        alert(`ÄÃ£ náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n thÃ nh cÃ´ng cho cÃ¡c mÃ£ Ä‘á»: ${data.codes_loaded.join(", ")}.`);
        await refreshSession();
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lá»—i náº¡p file Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: " + err.message);
      }
    });

    // CHáº¤M HÃ€NG LOáº T TUáº¦N Tá»°
    document.getElementById("filePicker").addEventListener("change", async (e) => {
      const files = Array.from(e.target.files);
      if (!files.length) return;

      e.target.value = "";

      const progressContainer = document.getElementById("batchProgressContainer");
      const progressBar = document.getElementById("batchProgressBar");
      const progressText = document.getElementById("batchProgressText");
      const currentFileText = document.getElementById("batchCurrentFile");

      progressContainer.classList.remove("hidden");
      isBatchCancelled = false;

      const totalFiles = files.length;
      let processedFiles = 0;
      let successCount = 0;
      let errorCount = 0;

      for (let i = 0; i < totalFiles; i++) {
        if (isBatchCancelled) {
          alert(`ÄÃ£ dá»«ng tiáº¿n trÃ¬nh cháº¥m hÃ ng loáº¡t. ÄÃ£ xá»­ lÃ½ ${processedFiles}/${totalFiles} tá»‡p.`);
          break;
        }

        const file = files[i];
        currentFileText.innerText = `Äang xá»­ lÃ½ (${i + 1}/${totalFiles}): ${file.name}`;

        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append("file", file);

        try {
          const res = await fetch("/api/grade-single-file", {
            method: "POST",
            body: fd
          });

          if (!res.ok) {
            const err = await res.json();
            throw new Error(err.detail || `HTTP Error: ${res.status}`);
          }

          const data = await res.json();
          if (data.new_submissions && data.new_submissions.length > 0) {
            for (let newSub of data.new_submissions) {
              const existingIdx = currentSubmissions.findIndex(s => s.submission_id === newSub.submission_id);
              if (existingIdx >= 0) {
                currentSubmissions[existingIdx] = newSub;
              } else {
                currentSubmissions.push(newSub);
              }
              if (newSub.has_error) errorCount++;
              else successCount++;
            }

            renderList();

            if (!selectedSubId && currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
              selectSubmission(currentSubmissions[currentSubmissions.length - 1].submission_id);
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.error(`Lá»—i cháº¥m file ${file.name}:`, err);
          errorCount++;
        }

        processedFiles++;
        const percent = Math.round((processedFiles / totalFiles) * 100);
        progressBar.style.width = `${percent}%`;
        progressText.innerText = `Tiáº¿n Ä‘á»™: ${processedFiles}/${totalFiles} (${percent}%)`;
      }

      setTimeout(() => {
        progressContainer.classList.add("hidden");
        progressBar.style.width = "0%";
        if (!isBatchCancelled) {
          alert(`ÄÃ£ hoÃ n táº¥t cháº¥m hÃ ng loáº¡t!\n- ThÃ nh cÃ´ng: ${successCount} bÃ i\n- Lá»—i nháº­n diá»‡n: ${errorCount} bÃ i`);
        }
        refreshSession();
      }, 800);
    });

    function cancelBatchGrading() {
      isBatchCancelled = true;
    }

    function renderList() {
      const el = document.getElementById("submissionList");
      document.getElementById("badgeCount").innerText = `${currentSubmissions.length} bÃ i`;

      if (currentSubmissions.length === 0) {
        el.innerHTML = `<div class="text-center text-slate-400 py-24 text-[11px]">ChÆ°a cÃ³ dá»¯ liá»‡u bÃ i thi.</div>`;
        return;
      }

      el.innerHTML = currentSubmissions.map(sub => {
        const isSelected = sub.submission_id === selectedSubId;
        const hasWarning = sub.anomalies && sub.anomalies.length > 0;
        const hasErr = sub.has_error;

        return `
          <div onclick="selectSubmission('${sub.submission_id}')"
               class="group relative p-2 rounded-lg cursor-pointer transition border text-left ${isSelected ? 'bg-indigo-50 border-indigo-500 shadow-sm' : 'hover:bg-slate-50 border-slate-200'}">
            <div class="flex justify-between items-start">
              <span class="font-bold text-[11px] text-slate-800 truncate max-w-[120px]" title="${sub.file_name}">${sub.file_name}</span>
              <span class="font-bold text-[11px] ${hasErr ? 'text-rose-600' : (hasWarning ? 'text-amber-600' : 'text-emerald-600')}">
                ${hasErr ? 'Lá»–I' : (sub.scores ? sub.scores.total + ' Ä‘' : '0 Ä‘')}
              </span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex justify-between items-center text-[10px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              <span>SBD: <b>${sub.sbd}</b> | Äá»: <b>${sub.exam_code}</b></span>
              ${hasErr ? `<span class="bg-rose-100 text-rose-800 px-1 py-0.2 rounded font-semibold text-[8px]">Lá»—i</span>` :
                (hasWarning ? `<span class="bg-amber-100 text-amber-800 px-1 py-0.2 rounded font-semibold text-[8px]">${sub.anomalies.length} cá»</span>` : `<span class="text-emerald-600 font-semibold text-[8px]">Há»£p lá»‡</span>`)}
            </div>
            <button onclick="event.stopPropagation(); deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}');"
                    class="opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 absolute top-1 right-1 bg-white hover:bg-rose-100 text-rose-600 p-1 rounded border border-slate-200 shadow-sm transition"
                    title="XÃ³a bÃ i thi nÃ y">
              <svg class="w-3 h-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" stroke-width="2" d="M19 7l-.867 12.142A2 2 0 0116.138 21H7.862a2 2 0 01-1.995-1.858L5 7m5 4v6m4-6v6m1-10V4a1 1 0 00-1-1h-4a1 1 0 00-1 1v3M4 7h16"></path></svg>
            </button>
          </div>`;
      }).join("");
    }

    function selectSubmission(subId) {
      selectedSubId = subId;
      localStorage.setItem("omr_active_sub_id", subId);
      renderList();
      const sub = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === subId);
      if (!sub) return;

      const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
      const emptyNotice = document.getElementById("sheetEmptyNotice");
      const versionTag = sub.updated_at ? `?v=${encodeURIComponent(sub.updated_at)}` : '';
      sheetImg.src = `/api/graded-image/${sub.submission_id}${versionTag}`;
      sheetImg.classList.remove("hidden");
      emptyNotice.classList.add("hidden");
      resetZoomSheet();

      const container = document.getElementById("reviewContainer");
      if (sub.has_error) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="p-6 bg-rose-50 border border-rose-200 rounded-xl text-center">
            <h3 class="text-sm font-bold text-rose-800 mb-1">KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ nháº­n diá»‡n bÃ i lÃ m</h3>
            <p class="text-xs text-rose-600 mb-4">${sub.error_msg || 'áº¢nh bÃ i thi bá»‹ máº¥t gÃ³c má»‘c Ä‘á»‹nh vá»‹.'}</p>
            <button onclick="deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}')" class="px-3.5 py-1.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-700 text-white rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">
              XÃ³a Báº£n Ghi NÃ y
            </button>
          </div>`;
        return;
      }

      const p1 = sub.details?.part1 || {};
      const p2 = sub.details?.part2 || {};
      const p3 = sub.details?.part3 || {};

      container.innerHTML = `
        <div class="flex justify-between items-center border-b pb-2.5 mb-3">
          <div>
            <h3 class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate max-w-[200px]">${sub.file_name}</h3>
            <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400">MÃ£ bÃ i: ${sub.submission_id}</p>
          </div>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
            <div class="text-right mr-1">
              <span class="text-[9px] text-slate-400 block font-bold leading-tight">Tá»”NG ÄIá»‚M</span>
              <span class="text-xl font-black text-rose-600" id="liveTotal">${sub.scores ? sub.scores.total : 0} <span class="text-[10px] font-normal text-slate-400">/ 10</span></span>
            </div>
            <button onclick="triggerInstantRegrade('${sub.submission_id}')" class="bg-indigo-900 hover:bg-indigo-800 text-white font-semibold text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg shadow">
              Cáº­p Nháº­t Káº¿t Quáº£
            </button>
            <button onclick="deleteSubmission('${sub.submission_id}', '${sub.file_name}')" class="bg-rose-100 hover:bg-rose-200 text-rose-700 font-bold text-xs px-2.5 py-1.5 rounded-lg transition" title="XÃ³a bÃ i thi nÃ y">
              XÃ³a
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        ${sub.anomalies && sub.anomalies.length > 0 ? `
          <div class="mb-3 bg-amber-50 border border-amber-300 rounded-xl p-2.5 shadow-xs">
            <h4 class="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-900 uppercase mb-1.5 flex items-center gap-1">
              <span>ðŸ” LÆ°u Ã½ Ä‘á»‘i soÃ¡t & Ã´ báº¥t thÆ°á»ng (${sub.anomalies.length}):</span>
            </h4>
            <div class="space-y-1.5">
              ${sub.anomalies.map(a => `
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5 bg-white p-2 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 shadow-xs">
                  ${a.crop_b64 ? `
                    <div class="shrink-0 relative group">
                      <img src="${a.crop_b64}" class="w-12 h-12 object-cover rounded-md border border-rose-300 shadow-xs cursor-pointer hover:border-rose-600 transition" title="Nháº¥p Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ³ng to" onclick="previewBubbleCrop('${a.crop_b64}', '${(a.msg || '').replace(/'/g, "\\'")}')" />
                      <span class="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-rose-600 text-white text-[7px] px-0.5 font-bold rounded-tl">Zoom</span>
                    </div>
                  ` : ''}
                  <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                    <p class="text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-950">${a.msg}</p>
                    <p class="text-[9px] text-slate-500 font-mono">${a.type}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              `).join("")}
            </div>
          </div>` : ""}

        <div class="mb-3 bg-slate-50 border rounded-lg p-2">
          <label class="block text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-700 uppercase mb-1">
            ThÃ´ng tin thÃ­ sinh (Äá»‘i soÃ¡t viáº¿t tay)
          </label>
          <img src="/api/crop-info/${sub.submission_id}?t=${Date.now()}" 
               class="w-full max-h-40 object-contain rounded border border-slate-300 bg-white shadow-xs mb-2 transition-all hover:scale-105 origin-top" 
               alt="ThÃ´ng tin viáº¿t tay thÃ­ sinh" />
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>

              <input type="text" id="editSbd" value="${sub.sbd}" class="w-full border rounded px-2 py-1 text-xs font-mono font-bold text-indigo-950 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-indigo-600 outline-none" />
            </div>

                </button>

            </div>

                </div>`).join("")}

    async function deleteSubmission(subId, fileName) {
      if (!confirm(`XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a bÃ i thi: "${fileName}" khá»i Ä‘á»£t cháº¥m?`)) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/delete-submission/${subId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
        if (res.ok) {
          if (selectedSubId === subId) {
            selectedSubId = null;
            localStorage.removeItem("omr_active_sub_id");
          }
          await refreshSession();
        } else {
          alert("Lá»—i: KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a bÃ i thi nÃ y.");
        }
      } catch (e) {
        alert("Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i: " + e.message);
      }
    }

    async function triggerInstantRegrade(subId) {
      const sub = currentSubmissions.find(s => s.submission_id === subId);
      if (!sub) return;

      const editMadeInput = document.getElementById("editMade");
      const editSbdInput = document.getElementById("editSbd");

      const newExamCode = editMadeInput ? editMadeInput.value.trim().padStart(4, '0') : sub.exam_code;
      const newSbd = editSbdInput ? editSbdInput.value.trim() : sub.sbd;

      const p1Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part1 || {})) {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`p1_q${q}`);
        p1Data[q] = inp ? inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() : sub.details.part1[q];
      }

      const p2Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part2 || {})) {
        p2Data[q] = {};
        for (let k of ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd']) {
          const inp = document.getElementById(`p2_q${q}_${k}`);
          p2Data[q][k] = inp ? inp.value.trim().toUpperCase() : (sub.details.part2[q] ? sub.details.part2[q][k] : "");
        }
      }

      const p3Data = {};
      for (let q in (sub.details?.part3 || {})) {
        const inp = document.getElementById(`p3_q${q}`);
        p3Data[q] = (inp && inp.value !== "") ? parseFloat(inp.value) : (sub.details.part3[q] ? sub.details.part3[q].normalized_value : null);
      }

      const payload = {
        submission_id: subId,
        sbd: newSbd,
        exam_code: newExamCode,
        part1: p1Data,
        part2: p2Data,
        part3: p3Data
      };

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/update-submission", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });

        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("MÃ¡y chá»§ pháº£n há»“i lá»—i khi cáº­p nháº­t.");

        const data = await res.json();
        const updated = data.updated_submission;

        const idx = currentSubmissions.findIndex(s => s.submission_id === subId);
        if (idx >= 0) {
          currentSubmissions[idx] = updated;
        }

        renderList();
        selectSubmission(subId);

        const sheetImg = document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg");
        if (sheetImg) {
          sheetImg.src = `/api/graded-image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Lá»—i khi cháº¥m láº¡i Ä‘iá»ƒm: " + err.message);
      }
    }

    function zoomSheet(delta) {
      currentSheetZoom = Math.max(0.5, Math.min(2.5, currentSheetZoom + delta));
      document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg").style.transform = `scale(${currentSheetZoom})`;
    }
    function resetZoomSheet() {
      currentSheetZoom = 1.0;
      document.getElementById("gradedSheetImg").style.transform = "scale(1.0)";
    }
    function openGradedTab() {
      if (selectedSubId) {
        window.open(`/api/graded-image/${selectedSubId}`, "_blank");
      }
    }

    function downloadTemplate() { window.location.href = "/api/download-template"; }

    // =========================================================================
    // Há»˜P THOáº I CHá»ŒN NÆ I LÆ¯U (SAVE AS DIALOG) TÃCH Há»¢P CHO PDF VÃ€ EXCEL
    // =========================================================================
    async function saveFileWithPicker(fileType) {
      const isPdf = fileType === 'pdf';
      const btn = document.getElementById(isPdf ? "btnExportPdf" : "btnExportExcel");
      const oldHtml = btn.innerHTML;
      btn.innerHTML = `<span class="inline-block animate-spin mr-1">â³</span> Äang táº¡o...`;
      btn.disabled = true;

      const endpoint = isPdf ? "/api/export-graded-pdf" : "/api/export-excel";
      const mimeType = isPdf 
        ? "application/pdf" 
        : "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet";
      const ext = isPdf ? ".pdf" : ".xlsx";
      const desc = isPdf ? "Táº­p phiáº¿u Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m PDF" : "Báº£ng Ä‘iá»ƒm Excel";

      try {
        const res = await fetch(endpoint);
        if (!res.ok) {
          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({ detail: "MÃ£ lá»—i HTTP: " + res.status }));
          throw new Error(errData.detail || "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xuáº¥t tá»‡p dá»¯ liá»‡u.");
        }

        // Láº¥y tÃªn tá»‡p gá»£i Ã½ tá»« header Content-Disposition
        let suggestedName = isPdf ? `TAP_PHIEU_DA_CHAM_${Date.now()}.pdf` : `BANG_DIEM_${Date.now()}.xlsx`;
        const disposition = res.headers.get("content-disposition");
        if (disposition && disposition.indexOf("filename=") !== -1) {
          const matches = /filename[^;=\n]*=((['"]).*?\2|[^;\n]*)/.exec(disposition);
          if (matches != null && matches[1]) {
            suggestedName = matches[1].replace(/['"]/g, '');
          }
        }

        const blob = await res.blob();

        // 1. Náº¾U TRÃŒNH DUYá»†T Há»– TRá»¢ FILE SYSTEM ACCESS API: Má»ž Cá»¬A Sá»” CHá»ŒN NÆ I LÆ¯U (SAVE AS)
        if ('showSaveFilePicker' in window) {
          try {
            const handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({
              suggestedName: suggestedName,
              types: [{
                description: desc,
                accept: { [mimeType]: [ext] }
              }]
            });
            const writable = await handle.createWritable();
            await writable.write(blob);
            await writable.close();
            alert(`[âœ“] ÄÃ£ lÆ°u tá»‡p thÃ nh cÃ´ng vÃ o vá»‹ trÃ­ báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ chá»n!`);
            return;
          } catch (pickerErr) {
            // Náº¿u ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng chá»§ Ä‘á»™ng nháº¥n 'Cancel' trÃªn cá»­a sá»• Save As
            if (pickerErr.name === 'AbortError') {
              return;
            }
            console.warn("showSaveFilePicker tháº¥t báº¡i, chuyá»ƒn sang cháº¿ Ä‘á»™ táº£i trá»±c tiáº¿p:", pickerErr);
          }
        }

        // 2. PHÆ¯Æ NG ÃN Dá»° PHÃ’NG AN TOÃ€N (FALLBACK)
        const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        const a = document.createElement("a");
        a.style.display = "none";
        a.href = url;
        a.download = suggestedName;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);
        window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);

      } catch (err) {
        alert("ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o xuáº¥t tá»‡p: " + err.message);
      } finally {
        btn.innerHTML = oldHtml;
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }

    // SMART AUTO-POLLING Tá»ª THIáº¾T Bá»Š DI Äá»˜NG (DUY NHáº¤T 1 VÃ’NG Láº¶P)
    setInterval(async () => {
      const activeEl = document.activeElement;
      if (activeEl && (activeEl.tagName === "INPUT" || activeEl.tagName === "TEXTAREA")) {
        return;
      }

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        if (!res.ok) return;
        const data = await res.json();
        const serverSubs = data.submissions || [];

        if (serverSubs.length !== currentSubmissions.length) {
          const isNewAdded = serverSubs.length > currentSubmissions.length;
          currentSubmissions = serverSubs;
          renderList();

          if (isNewAdded && currentSubmissions.length > 0) {
            const newestSub = currentSubmissions[currentSubmissions.length - 1];
            selectSubmission(newestSub.submission_id);

            const badge = document.getElementById("badgeCount");
            badge.className = "text-[10px] bg-emerald-500 text-white font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full animate-bounce";
            setTimeout(() => {
              badge.className = "text-[10px] bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-800 font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full";
            }, 1800);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        // Bá» qua lá»—i ngáº¯t quÃ£ng máº¡ng
      }
    }, 2000);
  </script>
</body>
</html>

  <script>
    // =========================================================================
    // KHá»žI Táº O BIáº¾N TRáº NG THÃI TOÃ€N Cá»¤C
    // =========================================================================
    let currentStream = null;
    let videoTrack = null;
    let facingMode = "environment";
    let isGrading = false;
    let opticalInterval = null;
    let lock4Frames = 0;
    let deferredPrompt = null;
    let latestSubId = null;
    let isTorchOn = false;
    let toastTimer = null;
    let sessionRetryTimer = null;

    let sessionData = {
      active_exam_id: null,
      exam_info: null,
      exam_data: null,
      submissions: [],
      all_exams: []
    };

    const RETICLES = [
      { id: "reticle-tl", name: "TrÃªn-TrÃ¡i", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-tr", name: "TrÃªn-Pháº£i", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-bl", name: "DÆ°á»›i-TrÃ¡i", matched: false },
      { id: "reticle-br", name: "DÆ°á»›i-Pháº£i", matched: false }
    ];

    // PhÃ¡t Ã¢m thanh pháº£n há»“i báº±ng Web Audio API
    function playBeep(isSuccess = true) {
      try {
        const AudioContext = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;
        if (!AudioContext) return;
        const ctx = new AudioContext();
        const osc = ctx.createOscillator();
        const gain = ctx.createGain();
        osc.connect(gain);
        gain.connect(ctx.destination);
        
        if (isSuccess) {
          osc.type = "sine";
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, ctx.currentTime); // D5
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(880, ctx.currentTime + 0.08); // A5
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.25, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
          osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
          osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.25);
        } else {
          osc.type = "triangle";
          osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(220, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.2, ctx.currentTime);
          gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.01, ctx.currentTime + 0.2);
          osc.start(ctx.currentTime);
          osc.stop(ctx.currentTime + 0.2);
        }
      } catch (e) {}
    }

    // Hiá»ƒn thá»‹ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o Toast khÃ´ng gÃ¢y Ä‘Æ¡ camera
    function showNotificationToast(msg, isError = true) {
      const toast = document.getElementById("floatingToast");
      const content = document.getElementById("floatingToastContent");
      const icon = document.getElementById("toastIcon");
      const text = document.getElementById("toastMsg");

      if (isError) {
        content.className = "bg-rose-950/95 border border-rose-500 text-rose-200 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 max-w-sm text-center";
        icon.innerText = "âš ï¸";
      } else {
        content.className = "bg-emerald-950/95 border border-emerald-500 text-emerald-200 px-4 py-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl backdrop-blur text-xs font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 max-w-sm text-center";
        icon.innerText = "âœ“";
      }
      text.innerText = msg;

      toast.classList.remove("-translate-y-12", "opacity-0");
      toast.classList.add("translate-y-0", "opacity-100");

      if (toastTimer) clearTimeout(toastTimer);
      toastTimer = setTimeout(() => {
        toast.classList.remove("translate-y-0", "opacity-100");
        toast.classList.add("-translate-y-12", "opacity-0");
      }, 3500);
    }

      }

    // =========================================================================
    // KHá»žI Äá»˜NG á»¨NG Dá»¤NG (Báº¬T CAMERA NGAY Láº¬P Tá»¨C TRONG 200MS, KHÃ”NG CHá»œ SERVER)

    window.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", () => {
      // 1. Má»Ÿ Camera tá»©c thÃ¬, khÃ´ng bá»‹ cháº·n bá»Ÿi máº¡ng
      startCamera();

      // 2. Táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u Ä‘á»£t thi ngáº§m vá»›i cÆ¡ cháº¿ tá»± Ä‘á»™ng thá»­ láº¡i khi mÃ¡y chá»§ Render khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng
      fetchSessionWithRetry();

      // 3. Khá»Ÿi Ä‘á»™ng vÃ²ng láº·p kiá»ƒm tra má»‘c quang há»c 90ms/chu ká»³
      opticalInterval = setInterval(runOpticalMarkerDetection, 90);

      // Cháº¡m mÃ n hÃ¬nh Ä‘á»ƒ báº­t video náº¿u bá»‹ cháº·n autoplay
      document.body.addEventListener("click", () => {
        const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
        if (video && video.paused) {
          video.play().catch(e => {});
        }
      }, { once: true });
    });

    // =========================================================================
    // ÄIá»€U HÆ¯á»šNG TABS
    // =========================================================================
    function switchTab(tabId) {
      document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(el => el.classList.remove("active"));
      document.getElementById(tabId).classList.add("active");

      const navs = {
        tabScanner: document.getElementById("navScanner"),
        tabResults: document.getElementById("navResults"),
        tabExam: document.getElementById("navExam")
      };

      Object.keys(navs).forEach(k => {
        if (k === tabId) {
          navs[k].className = "flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 text-emerald-400 font-bold text-[10px]";
        } else {
          navs[k].className = "flex flex-col items-center gap-0.5 text-slate-400 font-semibold text-[10px]";
        }
      });

      if (tabId === "tabScanner") {
        if (!currentStream) startCamera();
        if (!opticalInterval) opticalInterval = setInterval(runOpticalMarkerDetection, 90);
      } else {
        if (opticalInterval) { clearInterval(opticalInterval); opticalInterval = null; }
        if (tabId === "tabResults") renderSubmissionsList();
        if (tabId === "tabExam") renderExamsList();
      }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Äá»’NG Bá»˜ Dá»® LIá»†U PHIÃŠN CHáº¤M Vá»šI CÆ  CHáº¾ AUTO-RETRY KHI SERVER COLD-START
    // =========================================================================
    async function fetchSessionWithRetry() {
      const dot = document.getElementById("serverStatusDot");
      const statusText = document.getElementById("serverStatusText");

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/session");
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error("HTTP " + res.status);
        
        sessionData = await res.json();
        
        const count = sessionData.submissions ? sessionData.submissions.length : 0;
        document.getElementById("badgeSubCount").innerText = `${count} bÃ i`;

        if (sessionData.exam_info) {
          const title = `${sessionData.exam_info.subject} - Lá»šP ${sessionData.exam_info.class_name}`;
          document.getElementById("headerExamTitle").innerText = title;
          document.getElementById("headerExamSubtitle").innerText = `Há»c ká»³: ${sessionData.exam_info.period}`;

          document.getElementById("cardExamSubject").innerText = `${sessionData.exam_info.subject} (${sessionData.exam_info.class_name})`;
          document.getElementById("cardExamDetails").innerText = `Ká»³ thi: ${sessionData.exam_info.period} | NÄƒm há»c: ${sessionData.exam_info.school_year}`;
        }

        const hasKeys = sessionData.exam_info && sessionData.exam_info.is_configured;
        const statusBadge = document.getElementById("cardAnswerStatus");
        if (hasKeys) {
          statusBadge.innerText = "âœ“ ÄÃ£ náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n";
          statusBadge.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-emerald-950 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800";
        } else {
          statusBadge.innerText = "ChÆ°a cÃ³ Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n";
          statusBadge.className = "px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-bold bg-amber-950 text-amber-300 border border-amber-800";
        }

        if (sessionRetryTimer) {
          clearTimeout(sessionRetryTimer);
          sessionRetryTimer = null;
        }

      } catch (err) {
        if (dot) dot.className = "w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-amber-400 animate-ping";
        if (statusText) statusText.innerText = "Äang káº¿t ná»‘i láº¡i mÃ¡y chá»§...";
        sessionRetryTimer = setTimeout(fetchSessionWithRetry, 3500);
      }
    }

    async function refreshSessionData() {
      await fetchSessionWithRetry();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // CAMERA & NHáº¬N DIá»†N Má»C QUANG Há»ŒC CHUáº¨N XÃC
    // =========================================================================
    async function startCamera() {
      if (currentStream) {
        try {
          currentStream.getTracks().forEach(t => t.stop());
        } catch(e) {}
        currentStream = null;
      }

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      video.muted = true;
      video.setAttribute("autoplay", "");
      video.setAttribute("playsinline", "");
      video.setAttribute("muted", "");

      const constraintCandidates = [
        { video: { facingMode: { ideal: facingMode }, width: { ideal: 1920 }, height: { ideal: 1080 } }, audio: false },
        { video: { facingMode: { ideal: facingMode }, width: { ideal: 1280 }, height: { ideal: 720 } }, audio: false },
        { video: { facingMode: facingMode }, audio: false },
        { video: true, audio: false }
      ];

      let stream = null;
      let lastErr = null;

      for (const c of constraintCandidates) {
        try {
          stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia(c);
          if (stream) break;
        } catch (e) {
          lastErr = e;
        }
      }

      if (!stream) {
        console.warn("Camera getUserMedia failed:", lastErr);
        showNotificationToast("KhÃ´ng má»Ÿ Ä‘Æ°á»£c camera trá»±c tiáº¿p. HÃ£y báº¥m 'Chá»¥p mÃ¡y gá»‘c'!", true);
        return;
      }

      try {
        currentStream = stream;
        video.srcObject = stream;
        videoTrack = stream.getVideoTracks()[0];

        video.onloadedmetadata = async () => {
          try {
            await video.play();
          } catch (e) {
            console.warn("Video play error on metadata:", e);
          }
        };

        await video.play();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("Video play initial warning:", e);
      }
    }

    function switchCamera() {
      facingMode = (facingMode === "environment") ? "user" : "environment";
      startCamera();
    }

    async function toggleTorch() {
      if (videoTrack && videoTrack.getCapabilities && videoTrack.getCapabilities().torch) {
        try {
          isTorchOn = !isTorchOn;
          await videoTrack.applyConstraints({ advanced: [{ torch: isTorchOn }] });
          document.getElementById("torchLabel").innerText = isTorchOn ? "Táº¯t Ä‘Ã¨n" : "Báº­t Ä‘Ã¨n";
        } catch (e) {
          showNotificationToast("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘iá»u khiá»ƒn Ä‘Ã¨n Flash: " + e.message, true);
        }
      } else {
        showNotificationToast("Thiáº¿t bá»‹ khÃ´ng há»— trá»£ báº­t Ä‘Ã¨n Flash trá»±c tiáº¿p.", true);
      }
    }

    // Thuáº­t toÃ¡n quang há»c quÃ©t má»‘c Ä‘en chuáº©n xÃ¡c (chá»‰ nháº­n diá»‡n trÃªn ná»n giáº¥y tráº¯ng, chá»‘ng báº¯t nháº§m mÃ n hÃ¬nh Ä‘en)
    function runOpticalMarkerDetection() {
      if (isGrading) return;
      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      // Báº®T BUá»˜C: Video pháº£i sáºµn sÃ ng, khÃ´ng bá»‹ táº¡m dá»«ng vÃ  kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c video > 0
      if (!video || video.readyState < 2 || video.paused || video.videoWidth === 0 || video.videoHeight === 0) return;

      const vw = video.videoWidth;
      const vh = video.videoHeight;
      const cw = video.clientWidth;
      const ch = video.clientHeight;
      if (cw === 0 || ch === 0) return;

      const scale = Math.max(cw / vw, ch / vh);
      const offsetX = (cw - vw * scale) / 2;
      const offsetY = (ch - vh * scale) / 2;

      const canvas = document.getElementById("analysisCanvas");
      canvas.width = 36;
      canvas.height = 36;
      const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d", { willReadFrequently: true });

      let matchedCount = 0;

      RETICLES.forEach((reticle) => {
        const el = document.getElementById(reticle.id);
        const rect = el.getBoundingClientRect();

        const sampleW = Math.round((rect.width / scale) * 1.8);
        const sampleH = Math.round((rect.height / scale) * 1.8);
        const vidX = Math.round((rect.left + rect.width / 2 - offsetX) / scale);
        
        // HÆ¡i nÃ¢ng nháº¹ tÃ¢m láº¥y máº«u cho 2 má»‘c dÆ°á»›i lÃªn 6px Ä‘á»ƒ tÃ¢m ngáº¯m náº±m chÃ­nh xÃ¡c trÃªn má»‘c M4,
        // trÃ¡nh trÆ°á»ng há»£p má»‘c Ä‘en náº±m sÃ¡t viá»n giáº¥y bá»‹ lá»t ra ngoÃ i hoáº·c cháº¡m ná»n bÃ n Ä‘en
        const offsetYFactor = (reticle.id === "reticle-bl" || reticle.id === "reticle-br") ? -6 : 0;
        const vidY = Math.round((rect.top + rect.height / 2 + offsetYFactor - offsetY) / scale);

        const sx = Math.max(0, Math.min(vw - sampleW, vidX - sampleW / 2));
        const sy = Math.max(0, Math.min(vh - sampleH, vidY - sampleH / 2));

        ctx.drawImage(video, sx, sy, sampleW, sampleH, 0, 0, 36, 36);
        const imgData = ctx.getImageData(0, 0, 36, 36).data;

        let minLum = 255, maxLum = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum < minLum) minLum = lum;
          if (lum > maxLum) maxLum = lum;
        }

        const contrast = maxLum - minLum;
        const thresh = minLum + contrast * 0.40;
        let darkPixels = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < imgData.length; i += 4) {
          const lum = 0.299 * imgData[i] + 0.587 * imgData[i + 1] + 0.114 * imgData[i + 2];
          if (lum <= thresh) darkPixels++;
        }

        const darkRatio = darkPixels / (36 * 36);

        // ÄIá»€U KIá»†N Má»C ÄEN CHÃNH XÃC & Bá»€N Vá»®NG:
        // 1. maxLum >= 105: Báº®T BUá»˜C cÃ³ bá» máº·t giáº¥y tráº¯ng sÃ¡ng (chá»‘ng báº¯t nháº§m mÃ n hÃ¬nh Ä‘en / bÃ³ng tá»‘i)
        // 2. contrast >= 22.0: Äá»§ Ä‘á»™ tÆ°Æ¡ng pháº£n giá»¯a má»‘c Ä‘en vÃ  ná»n giáº¥y tráº¯ng
        // 3. minLum < maxLum * 0.82: Má»‘c Ä‘en tá»‘i rÃµ rá»‡t so vá»›i ná»n giáº¥y
        // 4. darkRatio tá»« 2% Ä‘áº¿n 65%: Khá»›p má»‘c Ä‘en hoÃ n háº£o ká»ƒ cáº£ khi mÃ©p bÃ n Ä‘en hoáº·c chá»¯ kÃ½ lá»t má»™t pháº§n vÃ o vÃ¹ng ngáº¯m
        const isMatched = (maxLum >= 105 && contrast >= 22.0 && minLum < maxLum * 0.82 && darkRatio >= 0.02 && darkRatio <= 0.65);
        reticle.matched = isMatched;

        if (isMatched) {
          el.classList.remove("unmatched");
          el.classList.add("matched");
          matchedCount++;
        } else {
          el.classList.remove("matched");
          el.classList.add("unmatched");
        }
      });

      updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount);
    }

    function updateGuidanceUI(matchedCount) {
      if (isGrading) return;

      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      const viewfinder = document.getElementById("viewfinder");

      // CHá»ˆ Tá»° Äá»˜NG CHá»¤P KHI ÄÃƒ NHáº¬N Äá»¦ 4 GÃ“C (matchedCount === 4) VÃ€ GIá»® Vá»®NG TAY 2 CHU Ká»² (~180ms)
      if (matchedCount === 4) {
        lock4Frames++;
        viewfinder.className = "relative w-[88%] max-w-[330px] aspect-[1/1.444] border-2 border-emerald-400 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask shadow-[0_0_28px_rgba(16,185,129,0.9)] transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-1";
        guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-emerald-200 bg-emerald-950/95 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-emerald-500 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";
        guideIcon.innerText = "âœ“";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÃƒ KHÃ“A Äá»¦ 4 Má»C! ÄANG CHá»¤P...";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Giá»¯ cháº¯c tay, há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘ang tá»± Ä‘á»™ng chá»¥p vÃ  cháº¥m bÃ i...";

        if (lock4Frames >= 2) {
          executeGradingProcess();
        }
        return;
      }

      // Náº¾U CHÆ¯A Äá»¦ 4 GÃ“C: RESET Bá»˜ Äáº¾M, TUYá»†T Äá»I KHÃ”NG Tá»° Äá»˜NG CHá»¤P!
      lock4Frames = 0;

      viewfinder.className = "relative w-[88%] max-w-[330px] aspect-[1/1.444] border-2 border-dashed border-emerald-400/70 rounded-2xl viewfinder-mask transition-all flex flex-col justify-between p-1";
      guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-300 bg-black/85 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500/50 transition-all flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";

      if (matchedCount === 3) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "ðŸ”";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÃ£ khá»›p 3/4 má»‘c. CÄƒn ná»‘t má»‘c cÃ²n láº¡i!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "ÄÃ£ tÃ¬m tháº¥y 3 má»‘c. ÄÆ°a ná»‘t gÃ³c má»‘c thá»© 4 vÃ o ngÃ m hoáº·c báº¥m 'Chá»¥p vÃ  cháº¥m ngay'!";
      } else if (matchedCount === 2) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "ðŸ“";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÃ£ khá»›p 2/4 má»‘c. Giá»¯ pháº³ng camera!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "Cáº§m Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i song song máº·t bÃ n Ä‘á»ƒ khá»›p cáº£ 4 má»‘c.";
      } else if (matchedCount === 1) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "ðŸŽ¯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÃ£ khá»›p 1/4 má»‘c. ÄÆ°a gáº§n hÆ¡n!";
        guideSubText.innerText = "CÄƒn chá»‰nh 4 má»‘c Ä‘en gÃ³c vÃ o 4 khung ngáº¯m.";
      } else {
        guideIcon.innerText = "ðŸŽ¯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÆ°a 4 gÃ³c má»‘c Ä‘en vÃ o khung";
        guideSubText.innerText = "CÄƒn 4 má»‘c Ä‘en gÃ³c vÃ o 4 khung ngáº¯m. Äá»§ 4 má»‘c mÃ¡y sáº½ tá»± chá»¥p.";
      }
    }

    function triggerManualCapture() {
      if (!isGrading) executeGradingProcess();
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // Gá»¬I áº¢NH LÃŠN MÃY CHá»¦ CHáº¤M THI (Tá»ª VIDEO CANVAS)
    // =========================================================================
    async function executeGradingProcess() {
      if (isGrading) return;
      isGrading = true;
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(60);

      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      
      guideIcon.innerText = "â³";
      guideBoxText.innerText = "Äang náº¯n pháº³ng & cháº¥m Ä‘iá»ƒm...";
      guideSubText.innerText = "Há»‡ thá»‘ng Ä‘ang Ä‘á»‘i chiáº¿u Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n vá»›i mÃ£ Ä‘á» bÃ i thi...";
      guideBox.className = "text-center text-[11px] font-bold text-amber-200 bg-amber-950/95 px-4 py-1.5 rounded-full mx-auto shadow-xl border border-amber-500 animate-pulse flex items-center gap-1.5 pointer-events-auto";

      const video = document.getElementById("videoElement");
      const canvas = document.getElementById("captureCanvas");
      canvas.width = video.videoWidth || 1920;
      canvas.height = video.videoHeight || 1080;
      canvas.getContext("2d").drawImage(video, 0, 0);

      canvas.toBlob(async (blob) => {
        if (!blob) {
          isGrading = false;
          resetGuidanceHUD();
          return;
        }
        await postGradeImageBlob(blob, `Mobile_${Date.now()}.jpg`);
      }, "image/jpeg", 0.92);
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // CHá»¤P Báº°NG CAMERA MÃY NATIVE (FAIL-SAFE 100% HOáº T Äá»˜NG Má»ŒI á»¨NG Dá»¤NG)
    // =========================================================================
    async function handleNativeCameraCapture(event) {
      const file = event.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      isGrading = true;
      if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate(60);

      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      guideIcon.innerText = "â³";
      guideBoxText.innerText = "Äang náº¯n pháº³ng & cháº¥m Ä‘iá»ƒm...";

      await postGradeImageBlob(file, file.name || `Native_${Date.now()}.jpg`);
      event.target.value = ""; // Reset file input
    }

    async function postGradeImageBlob(blobOrFile, filename) {
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", blobOrFile, filename);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/grade-single-file", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        
        if (!res.ok) {
          const errData = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
          const errDetail = errData.detail || `Lá»—i mÃ¡y chá»§ (${res.status})`;
          playBeep(false);
          if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 60, 100]);
          showNotificationToast(errDetail, true);
          return;
        }

        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.new_submissions && data.new_submissions.length > 0) {
          const sub = data.new_submissions[0];
          latestSubId = sub.submission_id;
          if (sub.has_error) {
            playBeep(false);
            if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([100, 60, 100]);
            showNotificationToast(sub.error_msg || "áº¢nh chá»¥p khÃ´ng Ä‘áº¡t chuáº©n. Vui lÃ²ng chá»¥p tháº³ng vÃ  Ä‘á»§ sÃ¡ng.", true);
            await refreshSessionData();
            renderSubmissionsList();
          } else {
            playBeep(true);
            if (navigator.vibrate) navigator.vibrate([60, 40, 120]);
            showResultToast(sub);
            await refreshSessionData();
            renderSubmissionsList();
          }
        }
      } catch (err) {
        playBeep(false);
        showNotificationToast("Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i mÃ¡y chá»§: " + err.message, true);
      } finally {
        isGrading = false;
        lock4Frames = 0;
        resetGuidanceHUD();
      }
    }

    function resetGuidanceHUD() {
      const guideBox = document.getElementById("guidanceBox");
      const guideBoxText = document.getElementById("guideBoxText");
      const guideIcon = document.getElementById("guideIcon");
      const guideSubText = document.getElementById("guideText");
      if (guideBox && guideBoxText && guideIcon && guideSubText) {
        guideIcon.innerText = "ðŸŽ¯";
        guideBoxText.innerText = "ÄÆ°a 4 gÃ³c má»‘c Ä‘en vÃ o khung";
        guideSubText.innerText = "CÄƒn 4 má»‘c Ä‘en gÃ³c vÃ o 4 khung ngáº¯m. Äá»§ 4 má»‘c mÃ¡y sáº½ tá»± chá»¥p.";

    function renderSubmissionsList() {
      const container = document.getElementById("resultsListContainer");

      const query = (document.getElementById("searchSbdInput").value || "").trim().toLowerCase();

      const filtered = subs.filter(s => {
        const sbd = (s.sbd || "").toLowerCase();
        const code = (s.exam_code || "").toLowerCase();
        return !query || sbd.includes(query) || code.includes(query);
      });

      if (filtered.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = `
          <div class="text-center text-slate-500 py-16 text-xs">
            ${query ? "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y bÃ i thi khá»›p sá»‘ bÃ¡o danh nÃ y." : "ChÆ°a cÃ³ bÃ i thi nÃ o Ä‘Æ°á»£c cháº¥m trong Ä‘á»£t nÃ y."}
          </div>
        `;
        return;
      }

      container.innerHTML = filtered.map(sub => {
        const isErr = sub.has_error;
        const total = sub.scores ? sub.scores.total : 0.0;
        return `
          <div onclick="openImageViewer('${sub.submission_id}', 'SBD: ${sub.sbd || 'ChÆ°a rÃµ'} - Äá»: ${sub.exam_code || '---'}')" class="bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-3 flex justify-between items-center active:scale-[0.99] transition cursor-pointer">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div class="w-10 h-10 ${isErr ? 'bg-rose-950 border-rose-800 text-rose-300' : 'bg-indigo-950 border-indigo-800 text-emerald-400'} border rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center font-black text-sm">
                ${isErr ? '!' : total}
              </div>
              <div>
                <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span class="text-xs font-bold text-white">SBD: ${sub.sbd || 'ChÆ°a rÃµ'}</span>
                  <span class="text-[9px] bg-slate-800 text-slate-400 px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded font-mono">Äá»: ${sub.exam_code || '---'}</span>
                </div>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5 truncate max-w-[180px]">
                  ${isErr ? (sub.error_msg || 'Lá»—i nháº­n diá»‡n') : `P1: ${sub.scores.p1} | P2: ${sub.scores.p2} | P3: ${sub.scores.p3}`}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2" onclick="event.stopPropagation()">

                ðŸ—‘ï¸
              </button>

    async function deleteSubmissionMobile(subId) {

    function openImageViewer(subId, title) {
      document.getElementById("viewerTitle").innerText = title;
      document.getElementById("viewerSubtitle").innerText = `MÃ£ bÃ i: ${subId}`;
      const img = document.getElementById("viewerImage");
      const loading = document.getElementById("viewerLoading");
      
      if (loading) {
        loading.classList.remove("hidden");
        loading.innerHTML = `
          <svg class="animate-spin w-4 h-4 text-emerald-400 inline" fill="none" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle class="opacity-25" cx="12" cy="12" r="10" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="4"></circle><path class="opacity-75" fill="currentColor" d="M4 12a8 8 0 018-8v8H4z"></path></svg>
          <span>Äang táº£i áº£nh bÃ i cháº¥m...</span>
        `;
      }
      img.classList.add("hidden");

      img.onload = () => {
        if (loading) loading.classList.add("hidden");
        img.classList.remove("hidden");
      };

      img.onerror = () => {
        if (!img.src.includes("/api/image/")) {
          img.src = `/api/image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
        } else {
          if (loading) loading.innerHTML = "âš ï¸ KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y áº£nh bÃ i thi Ä‘Ã£ cháº¥m.";
        }
      };

      img.src = `/api/graded-image/${subId}?t=${Date.now()}`;
      document.getElementById("imageViewerModal").classList.remove("hidden");
    }

    async function uploadAnswerKeyMobile(e) {
      const file = e.target.files[0];
      if (!file) return;

      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append("file", file);

      try {
        const res = await fetch("/api/upload-answer-key", { method: "POST", body: fd });
        const data = await res.json();
        if (data.status === "SUCCESS") {
          showNotificationToast(`âœ“ ÄÃ£ náº¡p thÃ nh cÃ´ng ${data.exam_codes.length} mÃ£ Ä‘á»!`, false);
          await refreshSessionData();
        } else {
          showNotificationToast("Lá»—i náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: " + (data.detail || "KhÃ´ng rÃµ"), true);
        }
      } catch (err) {
        showNotificationToast("Lá»—i káº¿t ná»‘i khi náº¡p Ä‘Ã¡p Ã¡n: " + err.message, true);
      }
    }

=== FILE: templates\sw.js ===
// Service Worker cho OMR Mobile PWA
const CACHE_NAME = 'omr-pwa-v1';
const ASSETS_TO_CACHE = [
  '/scanner',
  '/manifest.json',
  '/icons/icon-192.png',
  '/icons/icon-512.png'
];

self.addEventListener('install', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
      return cache.addAll(ASSETS_TO_CACHE);
    }).then(() => self.skipWaiting())
  );
});

self.addEventListener('activate', (event) => {
  event.waitUntil(
    caches.keys().then((keys) => {
      return Promise.all(
        keys.filter((key) => key !== CACHE_NAME).map((key) => caches.delete(key))
      );
    }).then(() => self.clients.claim())
  );
});

// Network-first strategy cho API vÃ  Static fallback
self.addEventListener('fetch', (event) => {
  const url = new URL(event.request.url);

  // KhÃ´ng cache cÃ¡c lá»‡nh POST hoáº·c cÃ¡c endpoint API cháº¥m thi
  if (event.request.method !== 'GET' || url.pathname.startsWith('/api/')) {
    return;
  }

  event.respondWith(
    fetch(event.request)
      .then((response) => {
        // LÆ°u báº£n cache cho cÃ¡c tÃ i nguyÃªn tÄ©nh
        if (response.status === 200) {
          const resClone = response.clone();
          caches.open(CACHE_NAME).then((cache) => {
            cache.put(event.request, resClone);
          });
        }
        return response;
      })
      .catch(() => caches.match(event.request))
  );
});

    if event == cv2.EVENT_LBUTTONDOWN:
        points.append((x, y))

        cv2.circle(
            display_image,
